===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Controllers/AuthController.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.Services;
using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.Controllers
{
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/tenant/{companyId:int}/auth")]
    public class AuthController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ITenantDbContextFactory _tenantFactory;
        private readonly JwtTokenService _jwt;

        public AuthController(ITenantDbContextFactory tenantFactory, JwtTokenService jwt)
        {
            _tenantFactory = tenantFactory;
            _jwt = jwt;
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/auth/login
        [HttpPost("login")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<LoginResponse>> Login(int companyId, LoginRequest request)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Username) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Password))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Username and password are required." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            // Look up the credential by username
            var credential = await tenant.Credentials
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.Username == request.Username);

            // Same message whether the username doesn't exist or the password is wrong —
            // do NOT reveal which one is wrong (standard practice).
            const string invalidMessage = "Invalid username or password.";

            if (credential is null)
                return Unauthorized(new { message = invalidMessage });

            if (!credential.IsActive)
                return Unauthorized(new { message = "This account has been deactivated." });

            if (credential.IsLocked)
                return Unauthorized(new { message = "This account is locked. Contact an administrator." });

            // Verify password against BCrypt hash
            bool passwordOk;
            try
            {
                passwordOk = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.Verify(request.Password, credential.PasswordHash);
            }
            catch
            {
                // Malformed hash in DB — treat as wrong password.
                passwordOk = false;
            }

            if (!passwordOk)
            {
                // Increment failed attempts; lock at 5
                credential.FailedLoginAttempts++;
                if (credential.FailedLoginAttempts >= 5)
                    credential.IsLocked = true;
                credential.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();

                return Unauthorized(new { message = invalidMessage });
            }

            // Success — reset counters, stamp login time
            credential.FailedLoginAttempts = 0;
            credential.LastLoginDate = DateTime.UtcNow;
            credential.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            // Load the employee for the token
            var employee = await tenant.Employees
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == credential.EmployeeId);

            if (employee is null)
                return Unauthorized(new { message = "Employee record not found." });

            if (!employee.IsActive)
                return Unauthorized(new { message = "This employee is inactive." });

            await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();

            // Issue token
            var token = _jwt.CreateToken(employee, companyId);
            var expiresAt = _jwt.GetExpiryUtc();

            return Ok(new LoginResponse
            {
                Token = token,
                EmployeeId = employee.EmployeeId,
                CompanyId = companyId,
                Role = employee.Role,
                FullName = employee.FullName,
                ExpiresAt = expiresAt,
            });
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Controllers/CampaignController.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.Controllers
{
    [ApiController]
    [Authorize]
    [Route("api/tenant/{companyId:int}/campaigns")]
    public class CampaignController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ITenantDbContextFactory _tenantFactory;

        public CampaignController(ITenantDbContextFactory tenantFactory)
        {
            _tenantFactory = tenantFactory;
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns
        // Returns all currently-active campaigns (IsActive + within date range). The POS
        // path — Pending/Rejected/Inactive campaigns are excluded by the IsActive filter.
        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<ActionResult<List<CampaignResponseDto>>> GetActiveCampaigns(int companyId)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var now = DateTime.UtcNow;

            var campaigns = await tenant.Campaigns
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(c => c.IsActive && now >= c.StartDate && now <= c.EndDate)
                .OrderBy(c => c.CampaignId)
                .Select(c => new CampaignResponseDto
                {
                    CampaignId = c.CampaignId,
                    PromoName = c.PromoName,
                    PromoCategory = c.PromoCategory,
                    DiscountType = c.DiscountType,
                    DiscountValue = c.DiscountValue,
                    TargetTier = c.TargetTier,
                    MinPurchaseAmount = c.MinPurchaseAmount,
                    MaxDiscountAmount = c.MaxDiscountAmount,
                    MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer = c.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer,
                    StartDate = c.StartDate,
                    EndDate = c.EndDate,
                    IsActive = c.IsActive,
                    Status = c.Status,
                    MadeBy = c.MadeBy,
                    CreatedByName = c.Creator != null
                        ? (c.Creator.FirstName + " " + c.Creator.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    DecidedBy = c.DecidedBy,
                    DecidedAt = c.DecidedAt,
                    DecisionNotes = c.DecisionNotes,
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(campaigns);
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns/{id}
        [HttpGet("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CampaignResponseDto>> GetCampaign(int companyId, int id)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var campaign = await tenant.Campaigns
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(c => c.CampaignId == id)
                .Select(c => new CampaignResponseDto
                {
                    CampaignId = c.CampaignId,
                    PromoName = c.PromoName,
                    PromoCategory = c.PromoCategory,
                    DiscountType = c.DiscountType,
                    DiscountValue = c.DiscountValue,
                    TargetTier = c.TargetTier,
                    MinPurchaseAmount = c.MinPurchaseAmount,
                    MaxDiscountAmount = c.MaxDiscountAmount,
                    MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer = c.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer,
                    StartDate = c.StartDate,
                    EndDate = c.EndDate,
                    IsActive = c.IsActive,
                    Status = c.Status,
                    MadeBy = c.MadeBy,
                    CreatedByName = c.Creator != null
                        ? (c.Creator.FirstName + " " + c.Creator.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    DecidedBy = c.DecidedBy,
                    DecidedAt = c.DecidedAt,
                    DecisionNotes = c.DecisionNotes,
                })
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

            if (campaign is null)
                return NotFound();

            return Ok(campaign);
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns/manage
        //
        // Returns EVERY campaign regardless of status or date range, ordered newest first.
        // This is the Manager's list. The POS keeps using GET /campaigns, which filters
        // to currently-active campaigns only.
        [HttpGet("manage")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<List<CampaignResponseDto>>> GetForManagement(int companyId)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var campaigns = await tenant.Campaigns
                .AsNoTracking()
                .OrderByDescending(c => c.CampaignId)
                .Select(c => new CampaignResponseDto
                {
                    CampaignId = c.CampaignId,
                    PromoName = c.PromoName,
                    PromoCategory = c.PromoCategory,
                    DiscountType = c.DiscountType,
                    DiscountValue = c.DiscountValue,
                    TargetTier = c.TargetTier,
                    MinPurchaseAmount = c.MinPurchaseAmount,
                    MaxDiscountAmount = c.MaxDiscountAmount,
                    MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer = c.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer,
                    StartDate = c.StartDate,
                    EndDate = c.EndDate,
                    IsActive = c.IsActive,
                    Status = c.Status,
                    MadeBy = c.MadeBy,
                    CreatedByName = c.Creator != null
                        ? (c.Creator.FirstName + " " + c.Creator.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    DecidedBy = c.DecidedBy,
                    DecidedAt = c.DecidedAt,
                    DecisionNotes = c.DecisionNotes,
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(campaigns);
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns
        //
        // Creates a campaign. Manager only. Status starts as "Pending" — it goes live
        // only when an Admin approves it from the Approvals tab.
        [HttpPost]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CampaignResponseDto>> CreateCampaign(
            int companyId,
            CreateCampaignRequest request)
        {
            var validationError = ValidateCampaignRequest(
                request.PromoName, request.PromoCategory, request.DiscountType,
                request.DiscountValue, request.TargetTier,
                request.MinPurchaseAmount, request.MaxDiscountAmount,
                request.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer, request.StartDate, request.EndDate);

            if (validationError is not null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = validationError });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var creator = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.MadeBy);

            if (creator is null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Creator not found." });

            if (!creator.Role.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only managers can create campaigns." });

            var campaign = new Campaign
            {
                PromoName = request.PromoName.Trim(),
                PromoCategory = request.PromoCategory.Trim(),
                DiscountType = request.DiscountType,
                DiscountValue = request.DiscountValue,
                TargetTier = request.TargetTier,
                MinPurchaseAmount = request.MinPurchaseAmount,
                MaxDiscountAmount = request.MaxDiscountAmount,
                MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer = request.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer,
                StartDate = request.StartDate,
                EndDate = request.EndDate,
                MadeBy = request.MadeBy,
                Status = "Pending",
                IsActive = false,
                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
            };

            tenant.Campaigns.Add(campaign);

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            // Reload with the Creator nav so CreatedByName is populated in the response.
            await tenant.Entry(campaign).Reference(c => c.Creator).LoadAsync();

            return Ok(MapToDto(campaign));
        }

        // PUT api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns/{id}
        [HttpPut("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CampaignResponseDto>> UpdateCampaign(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            UpdateCampaignRequest request)
        {
            var validationError = ValidateCampaignRequest(
                request.PromoName, request.PromoCategory, request.DiscountType,
                request.DiscountValue, request.TargetTier,
                request.MinPurchaseAmount, request.MaxDiscountAmount,
                request.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer, request.StartDate, request.EndDate);

            if (validationError is not null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = validationError });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var editor = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.MadeBy);

            if (editor is null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Editor not found." });

            if (!editor.Role.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only managers can edit campaigns." });

            var campaign = await tenant.Campaigns.FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CampaignId == id);
            if (campaign is null)
                return NotFound();

            campaign.PromoName = request.PromoName.Trim();
            campaign.PromoCategory = request.PromoCategory.Trim();
            campaign.DiscountType = request.DiscountType;
            campaign.DiscountValue = request.DiscountValue;
            campaign.TargetTier = request.TargetTier;
            campaign.MinPurchaseAmount = request.MinPurchaseAmount;
            campaign.MaxDiscountAmount = request.MaxDiscountAmount;
            campaign.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer = request.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer;
            campaign.StartDate = request.StartDate;
            campaign.EndDate = request.EndDate;
            campaign.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            // A Rejected campaign that's edited goes back to Pending — the Manager is
            // resubmitting. A Pending or Active campaign keeps its status; only a Rejected
            // one changes here.
            if (campaign.Status.Equals("Rejected", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                campaign.Status = "Pending";
                campaign.IsActive = false;
                campaign.DecidedBy = null;
                campaign.DecidedAt = null;
                campaign.DecisionNotes = null;
            }

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            await tenant.Entry(campaign).Reference(c => c.Creator).LoadAsync();

            return Ok(MapToDto(campaign));
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns/{id}/activate
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/activate")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CampaignResponseDto>> ActivateCampaign(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            CampaignActionRequest request)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var actor = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.PerformedBy);

            if (actor is null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Actor not found." });

            if (!actor.Role.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only managers can activate campaigns." });

            var campaign = await tenant.Campaigns.FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CampaignId == id);
            if (campaign is null)
                return NotFound();

            // Pending campaigns are frozen until an Admin decides. Rejected campaigns
            // must be edited (which resubmits them as Pending) before they can go live.
            if (campaign.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Pending campaigns must be approved by an admin before they can go live." });

            if (campaign.Status.Equals("Rejected", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Rejected campaigns must be edited and resubmitted for approval." });

            campaign.Status = "Active";
            campaign.IsActive = true;
            campaign.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            await tenant.Entry(campaign).Reference(c => c.Creator).LoadAsync();

            return Ok(MapToDto(campaign));
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns/{id}/deactivate
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/deactivate")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CampaignResponseDto>> DeactivateCampaign(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            CampaignActionRequest request)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var actor = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.PerformedBy);

            if (actor is null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Actor not found." });

            if (!actor.Role.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only managers can deactivate campaigns." });

            var campaign = await tenant.Campaigns.FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CampaignId == id);
            if (campaign is null)
                return NotFound();

            if (campaign.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Pending campaigns cannot be deactivated. They are awaiting admin approval." });

            campaign.Status = "Inactive";
            campaign.IsActive = false;
            campaign.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            await tenant.Entry(campaign).Reference(c => c.Creator).LoadAsync();

            return Ok(MapToDto(campaign));
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns/pending
        //
        // Returns only Pending campaigns, for the Admin's Approvals tab. Ordered oldest
        // first so the queue reads like a queue — oldest submission at the top.
        [HttpGet("pending")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<List<CampaignResponseDto>>> GetPendingCampaigns(int companyId)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var campaigns = await tenant.Campaigns
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(c => c.Status == "Pending")
                .OrderBy(c => c.CreatedAt)
                .Select(c => new CampaignResponseDto
                {
                    CampaignId = c.CampaignId,
                    PromoName = c.PromoName,
                    PromoCategory = c.PromoCategory,
                    DiscountType = c.DiscountType,
                    DiscountValue = c.DiscountValue,
                    TargetTier = c.TargetTier,
                    MinPurchaseAmount = c.MinPurchaseAmount,
                    MaxDiscountAmount = c.MaxDiscountAmount,
                    MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer = c.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer,
                    StartDate = c.StartDate,
                    EndDate = c.EndDate,
                    IsActive = c.IsActive,
                    Status = c.Status,
                    MadeBy = c.MadeBy,
                    CreatedByName = c.Creator != null
                        ? (c.Creator.FirstName + " " + c.Creator.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    DecidedBy = c.DecidedBy,
                    DecidedAt = c.DecidedAt,
                    DecisionNotes = c.DecisionNotes,
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(campaigns);
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns/{id}/approve
        //
        // Admin approves a Pending campaign. Flips it live and records the decision.
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/approve")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CampaignResponseDto>> ApproveCampaign(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            DecideCampaignRequest request)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var decider = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.DecidedBy);

            if (decider is null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Deciding employee not found." });

            if (!decider.Role.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                !decider.Role.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only admins can approve campaigns." });

            var campaign = await tenant.Campaigns.FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CampaignId == id);
            if (campaign is null)
                return NotFound();

            if (!campaign.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = $"Only pending campaigns can be approved (current status: {campaign.Status})." });

            campaign.Status = "Active";
            campaign.IsActive = true;
            campaign.DecidedBy = request.DecidedBy;
            campaign.DecidedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            campaign.DecisionNotes = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Notes) ? null : request.Notes.Trim();
            campaign.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            await tenant.Entry(campaign).Reference(c => c.Creator).LoadAsync();

            return Ok(MapToDto(campaign));
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns/{id}/reject
        //
        // Admin rejects a Pending campaign. Requires a reason. The campaign stays in the
        // DB so the Manager can edit and resubmit it.
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/reject")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CampaignResponseDto>> RejectCampaign(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            DecideCampaignRequest request)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Notes))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "A rejection reason is required." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var decider = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.DecidedBy);

            if (decider is null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Deciding employee not found." });

            if (!decider.Role.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                !decider.Role.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only admins can reject campaigns." });

            var campaign = await tenant.Campaigns.FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CampaignId == id);
            if (campaign is null)
                return NotFound();

            if (!campaign.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = $"Only pending campaigns can be rejected (current status: {campaign.Status})." });

            campaign.Status = "Rejected";
            campaign.IsActive = false;
            campaign.DecidedBy = request.DecidedBy;
            campaign.DecidedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            campaign.DecisionNotes = request.Notes.Trim();
            campaign.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            await tenant.Entry(campaign).Reference(c => c.Creator).LoadAsync();

            return Ok(MapToDto(campaign));
        }

        // ---- helpers ----

        private static string? ValidateCampaignRequest(
            string promoName, string promoCategory, string discountType,
            decimal discountValue, string targetTier,
            decimal? minPurchase, decimal? maxDiscount,
            int maxRedemptions, DateTime startDate, DateTime endDate)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(promoName))
                return "Promo name is required.";
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(promoCategory))
                return "Promo category is required.";
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(discountType))
                return "Discount type is required.";

            if (!discountType.Equals("Percentage", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                !discountType.Equals("FixedAmount", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return "Discount type must be Percentage or FixedAmount.";

            if (discountValue <= 0)
                return "Discount value must be greater than zero.";

            if (discountType.Equals("Percentage", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && discountValue > 100)
                return "Percentage discount cannot exceed 100.";

            if (maxDiscount.HasValue && maxDiscount.Value <= 0)
                return "Max discount must be greater than zero if set.";

            if (minPurchase.HasValue && minPurchase.Value < 0)
                return "Minimum purchase cannot be negative.";

            if (maxRedemptions <= 0)
                return "Max redemptions per customer must be at least 1.";

            if (endDate <= startDate)
                return "End date must be after start date.";

            return null;
        }

        private static CampaignResponseDto MapToDto(Campaign c) => new()
        {
            CampaignId = c.CampaignId,
            PromoName = c.PromoName,
            PromoCategory = c.PromoCategory,
            DiscountType = c.DiscountType,
            DiscountValue = c.DiscountValue,
            TargetTier = c.TargetTier,
            MinPurchaseAmount = c.MinPurchaseAmount,
            MaxDiscountAmount = c.MaxDiscountAmount,
            MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer = c.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer,
            StartDate = c.StartDate,
            EndDate = c.EndDate,
            IsActive = c.IsActive,
            Status = c.Status,
            MadeBy = c.MadeBy,
            CreatedByName = c.Creator != null
                ? (c.Creator.FirstName + " " + c.Creator.LastName).Trim()
                : "—",
            DecidedBy = c.DecidedBy,
            DecidedAt = c.DecidedAt,
            DecisionNotes = c.DecisionNotes,
        };
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Controllers/CustomerController.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.Controllers
{
    [ApiController]
    [Authorize]
    [Route("api/tenant/{companyId:int}/customers")]
    public class CustomerController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ITenantDbContextFactory _tenantFactory;

        public CustomerController(ITenantDbContextFactory tenantFactory)
        {
            _tenantFactory = tenantFactory;
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/customers?search=...
        // Returns all customers, or filters by name/phone/ID if search is provided.
        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/customers?search=...&page=1&pageSize=25
        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<CustomerResponseDto>>> GetCustomers(
             int companyId,
             [FromQuery] string? search,
             [FromQuery] string? activeFilter = null,
             [FromQuery] int page = 1,
             [FromQuery] int pageSize = 25)
        {
            if (page < 1) page = 1;
            if (pageSize < 1) pageSize = 25;
            if (pageSize > 200) pageSize = 200;

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            // activeFilter:
            //   null / "" / "active"    -> only IsActive = true (the default)
            //   "inactive"              -> only IsActive = false
            //   "all"                   -> no IsActive filter
            // Anything else falls back to the default (active-only).
            var filter = (activeFilter ?? string.Empty).Trim().ToLowerInvariant();

            var query = tenant.Customers.AsNoTracking();

            query = filter switch
            {
                "inactive" => query.Where(c => !c.IsActive),
                "all" => query,
                _ => query.Where(c => c.IsActive),
            };

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(search))
            {
                var term = search.Trim();
                query = query.Where(c =>
                    (c.FirstName + " " + c.LastName).Contains(term) ||
                    c.ContactNumber.Contains(term) ||
                    c.CustomerId.ToString() == term);
            }

            var totalCount = await query.CountAsync();

            var items = await query
                .OrderBy(c => c.CustomerId)
                .Skip((page - 1) * pageSize)
                .Take(pageSize)
                .Select(c => new CustomerResponseDto
                {
                    CustomerId = c.CustomerId,
                    FirstName = c.FirstName,
                    LastName = c.LastName,
                    FullName = (c.FirstName + " " + c.LastName).Trim(),
                    ContactNumber = c.ContactNumber,
                    Email = c.Email,
                    Birthday = c.Birthday,
                    LastVisitDate = c.LastVisitDate,
                    PointsBalance = c.PointsBalance,
                    Tier = c.PointsBalance >= 500 ? "Gold"
                         : c.PointsBalance >= 200 ? "Silver"
                         : "Regular",
                    IsActive = c.IsActive
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(new PagedResponse<CustomerResponseDto>
            {
                Items = items,
                TotalCount = totalCount,
                Page = page,
                PageSize = pageSize
            });
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/customers/{id}
        [HttpGet("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CustomerResponseDto>> GetCustomer(int companyId, int id)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var customer = await tenant.Customers
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(c => c.CustomerId == id && c.IsActive)
                .Select(c => new CustomerResponseDto
                {
                    CustomerId = c.CustomerId,
                    FirstName = c.FirstName,
                    LastName = c.LastName,
                    FullName = (c.FirstName + " " + c.LastName).Trim(),
                    ContactNumber = c.ContactNumber,
                    Email = c.Email,
                    Birthday = c.Birthday,
                    LastVisitDate = c.LastVisitDate,
                    PointsBalance = c.PointsBalance,
                    Tier = c.PointsBalance >= 500 ? "Gold"
                         : c.PointsBalance >= 200 ? "Silver"
                         : "Regular",
                    IsActive = c.IsActive
                })
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

            if (customer is null)
                return NotFound();

            return Ok(customer);
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/customers/find?query=...
        // Used by the POS customer search (ID or phone). Returns a single match or 404.
        [HttpGet("find")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CustomerResponseDto>> FindCustomer(
            int companyId,
            [FromQuery] string query)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(query))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Query is required." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var term = query.Trim();

            Customer? match = null;

            // Try ID first
            if (int.TryParse(term, out var id))
            {
                match = await tenant.Customers
                    .AsNoTracking()
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CustomerId == id && c.IsActive);
            }

            // Then phone (digits only)
            if (match is null)
            {
                var digits = new string(term.Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
                if (digits.Length > 0)
                {
                    match = await tenant.Customers
                        .AsNoTracking()
                        .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.ContactNumber.Contains(digits) && c.IsActive);
                }
            }

            if (match is null)
                return NotFound();

            return Ok(new CustomerResponseDto
            {
                CustomerId = match.CustomerId,
                FirstName = match.FirstName,
                LastName = match.LastName,
                FullName = (match.FirstName + " " + match.LastName).Trim(),
                ContactNumber = match.ContactNumber,
                Email = match.Email,
                Birthday = match.Birthday,
                LastVisitDate = match.LastVisitDate,
                PointsBalance = match.PointsBalance,
                Tier = match.PointsBalance >= 500 ? "Gold"
                     : match.PointsBalance >= 200 ? "Silver"
                     : "Regular",
                IsActive = match.IsActive
            });
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/customers
        [HttpPost]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CustomerResponseDto>> CreateCustomer(
            int companyId,
            CreateCustomerDto request)
        {
            // ---- Server-side validation ----
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.FirstName))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "First name is required." });
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.LastName))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Last name is required." });

            var cleanedPhone = new string((request.ContactNumber ?? "").Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
            if (cleanedPhone.Length < 10 || cleanedPhone.Length > 11)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Contact number must be 10-11 digits." });

            var cleanedEmail = string.Empty;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Email))
            {
                if (!request.Email.Contains('@') || !request.Email.Contains('.'))
                    return BadRequest(new { message = "Invalid email format." });
                cleanedEmail = request.Email.Trim().ToLower();
            }

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            // Only Baristas register new customers. Managers act on existing ones and
            // don't originate records. Read the caller from the JWT claim, look up
            // their role in the tenant DB.
            var callerEmployeeIdClaim = User.FindFirst("employeeId")?.Value;
            if (!int.TryParse(callerEmployeeIdClaim, out var callerEmployeeId))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Missing or invalid employee claim." });

            var caller = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == callerEmployeeId);

            if (caller is null)
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Caller not found." });

            if (!caller.Role.Equals("Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only baristas can register new customers." });

            var customer = new Customer
            {
                FirstName = request.FirstName.Trim(),
                LastName = request.LastName.Trim(),
                ContactNumber = cleanedPhone,
                Email = cleanedEmail,
                Birthday = request.Birthday,
                LastVisitDate = DateTime.UtcNow,
                PointsBalance = 0,
                IsActive = true,
                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
            };

            tenant.Customers.Add(customer);

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return Ok(new CustomerResponseDto
            {
                CustomerId = customer.CustomerId,
                FirstName = customer.FirstName,
                LastName = customer.LastName,
                FullName = (customer.FirstName + " " + customer.LastName).Trim(),
                ContactNumber = customer.ContactNumber,
                Email = customer.Email,
                Birthday = customer.Birthday,
                LastVisitDate = customer.LastVisitDate,
                PointsBalance = customer.PointsBalance,
                Tier = "Regular",
                IsActive = customer.IsActive
            });
        }

        // PUT api/tenant/{companyId}/customers/{id}
        [HttpPut("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<CustomerResponseDto>> UpdateCustomer(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            UpdateCustomerDto request)
        {
            // ---- Server-side validation ----
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.FirstName))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "First name is required." });
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.LastName))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Last name is required." });

            var cleanedPhone = new string((request.ContactNumber ?? "").Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
            if (cleanedPhone.Length < 10 || cleanedPhone.Length > 11)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Contact number must be 10-11 digits." });

            var cleanedEmail = string.Empty;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Email))
            {
                if (!request.Email.Contains('@') || !request.Email.Contains('.'))
                    return BadRequest(new { message = "Invalid email format." });
                cleanedEmail = request.Email.Trim().ToLower();
            }

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var customer = await tenant.Customers.FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CustomerId == id);
            if (customer is null)
                return NotFound();

            customer.FirstName = request.FirstName.Trim();
            customer.LastName = request.LastName.Trim();
            customer.ContactNumber = cleanedPhone;
            customer.Email = cleanedEmail;
            customer.Birthday = request.Birthday;
            customer.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return Ok(new CustomerResponseDto
            {
                CustomerId = customer.CustomerId,
                FirstName = customer.FirstName,
                LastName = customer.LastName,
                FullName = (customer.FirstName + " " + customer.LastName).Trim(),
                ContactNumber = customer.ContactNumber,
                Email = customer.Email,
                Birthday = customer.Birthday,
                LastVisitDate = customer.LastVisitDate,
                PointsBalance = customer.PointsBalance,
                Tier = customer.PointsBalance >= 500 ? "Gold"
                     : customer.PointsBalance >= 200 ? "Silver"
                     : "Regular",
                IsActive = customer.IsActive
            });
        }

        // DELETE api/tenant/{companyId}/customers/{id}
        // Soft delete — sets IsActive = false, does not remove the row. Preserves order history.
        [HttpDelete("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteCustomer(int companyId, int id)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            // Only Managers deactivate customers. Read the caller from the JWT claim.
            var callerEmployeeIdClaim = User.FindFirst("employeeId")?.Value;
            if (!int.TryParse(callerEmployeeIdClaim, out var callerEmployeeId))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Missing or invalid employee claim." });

            var caller = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == callerEmployeeId);

            if (caller is null)
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Caller not found." });

            if (!caller.Role.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only managers can deactivate customers." });

            var customer = await tenant.Customers.FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CustomerId == id);
            if (customer is null)
                return NotFound();

            customer.IsActive = false;
            customer.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return NoContent();
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/customers/{id}/reactivate
        // Reverses a soft delete — sets IsActive = true. All history preserved.
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/reactivate")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> ReactivateCustomer(int companyId, int id)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            // Only Managers reactivate customers.
            var callerEmployeeIdClaim = User.FindFirst("employeeId")?.Value;
            if (!int.TryParse(callerEmployeeIdClaim, out var callerEmployeeId))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Missing or invalid employee claim." });

            var caller = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == callerEmployeeId);

            if (caller is null)
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Caller not found." });

            if (!caller.Role.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only managers can reactivate customers." });

            var customer = await tenant.Customers.FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CustomerId == id);
            if (customer is null)
                return NotFound();

            customer.IsActive = true;
            customer.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return NoContent();
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/customers/{id}/ledger
        [HttpGet("{id:int}/ledger")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<List<PointLedgerResponseDto>>> GetLedger(int companyId, int id)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var entries = await tenant.PointLedgers
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(l => l.CustomerId == id)
                .OrderByDescending(l => l.Timestamp)
                .Select(l => new PointLedgerResponseDto
                {
                    PointLedgerId = l.PointLedgerId,
                    CustomerId = l.CustomerId,
                    PointsChange = l.PointsChange,
                    Type = l.Type,
                    Reason = l.Reason,
                    OrderId = l.OrderId,
                    PerformedBy = l.PerformedBy,
                    Timestamp = l.Timestamp
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(entries);
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/customers/{id}/orders
        [HttpGet("{id:int}/orders")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<List<OrderHistoryDto>>> GetOrderHistory(int companyId, int id)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var orders = await tenant.Orders
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(o => o.CustomerId == id)
                .OrderByDescending(o => o.OrderDate)
                .Select(o => new OrderHistoryDto
                {
                    OrderId = o.OrderId,
                    OrderDate = o.OrderDate,
                    TotalAmount = o.TotalAmount,
                    Status = o.Status
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(orders);
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Controllers/DevController.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Development-only utility endpoints. NOT for production use. This controller
    /// should be deleted once the one-time migrations are done.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/tenant/{companyId:int}/dev")]
    public class DevController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ITenantDbContextFactory _tenantFactory;

        public DevController(ITenantDbContextFactory tenantFactory)
        {
            _tenantFactory = tenantFactory;
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/dev/rehash-credentials
        //
        // One-time migration: reads every credential row whose PasswordHash does NOT
        // already look like a BCrypt hash, hashes its current value with BCrypt, and
        // saves. Safe to run multiple times — rows that are already hashed are skipped.
        [HttpPost("rehash-credentials")]
        public async Task<ActionResult> RehashCredentials(int companyId)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var credentials = await tenant.Credentials.ToListAsync();

            int updated = 0;
            int skipped = 0;

            foreach (var cred in credentials)
            {
                // BCrypt hashes start with $2a$, $2b$, or $2y$ and are 60 characters long.
                // Anything else is presumed to be plaintext.
                var looksHashed = cred.PasswordHash.StartsWith("$2") && cred.PasswordHash.Length == 60;

                if (looksHashed)
                {
                    skipped++;
                    continue;
                }

                // Hash the current plaintext value and overwrite.
                cred.PasswordHash = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.HashPassword(cred.PasswordHash);
                cred.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
                updated++;
            }

            if (updated > 0)
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();

            return Ok(new
            {
                message = "Rehash complete.",
                updated,
                skipped,
                total = credentials.Count,
            });
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Controllers/EmployeesController.cs =====
﻿using System.Security.Claims;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.Controllers
{
    [ApiController]
    [Authorize]   // any authenticated employee can hit this controller...
    [Route("api/tenant/{companyId:int}/employees")]
    public class EmployeesController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ITenantDbContextFactory _tenantFactory;

        public EmployeesController(ITenantDbContextFactory tenantFactory)
        {
            _tenantFactory = tenantFactory;
        }

        // ---- helpers ----

        private int GetCallerEmployeeId()
        {
            var claim = User.FindFirst("employeeId")?.Value
                ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Missing employeeId claim.");
            return int.Parse(claim);
        }

        private bool CallerIsAdmin() => User.IsInRole("Admin") || User.IsInRole("SuperAdmin");

        private void ForbidIfNotAdmin()
        {
            if (!CallerIsAdmin())
                throw new UnauthorizedAccessException();
        }

        // ---- endpoints ----

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/employees?search=&page=1&pageSize=25
        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<EmployeeResponseDto>>> GetEmployees(
            int companyId,
            [FromQuery] string? search,
            [FromQuery] int page = 1,
            [FromQuery] int pageSize = 25)
        {
            if (!CallerIsAdmin())
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only admins can manage employees." });

            if (page < 1) page = 1;
            if (pageSize < 1) pageSize = 25;
            if (pageSize > 200) pageSize = 200;

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var query = tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Include(e => e.Credential)
                .AsQueryable();

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(search))
            {
                var term = search.Trim();
                query = query.Where(e =>
                    (e.FirstName + " " + e.LastName).Contains(term) ||
                    e.ContactNumber.Contains(term) ||
                    (e.Credential != null && e.Credential.Username.Contains(term)) ||
                    e.EmployeeId.ToString() == term);
            }

            var totalCount = await query.CountAsync();

            var items = await query
                .OrderBy(e => e.EmployeeId)
                .Skip((page - 1) * pageSize)
                .Take(pageSize)
                .Select(e => new EmployeeResponseDto
                {
                    EmployeeId = e.EmployeeId,
                    FirstName = e.FirstName,
                    LastName = e.LastName,
                    FullName = (e.FirstName + " " + e.LastName).Trim(),
                    ContactNumber = e.ContactNumber,
                    Email = e.Email,
                    Role = e.Role,
                    IsActive = e.IsActive,
                    Username = e.Credential != null ? e.Credential.Username : null,
                    LastLoginDate = e.Credential != null ? e.Credential.LastLoginDate : null,
                    HasCredential = e.Credential != null
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(new PagedResponse<EmployeeResponseDto>
            {
                Items = items,
                TotalCount = totalCount,
                Page = page,
                PageSize = pageSize
            });
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/employees/{id}
        [HttpGet("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<EmployeeResponseDto>> GetEmployee(int companyId, int id)
        {
            if (!CallerIsAdmin())
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only admins can manage employees." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var employee = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Include(e => e.Credential)
                .Where(e => e.EmployeeId == id)
                .Select(e => new EmployeeResponseDto
                {
                    EmployeeId = e.EmployeeId,
                    FirstName = e.FirstName,
                    LastName = e.LastName,
                    FullName = (e.FirstName + " " + e.LastName).Trim(),
                    ContactNumber = e.ContactNumber,
                    Email = e.Email,
                    Role = e.Role,
                    IsActive = e.IsActive,
                    Username = e.Credential != null ? e.Credential.Username : null,
                    LastLoginDate = e.Credential != null ? e.Credential.LastLoginDate : null,
                    HasCredential = e.Credential != null
                })
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

            if (employee is null)
                return NotFound();

            return Ok(employee);
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/employees
        [HttpPost]
        public async Task<ActionResult<EmployeeResponseDto>> CreateEmployee(
            int companyId,
            CreateEmployeeRequest request)
        {
            if (!CallerIsAdmin())
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only admins can create employees." });

            // Validation
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.FirstName))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "First name is required." });
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.LastName))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Last name is required." });

            var cleanedPhone = new string((request.ContactNumber ?? "").Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
            if (cleanedPhone.Length < 10 || cleanedPhone.Length > 11)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Contact number must be 10-11 digits." });

            var cleanedEmail = string.Empty;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Email))
            {
                if (!request.Email.Contains('@') || !request.Email.Contains('.'))
                    return BadRequest(new { message = "Invalid email format." });
                cleanedEmail = request.Email.Trim().ToLower();
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Username) || request.Username.Trim().Length < 3)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Username must be at least 3 characters." });
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Password) || request.Password.Length < 4)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Password must be at least 4 characters." });

            // Role — Admin cannot create Admins
            var requestedRole = (request.Role ?? "Barista").Trim();
            if (requestedRole.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                requestedRole.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only the SuperAdmin can create Admin accounts." });
            }
            if (!requestedRole.Equals("Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                !requestedRole.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Role must be Barista or Manager." });
            }

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            // Uniqueness of username
            var usernameTaken = await tenant.Credentials
                .AnyAsync(c => c.Username == request.Username.Trim());
            if (usernameTaken)
                return Conflict(new { message = "That username is already taken." });

            var employee = new Employee
            {
                FirstName = request.FirstName.Trim(),
                LastName = request.LastName.Trim(),
                ContactNumber = cleanedPhone,
                Email = cleanedEmail,
                Role = requestedRole,
                IsActive = true,
                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
            };

            var credential = new Credential
            {
                Username = request.Username.Trim(),
                PasswordHash = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.HashPassword(request.Password),
                Employee = employee,
                FailedLoginAttempts = 0,
                IsLocked = false,
                IsActive = true,
                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
            };

            tenant.Employees.Add(employee);
            tenant.Credentials.Add(credential);

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return Ok(new EmployeeResponseDto
            {
                EmployeeId = employee.EmployeeId,
                FirstName = employee.FirstName,
                LastName = employee.LastName,
                FullName = employee.FullName,
                ContactNumber = employee.ContactNumber,
                Email = employee.Email,
                Role = employee.Role,
                IsActive = employee.IsActive,
                Username = credential.Username,
                LastLoginDate = credential.LastLoginDate,
                HasCredential = true
            });
        }

        // PUT api/tenant/{companyId}/employees/{id}
        [HttpPut("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<EmployeeResponseDto>> UpdateEmployee(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            UpdateEmployeeRequest request)
        {
            if (!CallerIsAdmin())
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only admins can update employees." });

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.FirstName))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "First name is required." });
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.LastName))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Last name is required." });

            var cleanedPhone = new string((request.ContactNumber ?? "").Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
            if (cleanedPhone.Length < 10 || cleanedPhone.Length > 11)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Contact number must be 10-11 digits." });

            var cleanedEmail = string.Empty;
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Email))
            {
                if (!request.Email.Contains('@') || !request.Email.Contains('.'))
                    return BadRequest(new { message = "Invalid email format." });
                cleanedEmail = request.Email.Trim().ToLower();
            }

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var employee = await tenant.Employees.FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == id);
            if (employee is null)
                return NotFound();

            // Guard: cannot edit an Admin's account
            if (employee.Role.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                employee.Role.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Admin accounts cannot be edited from this console." });

            // Guard: cannot edit an inactive employee. Reactivate them first, then edit.
            if (!employee.IsActive)
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Inactive employees cannot be edited. Reactivate them first." });

            // Guard: cannot change someone's role to Admin
            var requestedRole = (request.Role ?? "").Trim();
            if (requestedRole.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                requestedRole.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only the SuperAdmin can promote to Admin." });
            if (!requestedRole.Equals("Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                !requestedRole.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Role must be Barista or Manager." });

            // ---- Username change (optional) ----
            // If Username is provided, validate and update the credential in the same
            // SaveChanges. If it's null/empty, leave it alone. If it matches the current
            // value, that's a no-op — no error, no unnecessary update.
            var requestedUsername = (request.Username ?? string.Empty).Trim();
            Credential? credential = null;

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(requestedUsername))
            {
                if (requestedUsername.Length < 3)
                    return BadRequest(new { message = "Username must be at least 3 characters." });

                credential = await tenant.Credentials
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.EmployeeId == employee.EmployeeId);

                if (credential is null)
                    return BadRequest(new { message = "This employee has no credential to update." });

                if (!string.Equals(credential.Username, requestedUsername, StringComparison.Ordinal))
                {
                    // Uniqueness check — case-sensitive, matching CreateEmployee's AnyAsync.
                    var taken = await tenant.Credentials
                        .AnyAsync(c => c.Username == requestedUsername && c.EmployeeId != employee.EmployeeId);

                    if (taken)
                        return Conflict(new { message = "That username is already taken." });

                    credential.Username = requestedUsername;
                    credential.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
                }
            }

            employee.FirstName = request.FirstName.Trim();
            employee.LastName = request.LastName.Trim();
            employee.ContactNumber = cleanedPhone;
            employee.Email = cleanedEmail;
            employee.Role = requestedRole;
            employee.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            // Reload the credential (AsNoTracking is fine for the response DTO).
            credential = await tenant.Credentials
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.EmployeeId == employee.EmployeeId);

            return Ok(new EmployeeResponseDto
            {
                EmployeeId = employee.EmployeeId,
                FirstName = employee.FirstName,
                LastName = employee.LastName,
                FullName = employee.FullName,
                ContactNumber = employee.ContactNumber,
                Email = employee.Email,
                Role = employee.Role,
                IsActive = employee.IsActive,
                Username = credential?.Username,
                LastLoginDate = credential?.LastLoginDate,
                HasCredential = credential != null
            });
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/employees/{id}/deactivate
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/deactivate")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> DeactivateEmployee(int companyId, int id)
        {
            if (!CallerIsAdmin())
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only admins can deactivate employees." });

            var callerId = GetCallerEmployeeId();
            if (callerId == id)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "You cannot deactivate your own account." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var employee = await tenant.Employees
                .Include(e => e.Credential)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == id);
            if (employee is null)
                return NotFound();

            if (employee.Role.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                employee.Role.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Admin accounts cannot be deactivated from this console." });

            employee.IsActive = false;
            employee.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            if (employee.Credential is not null)
            {
                employee.Credential.IsActive = false;
                employee.Credential.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            }

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return NoContent();
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/employees/{id}/reactivate
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/reactivate")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> ReactivateEmployee(int companyId, int id)
        {
            if (!CallerIsAdmin())
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only admins can reactivate employees." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var employee = await tenant.Employees
                .Include(e => e.Credential)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == id);

            if (employee is null)
                return NotFound();

            if (employee.Role.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                employee.Role.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Admin accounts cannot be reactivated from this console." });

            employee.IsActive = true;
            employee.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            if (employee.Credential is not null)
            {
                employee.Credential.IsActive = true;
                employee.Credential.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            }

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return NoContent();
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/employees/{id}/reset-password
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/reset-password")]
        public async Task<IActionResult> ResetPassword(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            ResetPasswordRequest request)
        {
            if (!CallerIsAdmin())
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only admins can reset passwords." });

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.NewPassword) || request.NewPassword.Length < 4)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Password must be at least 4 characters." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var credential = await tenant.Credentials
                .Include(c => c.Employee)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.EmployeeId == id);

            if (credential is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "This employee has no credential to reset." });

            if (credential.Employee is not null &&
                (credential.Employee.Role.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                 credential.Employee.Role.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Admin passwords cannot be reset from this console." });

            // Guard: cannot reset a password for an inactive employee. Reactivate first.
            if (credential.Employee is not null && !credential.Employee.IsActive)
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Inactive employees cannot have their password reset. Reactivate them first." });

            credential.PasswordHash = BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.HashPassword(request.NewPassword);
            credential.FailedLoginAttempts = 0;
            credential.IsLocked = false;
            credential.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return NoContent();
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Controllers/FeedbackController.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.Controllers
{
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/tenant/{companyId:int}/feedback")]
    public class FeedbackController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ITenantDbContextFactory _tenantFactory;

        public FeedbackController(ITenantDbContextFactory tenantFactory)
        {
            _tenantFactory = tenantFactory;
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/feedback?search=...
        // Returns all tickets (newest first), optionally filtered by customer name,
        // category, status, or exact ticket ID.
        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/feedback?search=...&page=1&pageSize=25
        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<ActionResult<PagedResponse<FeedbackResponseDto>>> GetTickets(
            int companyId,
            [FromQuery] string? search,
            [FromQuery] string? status = null,
            [FromQuery] int page = 1,
            [FromQuery] int pageSize = 25)
        {
            if (page < 1) page = 1;
            if (pageSize < 1) pageSize = 25;
            if (pageSize > 200) pageSize = 200;

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var query = tenant.Feedbacks
            .AsNoTracking()
            .Where(f => f.IsActive);

            // Status filter — exact match, case-insensitive. Null/empty/whitespace means
            // "no status filter" (all statuses returned). The view supplies "Pending"
            // by default; "All Statuses" maps to null.
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(status))
            {
                var statusTerm = status.Trim().ToLower();
                query = query.Where(f => f.Status.ToLower() == statusTerm);
            }

            // Search — matches customer name, category, ticket ID. Note: Status is NO
            // LONGER matched here — status filtering is the dropdown's job now, so the
            // search box has a single responsibility.
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(search))
            {
                var term = search.Trim();
                var asInt = int.TryParse(term, out var parsed) ? parsed : (int?)null;

                query = query.Where(f =>
                    (f.Customer != null && (f.Customer.FirstName + " " + f.Customer.LastName).Contains(term)) ||
                    f.Category.Contains(term) ||
                    (asInt.HasValue && f.TicketId == asInt.Value));
            }

            var totalCount = await query.CountAsync();

            var items = await query
                .OrderByDescending(f => f.DateLogged)
                .Skip((page - 1) * pageSize)
                .Take(pageSize)
                .Select(f => new FeedbackResponseDto
                {
                    TicketId = f.TicketId,
                    CustomerId = f.CustomerId,
                    CustomerName = f.Customer != null
                        ? (f.Customer.FirstName + " " + f.Customer.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    LoggedBy = f.LoggedBy,
                    LoggedByName = f.LoggedByEmployee != null
                        ? (f.LoggedByEmployee.FirstName + " " + f.LoggedByEmployee.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    Rating = f.Rating,
                    Category = f.Category,
                    StaffNotes = f.StaffNotes,
                    Status = f.Status,
                    DateLogged = f.DateLogged,
                    DecidedBy = f.DecidedBy,
                    DecidedAt = f.DecidedAt,
                    DecisionNotes = f.DecisionNotes
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(new PagedResponse<FeedbackResponseDto>
            {
                Items = items,
                TotalCount = totalCount,
                Page = page,
                PageSize = pageSize
            });
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/feedback/{id}
        [HttpGet("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<FeedbackResponseDto>> GetTicket(int companyId, int id)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var ticket = await tenant.Feedbacks
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(f => f.TicketId == id)
                .Select(f => new FeedbackResponseDto
                {
                    TicketId = f.TicketId,
                    CustomerId = f.CustomerId,
                    CustomerName = f.Customer != null
                        ? (f.Customer.FirstName + " " + f.Customer.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    LoggedBy = f.LoggedBy,
                    LoggedByName = f.LoggedByEmployee != null
                        ? (f.LoggedByEmployee.FirstName + " " + f.LoggedByEmployee.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    Rating = f.Rating,
                    Category = f.Category,
                    StaffNotes = f.StaffNotes,
                    Status = f.Status,
                    DateLogged = f.DateLogged,
                    DecidedBy = f.DecidedBy,
                    DecidedAt = f.DecidedAt,
                    DecisionNotes = f.DecisionNotes
                })
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

            if (ticket is null)
                return NotFound();

            return Ok(ticket);
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/feedback
        [HttpPost]
        public async Task<ActionResult<FeedbackResponseDto>> LogFeedback(
            int companyId,
            LogFeedbackRequest request)
        {
            // ---- Server-side validation ----
            if (request.CustomerId <= 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Customer is required." });
            if (request.LoggedBy <= 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Logged-by employee is required." });
            if (request.Rating < 1 || request.Rating > 5)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Rating must be between 1 and 5 stars." });
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Category))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Category is required." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            // Verify the customer and employee exist (FK safety + clear error)
            var customerExists = await tenant.Customers.AnyAsync(c => c.CustomerId == request.CustomerId);
            if (!customerExists)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Customer not found." });

            var logger = await tenant.Employees
                    .AsNoTracking()
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.LoggedBy);

            if (logger is null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Employee not found." });

            // Only Baristas log feedback. Managers decide on it; Admins have no role here.
            if (!logger.Role.Equals("Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only baristas can log feedback tickets." });

            var feedback = new Feedback
            {
                CustomerId = request.CustomerId,
                LoggedBy = request.LoggedBy,
                Rating = request.Rating,
                Category = request.Category.Trim(),
                StaffNotes = request.StaffNotes?.Trim() ?? string.Empty,
                Status = "Pending",
                DateLogged = DateTime.UtcNow,
                IsActive = true,
                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
            };

            tenant.Feedbacks.Add(feedback);

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            // Reload with the joined names for the response
            var result = await tenant.Feedbacks
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(f => f.TicketId == feedback.TicketId)
                .Select(f => new FeedbackResponseDto
                {
                    TicketId = f.TicketId,
                    CustomerId = f.CustomerId,
                    CustomerName = f.Customer != null
                        ? (f.Customer.FirstName + " " + f.Customer.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    LoggedBy = f.LoggedBy,
                    LoggedByName = f.LoggedByEmployee != null
                        ? (f.LoggedByEmployee.FirstName + " " + f.LoggedByEmployee.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    Rating = f.Rating,
                    Category = f.Category,
                    StaffNotes = f.StaffNotes,
                    Status = f.Status,
                    DateLogged = f.DateLogged,
                    DecidedBy = f.DecidedBy,
                    DecidedAt = f.DecidedAt,
