                    DecisionNotes = f.DecisionNotes
                })
                .FirstAsync();

            return Ok(result);
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/feedback/{id}/cancel
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/cancel")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<FeedbackResponseDto>> CancelFeedback(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            [FromBody] CancelFeedbackRequest request)
        {
            if (request.CancelledBy <= 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Cancelling employee ID is required." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var feedback = await tenant.Feedbacks
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(f => f.TicketId == id);

            if (feedback is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "Ticket not found." });

            // ---- Business rules (moved here from the entity) ----
            if (feedback.LoggedBy != request.CancelledBy)
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only the employee who logged this ticket can cancel it." });

            if (!feedback.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = $"Only pending tickets can be cancelled (current status: {feedback.Status})." });

            // ---- Apply the cancellation ----
            feedback.Status = "Cancelled";
            feedback.IsActive = false;
            feedback.DecidedBy = request.CancelledBy;
            feedback.DecidedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            feedback.DecisionNotes = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Reason) ? null : request.Reason.Trim();
            feedback.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            // Return updated ticket
            var result = await tenant.Feedbacks
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(f => f.TicketId == feedback.TicketId)
                .Select(f => new FeedbackResponseDto
                {
                    TicketId = f.TicketId,
                    CustomerId = f.CustomerId,
                    CustomerName = f.Customer != null
                        ? (f.Customer.FirstName + " " + f.Customer.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    LoggedBy = f.LoggedBy,
                    LoggedByName = f.LoggedByEmployee != null
                        ? (f.LoggedByEmployee.FirstName + " " + f.LoggedByEmployee.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    Rating = f.Rating,
                    Category = f.Category,
                    StaffNotes = f.StaffNotes,
                    Status = f.Status,
                    DateLogged = f.DateLogged,
                    DecidedBy = f.DecidedBy,
                    DecidedAt = f.DecidedAt,
                    DecisionNotes = f.DecisionNotes
                })
                .FirstAsync();

            return Ok(result);
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/feedback/{id}/resolve
        //
        // Single decision endpoint. BonusPoints == 0 means "resolved, no gesture"
        // (the dismiss case). BonusPoints > 0 also writes a PointLedger row and bumps
        // the customer's PointsBalance. Manager-only. Only Pending tickets can be
        // resolved. Notes are optional and land in DecisionNotes only.
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/resolve")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<FeedbackResponseDto>> ResolveFeedback(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            ResolveFeedbackRequest request)
        {
            if (request.DecidedBy <= 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Deciding employee ID is required." });

            if (request.BonusPoints < 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Bonus points cannot be negative." });

            if (request.BonusPoints > 50)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Bonus points cannot exceed 50." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var decider = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.DecidedBy);

            if (decider is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "Deciding employee not found." });

            if (!decider.Role.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only managers can resolve feedback tickets." });

            var feedback = await tenant.Feedbacks
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(f => f.TicketId == id);

            if (feedback is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "Ticket not found." });

            if (!feedback.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = $"Only pending tickets can be resolved (current status: {feedback.Status})." });

            // Apply the decision
            feedback.Status = "Resolved";
            feedback.DecidedBy = request.DecidedBy;
            feedback.DecidedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            feedback.DecisionNotes = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Notes) ? null : request.Notes.Trim();
            feedback.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            // Bonus points, if any
            if (request.BonusPoints > 0)
            {
                var customer = await tenant.Customers
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CustomerId == feedback.CustomerId);

                if (customer is null)
                    return NotFound(new { message = "Customer not found." });

                customer.PointsBalance += request.BonusPoints;
                customer.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

                // NOTE: deliberately NOT touching customer.LastVisitDate. A feedback
                // bonus is bookkeeping, not a visit — writing it there would corrupt
                // the 14-day activity threshold used by the Status line and the
                // "We Miss You" promo eligibility rule.

                tenant.PointLedgers.Add(new PointLedger
                {
                    CustomerId = customer.CustomerId,
                    PointsChange = request.BonusPoints,
                    Type = "FeedbackBonus",
                    Reason = $"Feedback ticket #{feedback.TicketId} — {feedback.Category}",
                    PerformedBy = request.DecidedBy,
                    Timestamp = DateTime.UtcNow,
                    IsActive = true,
                    CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                    UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                });
            }

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            // Return updated ticket
            var result = await tenant.Feedbacks
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(f => f.TicketId == feedback.TicketId)
                .Select(f => new FeedbackResponseDto
                {
                    TicketId = f.TicketId,
                    CustomerId = f.CustomerId,
                    CustomerName = f.Customer != null
                        ? (f.Customer.FirstName + " " + f.Customer.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    LoggedBy = f.LoggedBy,
                    LoggedByName = f.LoggedByEmployee != null
                        ? (f.LoggedByEmployee.FirstName + " " + f.LoggedByEmployee.LastName).Trim()
                        : "—",
                    Rating = f.Rating,
                    Category = f.Category,
                    StaffNotes = f.StaffNotes,
                    Status = f.Status,
                    DateLogged = f.DateLogged,
                    DecidedBy = f.DecidedBy,
                    DecidedAt = f.DecidedAt,
                    DecisionNotes = f.DecisionNotes
                })
                .FirstAsync();

            return Ok(result);
        }
    }
}
    

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Controllers/OrderController.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.Controllers
{
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/tenant/{companyId:int}/orders")]
    public class OrdersController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ITenantDbContextFactory _tenantFactory;

        public OrdersController(ITenantDbContextFactory tenantFactory)
        {
            _tenantFactory = tenantFactory;
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/orders
        // Receives a completed cart from the POS. The server RE-COMPUTES every total from
        // the raw product/size/modifier IDs — it does NOT trust the client's numbers.
        //
        // PWD/Senior handling (stacked on top of promo, standard PH method):
        //   1. SubTotal = sum of line totals (recomputed from DB prices)
        //   2. PromoDiscount = campaign discount applied to SubTotal
        //   3. AfterPromo = SubTotal - PromoDiscount
        //   4. NetOfVat = AfterPromo / 1.12
        //   5. PwdDiscount = NetOfVat * 0.20  (only if HasPwdSeniorDiscount)
        //   6. TotalAmount = NetOfVat - PwdDiscount
        //   7. PointsEarned = (int)(TotalAmount / 10)
        [HttpPost]
        public async Task<ActionResult<OrderResponseDto>> CompleteOrder(
            int companyId,
            CompleteOrderRequest request)
        {
            // ---- Request-level validation ----
            if (request.CustomerId <= 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Customer is required." });
            if (request.CreatedBy <= 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Created-by employee is required." });
            if (request.Lines is null || request.Lines.Count == 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Order must have at least one line." });
            if (request.Lines.Any(l => l.Quantity <= 0))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Line quantity must be greater than zero." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            // ---- Verify customer and employee exist ----
            var customer = await tenant.Customers
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CustomerId == request.CustomerId);
            if (customer is null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Customer not found." });

            var employeeExists = await tenant.Employees
                .AnyAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.CreatedBy);
            if (!employeeExists)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Employee not found." });

            // ---- Look up all products / sizes / modifiers referenced ----
            var productIds = request.Lines.Select(l => l.ProductId).Distinct().ToList();
            var sizeIds = request.Lines.Select(l => l.ProductSizeId).Distinct().ToList();
            var modifierIds = request.Lines.SelectMany(l => l.ModifierIds).Distinct().ToList();

            var products = await tenant.Products
                .Where(p => productIds.Contains(p.ProductId))
                .ToListAsync();

            var sizes = await tenant.ProductSizes
                .Where(s => sizeIds.Contains(s.ProductSizeId))
                .ToListAsync();

            var modifiers = await tenant.ProductModifiers
                .Where(m => modifierIds.Contains(m.ProductModifierId))
                .ToListAsync();

            // ---- Build the Order entity, computing everything server-side ----
            var order = new Order
            {
                CustomerId = request.CustomerId,
                CreatedBy = request.CreatedBy,
                OrderDate = DateTime.UtcNow,
                Status = "Completed",
                IsActive = true,
                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                HasPwdSeniorDiscount = request.HasPwdSeniorDiscount,
            };

            decimal subTotal = 0m;

            foreach (var line in request.Lines)
            {
                var product = products.FirstOrDefault(p => p.ProductId == line.ProductId);
                var size = sizes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.ProductSizeId == line.ProductSizeId);

                if (product is null)
                    return BadRequest(new { message = $"Product {line.ProductId} not found." });
                if (size is null)
                    return BadRequest(new { message = $"Product size {line.ProductSizeId} not found." });
                if (size.ProductId != product.ProductId)
                    return BadRequest(new { message = $"Size {size.ProductSizeId} does not belong to product {product.ProductId}." });

                var orderItem = new OrderItem
                {
                    ProductId = product.ProductId,
                    ProductSizeId = size.ProductSizeId,
                    SizeNameSnapshot = size.SizeName,
                    BaseSizePrice = size.Price,
                    Quantity = line.Quantity,
                };

                decimal lineUnitPrice = size.Price;

                foreach (var modId in line.ModifierIds)
                {
                    var modifier = modifiers.FirstOrDefault(m => m.ProductModifierId == modId);
                    if (modifier is null)
                        return BadRequest(new { message = $"Modifier {modId} not found." });

                    orderItem.OrderItemModifiers.Add(new OrderItemModifier
                    {
                        ProductModifierId = modifier.ProductModifierId,
                        ProductModifierNameSnapshot = modifier.Name,
                        UnitPrice = modifier.Price,
                    });

                    lineUnitPrice += modifier.Price;
                }

                orderItem.UnitPrice = lineUnitPrice;
                orderItem.SubTotal = lineUnitPrice * line.Quantity;

                order.OrderItems.Add(orderItem);
                subTotal += orderItem.SubTotal;
            }

            order.SubTotal = subTotal;

            // ---- Promo ----
            decimal promoDiscount = 0m;

            if (request.CampaignId.HasValue)
            {
                var campaign = await tenant.Campaigns
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CampaignId == request.CampaignId.Value);

                if (campaign is null)
                    return BadRequest(new { message = $"Campaign {request.CampaignId.Value} not found." });
                if (!campaign.IsActive || DateTime.UtcNow < campaign.StartDate || DateTime.UtcNow > campaign.EndDate)
                    return BadRequest(new { message = "Campaign is not currently active." });

                promoDiscount = CalculatePromoDiscount(campaign, order.SubTotal);

                order.CampaignRedeems.Add(new CampaignRedeem
                {
                    CampaignId = campaign.CampaignId,
                    DiscountUsed = promoDiscount,
                    RedeemDate = DateTime.UtcNow,
                    IsActive = true,
                    CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                    UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                });
            }

            order.TotalDiscount = promoDiscount;

            // ---- PWD/Senior (stacked, standard PH method) ----
            // Compute from the post-promo net, VAT-exempted first, then 20% off.
            var afterPromo = Math.Max(0m, order.SubTotal - order.TotalDiscount);

            if (request.HasPwdSeniorDiscount)
            {
                var netOfVat = afterPromo / 1.12m;
                var pwdDiscount = decimal.Round(netOfVat * 0.20m, 2);

                order.PwdSeniorDiscountAmount = pwdDiscount;
                order.TotalAmount = decimal.Round(netOfVat - pwdDiscount, 2);
            }
            else
            {
                order.PwdSeniorDiscountAmount = 0m;
                order.TotalAmount = afterPromo;
            }

            // ---- Points: 1 per ₱10 net spend, on the FINAL total ----
            order.PointsEarned = (int)(order.TotalAmount / 10m);

            // ---- Write the Order first so OrderId is populated for the ledger ----
            tenant.Orders.Add(order);

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            // ---- Award points + write ledger row ----
            if (order.PointsEarned > 0)
            {
                customer.PointsBalance += order.PointsEarned;
                customer.LastVisitDate = DateTime.UtcNow;
                customer.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

                tenant.PointLedgers.Add(new PointLedger
                {
                    CustomerId = customer.CustomerId,
                    PointsChange = order.PointsEarned,
                    Type = "Earned",
                    Reason = $"Order #{order.OrderId} Spend",
                    OrderId = order.OrderId,
                    Timestamp = DateTime.UtcNow,
                    IsActive = true,
                    CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                    UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                });

                try
                {
                    await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
                }
                catch (DbUpdateException ex)
                {
                    return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
                }
            }

            return Ok(new OrderResponseDto
            {
                OrderId = order.OrderId,
                CustomerId = order.CustomerId,
                CustomerName = (customer.FirstName + " " + customer.LastName).Trim(),
                CreatedBy = order.CreatedBy,
                SubTotal = order.SubTotal,
                TotalDiscount = order.TotalDiscount,
                TotalAmount = order.TotalAmount,
                PointsEarned = order.PointsEarned,
                OrderDate = order.OrderDate,
                Status = order.Status,
            });
        }

        private static decimal CalculatePromoDiscount(Campaign campaign, decimal orderTotal)
        {
            if (orderTotal <= 0) return 0m;
            if (campaign.MinPurchaseAmount.HasValue && orderTotal < campaign.MinPurchaseAmount.Value)
                return 0m;

            decimal discount;
            if (campaign.DiscountType.Equals("Percentage", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                discount = orderTotal * (campaign.DiscountValue / 100m);
                if (campaign.MaxDiscountAmount.HasValue && discount > campaign.MaxDiscountAmount.Value)
                    discount = campaign.MaxDiscountAmount.Value;
            }
            else
            {
                discount = campaign.DiscountValue;
            }

            return discount > orderTotal ? orderTotal : discount;
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Controllers/PointAdjustmentController.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Point-adjustment endpoints. Business rules (role checks, self-approval
    /// prevention, balance update, ledger write) live here on the server — the
    /// client cannot bypass them.
    /// </summary>
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/tenant/{companyId:int}/point-adjustments")]
    public class PointAdjustmentController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ITenantDbContextFactory _tenantFactory;

        public PointAdjustmentController(ITenantDbContextFactory tenantFactory)
        {
            _tenantFactory = tenantFactory;
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/point-adjustments/pending?customerId=...
        // If customerId is provided, returns only that customer's pending requests.
        // Otherwise returns every pending request.
        [HttpGet("pending")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<List<PointAdjustmentResponseDto>>> GetPending(
            int companyId,
            [FromQuery] int? customerId)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var query = tenant.PointAdjustmentRequests
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(r => r.Status == "Pending");

            if (customerId.HasValue)
                query = query.Where(r => r.CustomerId == customerId.Value);

            var results = await query
                .OrderBy(r => r.RequestedAt)
                .Select(r => new PointAdjustmentResponseDto
                {
                    PointAdjustmentRequestId = r.PointAdjustmentRequestId,
                    CustomerId = r.CustomerId,
                    PointsChange = r.PointsChange,
                    Reason = r.Reason,
                    RequestedBy = r.RequestedBy,
                    RequestedAt = r.RequestedAt,
                    Status = r.Status,
                    ApprovedBy = r.ApprovedBy,
                    DecidedAt = r.DecidedAt,
                    DecisionNotes = r.DecisionNotes
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(results);
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/point-adjustments
        // Creates a Pending request. Role check: requester must be Manager+.
        [HttpPost]
        public async Task<ActionResult<PointAdjustmentResponseDto>> RequestAdjustment(
            int companyId,
            CreatePointAdjustmentDto request)
        {
            if (request.PointsChange == 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Points change cannot be zero." });
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Reason))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "A reason is required." });
            if (request.RequestedBy <= 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Requesting employee ID is required." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            // Role check on the server
            var requester = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.RequestedBy);

            if (requester is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "Requesting employee not found." });

            // Only Baristas submit point-adjustment requests. Managers decide them;
            // Admins have no role in this chain at all.
            if (!requester.IsActive)
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Inactive employee cannot request adjustments." });

            if (!requester.Role.Equals("Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only baristas can submit point adjustment requests." });

            var customer = await tenant.Customers
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CustomerId == request.CustomerId);

            if (customer is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "Customer not found." });

            var entity = new PointAdjustmentRequest
            {
                CustomerId = request.CustomerId,
                PointsChange = request.PointsChange,
                Reason = request.Reason.Trim(),
                RequestedBy = request.RequestedBy,
                RequestedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                Status = "Pending",
                IsActive = true,
                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
            };

            tenant.PointAdjustmentRequests.Add(entity);

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return Ok(MapToDto(entity));
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/point-adjustments/{id}/approve
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/approve")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<PointAdjustmentResponseDto>> Approve(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            DecidePointAdjustmentDto request)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var approver = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.DecidedBy);

            if (approver is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "Approving employee not found." });
            if (!IsManagerOnly(approver.Role))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only managers can approve point adjustment requests." });

            var entity = await tenant.PointAdjustmentRequests
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(r => r.PointAdjustmentRequestId == id);

            if (entity is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "Request not found." });
            if (!entity.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return Conflict(new { message = $"This request has already been {entity.Status}." });
            if (entity.RequestedBy == request.DecidedBy)
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "An employee cannot approve their own adjustment request." });

            var customer = await tenant.Customers
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(c => c.CustomerId == entity.CustomerId);

            if (customer is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "Customer not found." });

            var newBalance = customer.PointsBalance + entity.PointsChange;
            if (newBalance < 0)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Adjustment would result in a negative points balance." });

            // Apply the change. NOTE: deliberately NOT touching customer.LastVisitDate —
            // that field is "when the customer last made a transaction," and a points
            // adjustment (an admin bookkeeping action) is not a visit. Writing it here
            // would corrupt the 14-day activity threshold used by the Status line and by
            // the "We Miss You" promo eligibility rule.
            customer.PointsBalance = newBalance;
            customer.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            // Write the ledger row
            tenant.PointLedgers.Add(new PointLedger
            {
                CustomerId = customer.CustomerId,
                PointsChange = entity.PointsChange,
                Type = "Adjustment",
                Reason = entity.Reason,
                PerformedBy = request.DecidedBy,
                Timestamp = DateTime.UtcNow,
                IsActive = true,
                CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
            });

            // Mark the request decided
            entity.Status = "Approved";
            entity.ApprovedBy = request.DecidedBy;
            entity.DecidedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            entity.DecisionNotes = request.Notes?.Trim();
            entity.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return Ok(MapToDto(entity));
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/point-adjustments/{id}/reject
        [HttpPost("{id:int}/reject")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<PointAdjustmentResponseDto>> Reject(
            int companyId,
            int id,
            DecidePointAdjustmentDto request)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(request.Notes))
                return BadRequest(new { message = "A rejection reason is required." });

            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var approver = await tenant.Employees
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EmployeeId == request.DecidedBy);

            if (approver is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "Approving employee not found." });
            if (!IsManagerOnly(approver.Role))
                return StatusCode(403, new { message = "Only managers can reject point adjustment requests." });

            var entity = await tenant.PointAdjustmentRequests
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(r => r.PointAdjustmentRequestId == id);

            if (entity is null)
                return NotFound(new { message = "Request not found." });
            if (!entity.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return Conflict(new { message = $"This request has already been {entity.Status}." });

            entity.Status = "Rejected";
            entity.ApprovedBy = request.DecidedBy;
            entity.DecidedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;
            entity.DecisionNotes = request.Notes.Trim();
            entity.UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow;

            try
            {
                await tenant.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (DbUpdateException ex)
            {
                return Conflict(new { message = ex.InnerException?.Message ?? ex.Message });
            }

            return Ok(MapToDto(entity));
        }

        // ---- Helpers ----

        // "Manager only" means exactly the Manager role. Admin and SuperAdmin are NOT
        // approvers in the point-adjustment chain — Barista submits, Manager decides.
        private static bool IsManagerOnly(string role) =>
            role.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

        private static PointAdjustmentResponseDto MapToDto(PointAdjustmentRequest r) => new()
        {
            PointAdjustmentRequestId = r.PointAdjustmentRequestId,
            CustomerId = r.CustomerId,
            PointsChange = r.PointsChange,
            Reason = r.Reason,
            RequestedBy = r.RequestedBy,
            RequestedAt = r.RequestedAt,
            Status = r.Status,
            ApprovedBy = r.ApprovedBy,
            DecidedAt = r.DecidedAt,
            DecisionNotes = r.DecisionNotes,
        };
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Controllers/ProductController.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.Controllers
{
    [ApiController]
    [Route("api/tenant/{companyId:int}/products")]
    public class ProductController : ControllerBase
    {
        private readonly ITenantDbContextFactory _tenantFactory;

        public ProductController(ITenantDbContextFactory tenantFactory)
        {
            _tenantFactory = tenantFactory;
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/products
        // Returns all active, available products with their sizes nested.
        [HttpGet]
        public async Task<ActionResult<List<ProductResponseDto>>> GetProducts(int companyId)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var products = await tenant.Products
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(p => p.IsActive && p.IsAvailable)
                .OrderBy(p => p.ProductId)
                .Select(p => new ProductResponseDto
                {
                    ProductId = p.ProductId,
                    ProductCode = p.ProductCode,
                    ProductName = p.ProductName,
                    ProductCategory = p.ProductCategory,
                    BasePrice = p.BasePrice,
                    ImageUrl = p.ImageUrl,
                    LocalImagePath = p.LocalImagePath,
                    IsAvailable = p.IsAvailable,
                    Sizes = p.ProductSizes
                        .Where(s => s.IsActive)
                        .OrderBy(s => s.Price)
                        .Select(s => new ProductSizeResponseDto
                        {
                            ProductSizeId = s.ProductSizeId,
                            SizeName = s.SizeName,
                            Price = s.Price
                        })
                        .ToList()
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(products);
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/products/{id}
        [HttpGet("{id:int}")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<ProductResponseDto>> GetProduct(int companyId, int id)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var product = await tenant.Products
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(p => p.ProductId == id)
                .Select(p => new ProductResponseDto
                {
                    ProductId = p.ProductId,
                    ProductCode = p.ProductCode,
                    ProductName = p.ProductName,
                    ProductCategory = p.ProductCategory,
                    BasePrice = p.BasePrice,
                    ImageUrl = p.ImageUrl,
                    LocalImagePath = p.LocalImagePath,
                    IsAvailable = p.IsAvailable,
                    Sizes = p.ProductSizes
                        .Where(s => s.IsActive)
                        .OrderBy(s => s.Price)
                        .Select(s => new ProductSizeResponseDto
                        {
                            ProductSizeId = s.ProductSizeId,
                            SizeName = s.SizeName,
                            Price = s.Price
                        })
                        .ToList()
                })
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync();

            if (product is null)
                return NotFound();

            return Ok(product);
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/products/categories
        // Returns distinct categories as a plain list of strings.
        [HttpGet("categories")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<List<string>>> GetCategories(int companyId)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var categories = await tenant.Products
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(p => p.IsActive && p.IsAvailable)
                .Select(p => p.ProductCategory)
                .Distinct()
                .OrderBy(c => c)
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(categories);
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/products/modifiers
        // Returns all active product modifiers (the add-ons checklist).
        [HttpGet("modifiers")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<List<ProductModifierResponseDto>>> GetModifiers(int companyId)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var modifiers = await tenant.ProductModifiers
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(m => m.IsActive)
                .OrderBy(m => m.ProductModifierId)
                .Select(m => new ProductModifierResponseDto
                {
                    ProductModifierId = m.ProductModifierId,
                    Name = m.Name,
                    Category = m.Category,
                    Price = m.Price
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(modifiers);
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/products/full-catalog
        // Returns products (with sizes), categories, and modifiers in ONE response, so the
        // client needs a single HTTP call instead of three. Each API call here involves a
        // tenant DB round-trip, so combining them is a meaningful speed-up.
        [HttpGet("full-catalog")]
        public async Task<ActionResult<FullCatalogResponseDto>> GetFullCatalog(int companyId)
        {
            await using var tenant = await _tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

            var products = await tenant.Products
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(p => p.IsActive && p.IsAvailable)
                .OrderBy(p => p.ProductId)
                .Select(p => new ProductResponseDto
                {
                    ProductId = p.ProductId,
                    ProductCode = p.ProductCode,
                    ProductName = p.ProductName,
                    ProductCategory = p.ProductCategory,
                    BasePrice = p.BasePrice,
                    ImageUrl = p.ImageUrl,
                    LocalImagePath = p.LocalImagePath,
                    IsAvailable = p.IsAvailable,
                    Sizes = p.ProductSizes
                        .Where(s => s.IsActive)
                        .OrderBy(s => s.Price)
                        .Select(s => new ProductSizeResponseDto
                        {
                            ProductSizeId = s.ProductSizeId,
                            SizeName = s.SizeName,
                            Price = s.Price
                        })
                        .ToList()
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            var categories = products
                .Select(p => p.ProductCategory)
                .Distinct()
                .OrderBy(c => c)
                .ToList();

            var modifiers = await tenant.ProductModifiers
                .AsNoTracking()
                .Where(m => m.IsActive)
                .OrderBy(m => m.ProductModifierId)
                .Select(m => new ProductModifierResponseDto
                {
                    ProductModifierId = m.ProductModifierId,
                    Name = m.Name,
                    Category = m.Category,
                    Price = m.Price
                })
                .ToListAsync();

            return Ok(new FullCatalogResponseDto
            {
                Products = products,
                Categories = categories,
                Modifiers = modifiers
            });
        }
    }
}

﻿namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

public class CampaignResponseDto
{
    public int CampaignId { get; set; }
    public string PromoName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string PromoCategory { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string DiscountType { get; set; } = "Percentage";
    public decimal DiscountValue { get; set; }
    public string TargetTier { get; set; } = "All";
    public decimal? MinPurchaseAmount { get; set; }
    public decimal? MaxDiscountAmount { get; set; }
    public int MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer { get; set; }
    public DateTime StartDate { get; set; }
    public DateTime EndDate { get; set; }
    public bool IsActive { get; set; }
    public string Status { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    // Who created the campaign. CreatedByName is resolved from the Creator nav property
    // so the client doesn't have to do a second lookup.
    public int MadeBy { get; set; }
    public string CreatedByName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    // Decision attribution — populated once the campaign leaves "Pending".
    public int? DecidedBy { get; set; }
    public DateTime? DecidedAt { get; set; }
    public string? DecisionNotes { get; set; }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/DTOs/CreatePointAdjustmentDto.cs =====
﻿namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

public class CreatePointAdjustmentDto
{
    public int CustomerId { get; set; }
    public int PointsChange { get; set; }

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/DTOs/PagedResponse.cs =====
﻿namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

/// <summary>
/// Standard shape for any paginated list endpoint. Reused by Feedback, Customers,
/// Products — anything that returns rows in pages.
/// </summary>
public class PagedResponse<T>
{
    public List<T> Items { get; set; } = new();
    public int TotalCount { get; set; }
    public int Page { get; set; }
    public int PageSize { get; set; }

    public int TotalPages => PageSize <= 0
        ? 0
        : (int)Math.Ceiling((double)TotalCount / PageSize);

    public bool HasPrevious => Page > 1;
    public bool HasNext => Page < TotalPages;
}

﻿namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

public class ResolveFeedbackRequest
{
    /// <summary>The Manager making the decision. Server verifies the role.</summary>
    public int DecidedBy { get; set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Bonus points awarded as part of the resolution. 0 means "resolved, no gesture"
    /// (the dismiss case). Maximum enforced server-side at 50.
    /// </summary>
    public int BonusPoints { get; set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Optional note from the Manager. Goes into Feedback.DecisionNotes only; the
    /// PointLedger row (when BonusPoints > 0) uses a fixed auto-generated Reason so
    /// the points grid stays scannable.
    /// </summary>
    public string? Notes { get; set; }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Program.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.Services;
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.data;
using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authentication.JwtBearer;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
using System.Text;

var builder = WebApplication.CreateBuilder(args);

builder.Services.AddDbContext<MasterCrmDbContext>(options =>
  options.UseSqlServer(
    builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("LocalMasterCrm")));

builder.Services.AddDbContext<TenantCrmDbContext>(options =>
  options.UseSqlServer(
    builder.Configuration.GetConnectionString("LocalTenantCrm")));

builder.Services.AddControllers();
// Learn more about configuring OpenAPI at https://aka.ms/aspnet/openapi

var jwtSection = builder.Configuration.GetSection("Jwt");
var jwtSecret = jwtSection["Secret"]
    ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Jwt:Secret is not configured.");
var jwtIssuer = jwtSection["Issuer"] ?? "MorcaffeinApi";
var jwtAudience = jwtSection["Audience"] ?? "MorcaffeinClient";

builder.Services
    .AddAuthentication(JwtBearerDefaults.AuthenticationScheme)
    .AddJwtBearer(options =>
    {
        options.TokenValidationParameters = new TokenValidationParameters
        {
            ValidateIssuer = true,
            ValidateAudience = true,
            ValidateLifetime = true,
            ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,
            ValidIssuer = jwtIssuer,
            ValidAudience = jwtAudience,
            IssuerSigningKey = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(jwtSecret)),
            ClockSkew = TimeSpan.Zero   // no grace period on expiry — 8h means 8h
        };
    });

builder.Services.AddAuthorization();

builder.Services.AddScoped<JwtTokenService>();

builder.Services.AddOpenApi();

builder.Services.AddScoped<ITenantDatabaseResolver, TenantDatabaseResolver>();
builder.Services.AddScoped<ITenantDbContextFactory, TenantDbContextFactory>();

var app = builder.Build();

// Configure the HTTP request pipeline.
if (app.Environment.IsDevelopment())
{
    app.MapOpenApi();
}

app.UseAuthentication();
app.UseAuthorization();

app.MapControllers();

app.MapPost("/companies", async (
    
    Company company,
    
    MasterCrmDbContext db) =>
{

    db.Companies.Add(company);

    await db.SaveChangesAsync();

    return Results.Created($"/companies/{company.CompanyId}", company);
});

app.MapPost("/devices", async (

  Device device,

  MasterCrmDbContext db) =>

{

    db.Devices.Add(device);

    await db.SaveChangesAsync();



    return Results.Created($"/devices/{device.DeviceId}", device);

});

app.MapPost("/company-databases", async (

  CompanyDatabase companyDatabase,

  MasterCrmDbContext db) =>

{

    db.CompanyDatabases.Add(companyDatabase);

    await db.SaveChangesAsync();



    return Results.Created(

      $"/company-databases/{companyDatabase.CompanyDatabaseId}",

      companyDatabase);

});

app.MapGet("/test-tenant/{companyId:int}", async (
    int companyId,
    ITenantDbContextFactory tenantFactory) =>
{
    await using var tenantDb = await tenantFactory.CreateAsync(companyId);

    var customerCount = await tenantDb.Customers.CountAsync();

    return Results.Ok(new
    {
        companyId,
        customerCount
    });
});

app.Run();


===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.api/Services/JwtTokenService.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
using System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt;
using System.Security.Claims;
using System.Text;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.api.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Builds and signs JWTs for authenticated employees. Reads the Jwt:Secret/Issuer/
    /// Audience/ExpiryHours values from appsettings.json.
    /// </summary>
    public class JwtTokenService
    {
        private readonly string _secret;
        private readonly string _issuer;
        private readonly string _audience;
        private readonly int _expiryHours;

        public JwtTokenService(IConfiguration configuration)
        {
            var section = configuration.GetSection("Jwt");
            _secret = section["Secret"] ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Jwt:Secret is not configured.");
            _issuer = section["Issuer"] ?? "MorcaffeinApi";
            _audience = section["Audience"] ?? "MorcaffeinClient";
            _expiryHours = int.TryParse(section["ExpiryHours"], out var h) ? h : 8;
        }

        public DateTime GetExpiryUtc() => DateTime.UtcNow.AddHours(_expiryHours);

        public string CreateToken(Employee employee, int companyId)
        {
            var expiresAt = GetExpiryUtc();

            var claims = new[]
            {
                new Claim("employeeId", employee.EmployeeId.ToString()),
                new Claim("companyId", companyId.ToString()),
                new Claim(ClaimTypes.Role, employee.Role),
                new Claim(ClaimTypes.Name, employee.FullName),
            };

            var key = new SymmetricSecurityKey(Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(_secret));
            var credentials = new SigningCredentials(key, SecurityAlgorithms.HmacSha256);

            var token = new JwtSecurityToken(
                issuer: _issuer,
                audience: _audience,
                claims: claims,
                notBefore: DateTime.UtcNow,
                expires: expiresAt,
                signingCredentials: credentials);

            return new JwtSecurityTokenHandler().WriteToken(token);
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.domain/entities/BasePerson.cs =====
namespace CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;

public abstract class BasePerson : BaseEntity
{
    public string FirstName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string LastName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string ContactNumber { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string Email { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    // Plain formatting, not a business rule — kept as a computed property rather than
    // moved to a Controller since it has no validation and no external dependency.
    public string FullName => $"{FirstName} {LastName}".Trim();
}


===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.domain/entities/Campaign.cs =====
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;

public class Campaign : BaseEntity
{
    public int CampaignId { get; set; }
    public string PromoName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string PromoCategory { get; set; } = string.Empty;

    public string DiscountType { get; set; } = "Percentage";

    [Column(TypeName = "decimal(18,2)")]
    public decimal DiscountValue { get; set; }

    public string TargetTier { get; set; } = "All";

    [Column(TypeName = "decimal(18,2)")]
    public decimal? MinPurchaseAmount { get; set; }

    [Column(TypeName = "decimal(18,2)")]
    public decimal? MaxDiscountAmount { get; set; }

    public int MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer { get; set; } = 1;

    public int? RequiredProductId { get; set; }
    public int? RewardProductId { get; set; }

    public DateTime StartDate { get; set; }
    public DateTime EndDate { get; set; }

    public int MadeBy { get; set; }

    public string Status { get; set; } = "Active";
    public int? DecidedBy { get; set; }
    public DateTime? DecidedAt { get; set; }
    public string? DecisionNotes { get; set; }

    public virtual Employee? Creator { get; set; }
    public virtual Employee? Decider { get; set; }
    public virtual Product? RequiredProduct { get; set; }
    public virtual Product? RewardProduct { get; set; }
    public virtual ICollection<CampaignRedeem> CampaignRedeems { get; set; } = new List<CampaignRedeem>();
}

using System;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;

public class Feedback : BaseEntity
{
    public int TicketId { get; set; }
    public int CustomerId { get; set; }
    public int LoggedBy { get; set; }
    public int Rating { get; set; } // CSAT score 1 to 5
    public string Category { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string StaffNotes { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string Status { get; set; } = "Pending"; // "Pending", "Resolved", "Dismissed"
    public DateTime DateLogged { get; set; } = DateTime.UtcNow;

    // Decision attribution — populated only once Status leaves "Pending".
    public int? DecidedBy { get; set; }
    public DateTime? DecidedAt { get; set; }
    public string? DecisionNotes { get; set; }

    public virtual Customer? Customer { get; set; }
    public virtual Employee? LoggedByEmployee { get; set; }
    public virtual Employee? DecidedByEmployee { get; set; }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.domain/entities/OrderItem.cs =====
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;

public class OrderItem
{
    public int OrderItemId { get; set; }
    public int OrderId { get; set; }
    public int ProductId { get; set; }
    public int ProductSizeId { get; set; }
    public string SizeNameSnapshot { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public decimal BaseSizePrice { get; set; }
    public int Quantity { get; set; }

    // Stored, not computed — TransactionController recalculates and assigns these whenever
    // the line's quantity or modifiers change (UnitPrice = BaseSizePrice + sum of modifier
    // prices; SubTotal = Quantity * UnitPrice).
    public decimal UnitPrice { get; set; }
    public decimal SubTotal { get; set; }

    public virtual Order? Order { get; set; }
    public virtual Product? Product { get; set; }
    public virtual ProductSize? ProductSize { get; set; }
    public virtual ICollection<OrderItemModifier> OrderItemModifiers { get; set; } = new List<OrderItemModifier>();
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.domain/entities/PromoCategories.cs =====
﻿namespace CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;

/// <summary>
/// The set of promo-category strings the system understands. Referenced by
/// TransactionController (promo eligibility rules) and by the campaign create form
/// (so the dropdown items come from one place). Previously these were raw string
/// literals scattered across the codebase.
///
/// Not every category has special gating logic — "Seasonal" and "Loyalty" are
/// simply descriptive; "Birthday" and "Inactive" trigger eligibility checks.
/// </summary>
public static class PromoCategories
{
    /// <summary>General discount with no gating beyond dates/tier/spend.</summary>
    public const string Seasonal = "Seasonal";

    /// <summary>Birthday-today eligibility gate.</summary>
    public const string Birthday = "Birthday";

    /// <summary>Inactive-14-days eligibility gate.</summary>
    public const string Inactive = "Inactive";

    /// <summary>Tier-based loyalty promo.</summary>
    public const string Loyalty = "Loyalty";

    /// <summary>Wildcard tier value — "this promo applies to any tier."</summary>
    public const string AllTiers = "All";
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure/data/AuditInterceptor.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Diagnostics;
using System;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.data;

public class AuditInterceptor : SaveChangesInterceptor
{
    public override InterceptionResult<int> SavingChanges(DbContextEventData eventData, InterceptionResult<int> result)
    {
        UpdateTimestamps(eventData.Context);
        return base.SavingChanges(eventData, result);
    }

    public override ValueTask<InterceptionResult<int>> SavingChangesAsync(
        DbContextEventData eventData,
        InterceptionResult<int> result,
        CancellationToken cancellationToken = default)
    {
        UpdateTimestamps(eventData.Context);
        return base.SavingChangesAsync(eventData, result, cancellationToken);
    }

    private static void UpdateTimestamps(DbContext? context)
    {
        if (context == null) return;

        var entries = context.ChangeTracker.Entries<BaseEntity>()
            .Where(e => e.State == EntityState.Added || e.State == EntityState.Modified);

        DateTime now = DateTime.UtcNow;

        foreach (var entry in entries)
        {
            if (entry.State == EntityState.Added)
            {
                entry.Property(e => e.CreatedAt).CurrentValue = now;
            }

            entry.Property(e => e.UpdatedAt).CurrentValue = now;
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure/data/MasterCrmDbContext.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;



namespace CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.data
{
    public class MasterCrmDbContext : IdentityDbContext
    {
        public DbSet<Company> Companies => Set<Company>();
        public DbSet<CompanyDatabase> CompanyDatabases => Set<CompanyDatabase>();
        public DbSet<Device> Devices => Set<Device>();

        public MasterCrmDbContext(DbContextOptions<MasterCrmDbContext> options) : base(options)
        { }

        protected override void OnConfiguring(DbContextOptionsBuilder optionsBuilder)
        {
            base.OnConfiguring(optionsBuilder);

            optionsBuilder.AddInterceptors(new AuditInterceptor());
        }

        protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder builder)
        {
            base.OnModelCreating(builder);

            builder.Entity<Company>(entity =>

            {

                entity.HasKey(x => x.CompanyId);



                entity.Property(x => x.CompanyCode)

                  .HasMaxLength(50)

                  .IsRequired();



                entity.Property(x => x.CompanyName)

                  .HasMaxLength(200)

                  .IsRequired();



                entity.HasIndex(x => x.CompanyCode)

                  .IsUnique();

            });



            builder.Entity<CompanyDatabase>(entity =>

            {

                entity.HasKey(x => x.CompanyDatabaseId);



                entity.Property(x => x.ServerName)

                  .HasMaxLength(200)

                  .IsRequired();



                entity.Property(x => x.DatabaseName)

                  .HasMaxLength(200)

                  .IsRequired();



                entity.HasOne(x => x.Company)

                  .WithMany()

                  .HasForeignKey(x => x.CompanyId)

                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            });

            builder.Entity<Device>(entity =>
            {
                entity.HasKey(x => x.DeviceId);

                entity.Property(x => x.DeviceCode)
                .HasMaxLength(50).IsRequired();

                entity.Property(x => x.DeviceName)
                .HasMaxLength(200).IsRequired();

                entity.HasOne(x => x.Company)
                .WithMany(x => x.Devices)
                .HasForeignKey(x => x.CompanyId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

                entity.HasIndex(x => new { x.CompanyId, x.DeviceCode })
                .IsUnique();

            });
        }

    }

}



===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure/data/MasterCrmDbContextFactory.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.data
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Design-time only: used by `dotnet ef migrations add/update` for MasterCrmDbContext.
    /// Points at the local master DB. Never runs at runtime.
    /// </summary>
    public class MasterCrmDbContextFactory : IDesignTimeDbContextFactory<MasterCrmDbContext>
    {
        public MasterCrmDbContext CreateDbContext(string[] args)
        {
            const string connectionString =
                "Server=(localdb)\\MSSQLLocalDB;" +
                "Database=CRM_Morcaffein_MasterCrm;" +
                "Trusted_Connection=True;" +
                "TrustServerCertificate=True;" +
                "MultipleActiveResultSets=True;";

            var options = new DbContextOptionsBuilder<MasterCrmDbContext>()
                .UseSqlServer(connectionString)
                .Options;

            return new MasterCrmDbContext(options);
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure/data/TenantCrmDbContext.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Identity.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.data;

public class TenantCrmDbContext : IdentityDbContext
{
    // ===== TENANT CRM DOMAIN TABLES =====
    public DbSet<Product> Products => Set<Product>();
    public DbSet<ProductSize> ProductSizes => Set<ProductSize>();
    public DbSet<Customer> Customers => Set<Customer>();
    public DbSet<Employee> Employees => Set<Employee>();
    public DbSet<Credential> Credentials => Set<Credential>();
    public DbSet<Order> Orders => Set<Order>();
    public DbSet<OrderItem> OrderItems => Set<OrderItem>();
    public DbSet<Campaign> Campaigns => Set<Campaign>();
    public DbSet<CampaignRedeem> CampaignRedeems => Set<CampaignRedeem>();
    public DbSet<Feedback> Feedbacks => Set<Feedback>();
    public DbSet<PointLedger> PointLedgers => Set<PointLedger>();
    public DbSet<ProductModifier> ProductModifiers => Set<ProductModifier>();
    public DbSet<OrderItemModifier> OrderItemModifiers => Set<OrderItemModifier>();
    public DbSet<PointAdjustmentRequest> PointAdjustmentRequests => Set<PointAdjustmentRequest>();

    public TenantCrmDbContext(DbContextOptions<TenantCrmDbContext> options) : base(options)
    { }

    protected override void OnConfiguring(DbContextOptionsBuilder optionsBuilder)
    {
        base.OnConfiguring(optionsBuilder);
        optionsBuilder.AddInterceptors(new AuditInterceptor());
    }

    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder builder)
    {
        base.OnModelCreating(builder);

        // 1. PRODUCT
        builder.Entity<Product>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(p => p.ProductId);

            entity.Property(p => p.ProductCode)
                  .HasMaxLength(50)
                  .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(p => p.ProductName)
                  .HasMaxLength(200)
                  .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(p => p.ImageUrl).HasMaxLength(500);
            entity.Property(p => p.LocalImagePath).HasMaxLength(500);

            entity.Property(p => p.BasePrice)
                  .HasPrecision(18, 2);

            entity.HasIndex(p => p.ProductCode)
                  .IsUnique();
        });

        builder.Entity<ProductModifier>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(pm => pm.ProductModifierId);
            entity.Property(pm => pm.Name).HasMaxLength(100).IsRequired();
            entity.Property(pm => pm.Category).HasMaxLength(50);
            entity.Property(pm => pm.Price).HasPrecision(18, 2);
        });

        builder.Entity<OrderItemModifier>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(oim => oim.OrderItemModifierId);
            entity.Property(oim => oim.ProductModifierNameSnapshot).HasMaxLength(100);
            entity.Property(oim => oim.UnitPrice).HasPrecision(18, 2);

            entity.HasOne(oim => oim.OrderItem)
                  .WithMany(oi => oi.OrderItemModifiers)
                  .HasForeignKey(oim => oim.OrderItemId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            entity.HasOne(oim => oim.ProductModifier)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(oim => oim.ProductModifierId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // 2. PRODUCT SIZE
        builder.Entity<ProductSize>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(ps => ps.ProductSizeId);

            entity.Property(ps => ps.SizeName)
                  .HasMaxLength(50)
                  .IsRequired();

            entity.Property(ps => ps.Price)
                  .HasPrecision(18, 2);

            entity.HasOne(ps => ps.Product)
                  .WithMany(p => p.ProductSizes)
                  .HasForeignKey(ps => ps.ProductId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });

        // 3. ORDER
        builder.Entity<Order>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(o => o.OrderId);
            entity.Property(o => o.SubTotal).HasPrecision(18, 2);
            entity.Property(o => o.TotalDiscount).HasPrecision(18, 2);
            entity.Property(o => o.TotalAmount).HasPrecision(18, 2);
            entity.Property(o => o.PointsEarned).IsRequired();

            entity.HasOne(o => o.Customer)
                  .WithMany(c => c.Orders)
                  .HasForeignKey(o => o.CustomerId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasOne(o => o.Employee)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(o => o.CreatedBy)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // 4. ORDER ITEM
        builder.Entity<OrderItem>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(oi => oi.OrderItemId);

            // Stored Properties
            entity.Property(oi => oi.BaseSizePrice).HasPrecision(18, 2);
            entity.Property(oi => oi.SizeNameSnapshot).HasMaxLength(50);

            // Dynamic Computed Properties (Not stored directly in DB table)
            entity.Ignore(oi => oi.UnitPrice);
            entity.Ignore(oi => oi.SubTotal);

            // Relationships
            entity.HasOne(oi => oi.Order)
                  .WithMany(o => o.OrderItems)
                  .HasForeignKey(oi => oi.OrderId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            entity.HasOne(oi => oi.Product)
                  .WithMany(p => p.OrderItems)
                  .HasForeignKey(oi => oi.ProductId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasOne(oi => oi.ProductSize)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(oi => oi.ProductSizeId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // 5. CAMPAIGN

        builder.Entity<Campaign>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(c => c.CampaignId);
            entity.Property(c => c.PromoName).HasMaxLength(200).IsRequired();
            entity.Property(c => c.PromoCategory).HasMaxLength(100).IsRequired();
            entity.Property(c => c.Status).HasMaxLength(50).IsRequired();
            entity.Property(c => c.DecisionNotes).HasMaxLength(500);
            entity.Property(c => c.DiscountValue).HasPrecision(18, 2);

            entity.HasOne(c => c.Creator)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(c => c.MadeBy)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasOne(c => c.Decider)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(c => c.DecidedBy)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // 6. CAMPAIGN REDEEM
        builder.Entity<CampaignRedeem>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(cr => cr.RedeemId);
            entity.Property(cr => cr.DiscountUsed).HasPrecision(18, 2);

            entity.HasOne(cr => cr.Campaign)
                  .WithMany(c => c.CampaignRedeems)
                  .HasForeignKey(cr => cr.CampaignId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasOne(cr => cr.Order)
                  .WithMany(o => o.CampaignRedeems)
                  .HasForeignKey(cr => cr.OrderId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);
        });

        // 7. FEEDBACK
        builder.Entity<Feedback>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(f => f.TicketId);
            entity.Property(f => f.Category).HasMaxLength(100);
            entity.Property(f => f.Status).HasMaxLength(50);
            entity.Property(f => f.DecisionNotes).HasMaxLength(500);

            entity.HasOne(f => f.Customer)
                  .WithMany(c => c.Feedbacks)
                  .HasForeignKey(f => f.CustomerId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasOne(f => f.LoggedByEmployee)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(f => f.LoggedBy)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasOne(f => f.DecidedByEmployee)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(f => f.DecidedBy)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });

        // 8. POINT LEDGER
        builder.Entity<PointLedger>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(pl => pl.PointLedgerId);
            entity.Property(pl => pl.Type).HasMaxLength(50).IsRequired();
            entity.Property(pl => pl.Reason).HasMaxLength(250);

            entity.HasOne(pl => pl.Customer)
                  .WithMany(c => c.PointLedgers)
                  .HasForeignKey(pl => pl.CustomerId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            entity.HasOne(pl => pl.Order)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(pl => pl.OrderId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);

            entity.HasOne(pl => pl.PerformedByEmployee)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(pl => pl.PerformedBy)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull);
        });

        // 9. POINT ADJUSTMENT REQUEST
        builder.Entity<PointAdjustmentRequest>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(r => r.PointAdjustmentRequestId);
            entity.Property(r => r.Reason).HasMaxLength(250).IsRequired();
            entity.Property(r => r.Status).HasMaxLength(50).IsRequired();
            entity.Property(r => r.DecisionNotes).HasMaxLength(250);

            entity.HasOne(r => r.Customer)
                  .WithMany(c => c.PointAdjustmentRequests)
                  .HasForeignKey(r => r.CustomerId)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade);

            entity.HasOne(r => r.Requester)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(r => r.RequestedBy)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);

            entity.HasOne(r => r.Approver)
                  .WithMany()
                  .HasForeignKey(r => r.ApprovedBy)
                  .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict);
        });
    }
}

﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Design;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.data
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Design-time only: used by `dotnet ef migrations add/update`. The connection string
    /// is inlined because this file never runs at runtime — it only exists so EF's
    /// migration tooling can build a TenantCrmDbContext without the full DI stack.
    ///
    /// If the tenant DB ever moves, update the string here AND in the API's
    /// appsettings.json. This is the only place the string is duplicated.
    /// </summary>
    public class TenantCrmDbContextFactory : IDesignTimeDbContextFactory<TenantCrmDbContext>
    {
        public TenantCrmDbContext CreateDbContext(string[] args)
        {
            //const string connectionString =
            //    "Server=db67068.public.databaseasp.net;" +
            //    "Database=db67068;" +
            //    "User Id=db67068;" +
            //    "Password=iG#9H8g?2d@A;" +
            //    "Encrypt=True;" +
            //    "TrustServerCertificate=True;" +
            //    "MultipleActiveResultSets=True;";

            const string connectionString =
                "Server=(localdb)\\MSSQLLocalDB;" +
                "Database=CRM_Morcaffein_TenantCrm;" +
                "Trusted_Connection=True;" +
                "TrustServerCertificate=True;" +
                "MultipleActiveResultSets=True;";

            var options = new DbContextOptionsBuilder<TenantCrmDbContext>()
                .UseSqlServer(connectionString)
                .Options;

            return new TenantCrmDbContext(options);
        }
    }
}
