===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure/services/TenantDatabaseInfo.cs =====
﻿using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Text;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services
{
    public class TenantDatabaseInfo
    {
        public string ServerName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string DatabaseName { get; set; } = string.Empty;
        public string CredentialKey { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }
}


===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure/services/TenantDatabaseResolver.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services
{
    public class TenantDatabaseResolver : ITenantDatabaseResolver
    {
        private readonly MasterCrmDbContext _masterDb;

        // Process-lifetime cache. CompanyDatabase rows don't change at runtime, so once we
        // resolve a tenant's DB info, we never need to hit the master DB again for that ID.
        // Avoids a remote round-trip on every single API call.
        private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<int, TenantDatabaseInfo> _cache = new();

        public TenantDatabaseResolver(MasterCrmDbContext masterDb)
        {
            _masterDb = masterDb;
        }

        public async Task<TenantDatabaseInfo> GetDatabaseInfoAsync(int companyId)
        {
            if (_cache.TryGetValue(companyId, out var cached))
                return cached;

            var tenantDatabase = await _masterDb.CompanyDatabases
                .AsNoTracking()
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(x =>
                    x.CompanyId == companyId &&
                    x.IsActive);

            if (tenantDatabase == null)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(
                    $"No active tenant database found for CompanyId {companyId}.");
            }

            var info = new TenantDatabaseInfo
            {
                ServerName = tenantDatabase.ServerName,
                DatabaseName = tenantDatabase.DatabaseName,
                CredentialKey = tenantDatabase.CredentialKey
            };

            _cache[companyId] = info;
            return info;
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure/services/TenantDbContextFactory.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.data;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Configuration;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.infrastructure.services
{
    public class TenantDbContextFactory : ITenantDbContextFactory
    {
        private readonly ITenantDatabaseResolver _resolver;
        private readonly IConfiguration _configuration;

        // Cache built DbContextOptions per company. DbContextOptions is designed to be
        // built ONCE and reused — building it per call forces EF to re-evaluate the
        // provider, break the connection pool, and open a fresh SQL connection every
        // request. This one cache is the difference between ~500ms and ~50ms per call.
        private static readonly ConcurrentDictionary<int, DbContextOptions<TenantCrmDbContext>> _optionsCache = new();

        public TenantDbContextFactory(
            ITenantDatabaseResolver resolver,
            IConfiguration configuration)
        {
            _resolver = resolver;
            _configuration = configuration;
        }

        public async Task<TenantCrmDbContext> CreateAsync(int companyId)
        {
            var options = await GetOrBuildOptionsAsync(companyId);
            return new TenantCrmDbContext(options);
        }

        private async Task<DbContextOptions<TenantCrmDbContext>> GetOrBuildOptionsAsync(int companyId)
        {
            if (_optionsCache.TryGetValue(companyId, out var cached))
                return cached;

            var databaseInfo = await _resolver.GetDatabaseInfoAsync(companyId);

            // LocalDB uses Windows authentication (Trusted_Connection) — there's no
            // user/password to look up. Cloud SQL Server needs credentials from config.
            var isLocalDb = databaseInfo.ServerName.Contains("(localdb)", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            string connectionString;

            if (isLocalDb)
            {
                connectionString =
                    $"Server={databaseInfo.ServerName};" +
                    $"Database={databaseInfo.DatabaseName};" +
                    $"Trusted_Connection=True;" +
                    $"TrustServerCertificate=True;" +
                    $"MultipleActiveResultSets=True;";
            }
            else
            {
                var userId = _configuration[$"TenantCredentials:{databaseInfo.CredentialKey}:UserId"];
                var password = _configuration[$"TenantCredentials:{databaseInfo.CredentialKey}:Password"];

                if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userId) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(password))
                {
                    throw new InvalidOperationException(
                        $"Credentials not found for key '{databaseInfo.CredentialKey}'.");
                }

                connectionString =
                    $"Server={databaseInfo.ServerName};" +
                    $"Database={databaseInfo.DatabaseName};" +
                    $"User Id={userId};" +
                    $"Password={password};" +
                    $"Encrypt=True;" +
                    $"TrustServerCertificate=True;" +
                    $"MultipleActiveResultSets=True;" +
                    $"Min Pool Size=2;" +
                    $"Max Pool Size=10;" +
                    $"Connection Lifetime=300;";
            }

            var options = new DbContextOptionsBuilder<TenantCrmDbContext>()
                .UseSqlServer(connectionString)
                .Options;

            _optionsCache[companyId] = options;
            return options;
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Controllers/CampaignController.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services;
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// UI controller for the Campaign tab (WinForms). Pure coordination: the View calls
    /// these methods, they forward to the API. All business rules live on the API side.
    ///
    /// Also responsible for invalidating the POS's CampaignCache whenever a Manager
    /// creates, edits, activates, or deactivates a campaign — otherwise the POS would
    /// keep serving stale active-campaign lists until the app restarts.
    /// </summary>
    public class CampaignController
    {
        private readonly CampaignApi _api;

        public CampaignController()
        {
            _api = new CampaignApi();
        }

        // ---- List ----

        /// <summary>Every campaign regardless of status — the Manager's list.</summary>
        public Task<List<CampaignResponseDto>> GetAllAsync()
            => _api.GetForManagementAsync();

        /// <summary>Currently-active campaigns only — used by the POS.</summary>
        public Task<List<CampaignResponseDto>> GetActiveAsync()
            => _api.GetActiveCampaignsAsync();

        public Task<CampaignResponseDto?> GetByIdAsync(int campaignId)
            => _api.GetByIdAsync(campaignId);

        // ---- Mutations ----
        //
        // Each mutation invalidates CampaignCache on success, so the POS picks up the
        // change without needing an app restart. Invalidating on failure would be wrong —
        // a rejected edit shouldn't reset the cache.

        public async Task<CampaignResponseDto> CreateAsync(
            string promoName, string promoCategory, string discountType,
            decimal discountValue, string targetTier,
            decimal? minPurchaseAmount, decimal? maxDiscountAmount,
            int maxRedemptionsPerCustomer, DateTime startDate, DateTime endDate,
            int madeByEmployeeId)
        {
            var request = new CreateCampaignRequest
            {
                PromoName = promoName,
                PromoCategory = promoCategory,
                DiscountType = discountType,
                DiscountValue = discountValue,
                TargetTier = targetTier,
                MinPurchaseAmount = minPurchaseAmount,
                MaxDiscountAmount = maxDiscountAmount,
                MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer = maxRedemptionsPerCustomer,
                StartDate = startDate,
                EndDate = endDate,
                MadeBy = madeByEmployeeId,
            };

            var result = await _api.CreateAsync(request);
            CampaignCache.Reset();
            return result;
        }

        public async Task<CampaignResponseDto> UpdateAsync(
            int campaignId,
            string promoName, string promoCategory, string discountType,
            decimal discountValue, string targetTier,
            decimal? minPurchaseAmount, decimal? maxDiscountAmount,
            int maxRedemptionsPerCustomer, DateTime startDate, DateTime endDate,
            int madeByEmployeeId)
        {
            var request = new UpdateCampaignRequest
            {
                PromoName = promoName,
                PromoCategory = promoCategory,
                DiscountType = discountType,
                DiscountValue = discountValue,
                TargetTier = targetTier,
                MinPurchaseAmount = minPurchaseAmount,
                MaxDiscountAmount = maxDiscountAmount,
                MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer = maxRedemptionsPerCustomer,
                StartDate = startDate,
                EndDate = endDate,
                MadeBy = madeByEmployeeId,
            };

            var result = await _api.UpdateAsync(campaignId, request);
            CampaignCache.Reset();
            return result;
        }

        public async Task<CampaignResponseDto> ActivateAsync(int campaignId, int performedByEmployeeId)
        {
            var result = await _api.ActivateAsync(campaignId, performedByEmployeeId);
            CampaignCache.Reset();
            return result;
        }

        public async Task<CampaignResponseDto> DeactivateAsync(int campaignId, int performedByEmployeeId)
        {
            var result = await _api.DeactivateAsync(campaignId, performedByEmployeeId);
            CampaignCache.Reset();
            return result;
        }

        // ---- Admin approval flow ----

        /// <summary>Pending campaigns only — the Admin's Approvals queue.</summary>
        public Task<List<CampaignResponseDto>> GetPendingAsync()
            => _api.GetPendingForApprovalAsync();

        public async Task<CampaignResponseDto> ApproveAsync(int campaignId, int decidedByEmployeeId, string? notes)
        {
            var result = await _api.ApproveAsync(campaignId, decidedByEmployeeId, notes);
            // Approved campaigns become Active, so the POS's cached active list is now stale.
            CampaignCache.Reset();
            return result;
        }

        public async Task<CampaignResponseDto> RejectAsync(int campaignId, int decidedByEmployeeId, string notes)
        {
            var result = await _api.RejectAsync(campaignId, decidedByEmployeeId, notes);
            // Rejected campaigns are not Active, so the POS cache is unaffected in practice —
            // but reset anyway for consistency, in case a previously-active campaign was
            // somehow rejected (it can't be, but the reset is cheap and safe).
            CampaignCache.Reset();
            return result;
        }
    }
}

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers

    /// <summary>

    public class CustomerController
    {
        private readonly CustomerApi _customers;
        private readonly PointAdjustmentApi _adjustments;

        public CustomerController()
        {
            _customers = new CustomerApi();
            _adjustments = new PointAdjustmentApi();
        }

        // ---- Customers ----

        public Task<PagedResponse<CustomerResponseDto>> SearchAsync(
            string searchText, int page = 1, int pageSize = 25, string? activeFilter = null)

                ContactNumber = phone,

                Birthday = birthday,

                        LastName = lastName,
                        ContactNumber = phone,
                        Email = email,
                        Birthday = birthday,
                    };

                }

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Controllers/TransactionController.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services;
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Controller for the Transactions/POS tab (MVC). One instance represents a single
    /// in-progress sale for one customer. Owns every business rule for the cart/promo
    /// flow — cart-item pricing, discount calculation, promo eligibility, and the
    /// stacked PWD/Senior discount (standard PH method).
    ///
    /// Cart is CLIENT-SIDE: the in-progress Order is built and mutated in memory here.
    /// Only CompleteSaleAsync hits the network — it POSTs the whole cart to the API,
    /// which recomputes all totals server-side and persists everything.
    /// </summary>
    public class TransactionController
    {
        private readonly OrderApi _orderApi;
        private readonly IReadOnlyList<Product> _allProducts;
        private readonly IReadOnlyList<string> _categories;
        private readonly IReadOnlyList<ProductModifier> _modifiers;
        private readonly IReadOnlyList<Campaign> _activeCampaigns;

        public Order Order { get; private set; }
        public Customer Customer { get; private set; }

        public IReadOnlyList<string> Categories => _categories;

        public decimal SubTotal => Order.SubTotal;
        public decimal TotalDiscount => Order.TotalDiscount;
        public decimal PwdSeniorDiscountAmount => Order.PwdSeniorDiscountAmount;
        public decimal TotalAmount => Order.TotalAmount;
        public bool HasItems => Order.OrderItems.Count > 0;
        public bool PwdSeniorApplied => Order.HasPwdSeniorDiscount;
        public IReadOnlyList<OrderItem> CartItems => Order.OrderItems.ToList();

        public int? CompletedOrderId { get; private set; }
        public int CompletedPointsEarned { get; private set; }
        public string CompletedCustomerName { get; set; } = string.Empty;

        public TransactionController(
            IReadOnlyList<Product> allProducts,
            IReadOnlyList<string> categories,
            IReadOnlyList<ProductModifier> modifiers,
            IReadOnlyList<Campaign> activeCampaigns,
            Customer customer,
            int createdByEmployeeId)
        {
            _orderApi = new OrderApi();
            _allProducts = allProducts;
            _categories = categories;
            _modifiers = modifiers;
            _activeCampaigns = activeCampaigns;
            Customer = customer;

            Order = new Order
            {
                CustomerId = customer.CustomerId,
                CreatedBy = createdByEmployeeId,
                OrderDate = DateTime.UtcNow,
                Status = "Pending",
            };
        }

        public void SwitchCustomer(Customer newCustomer)
        {
            Order.CustomerId = newCustomer.CustomerId;
            Customer = newCustomer;
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<Product> GetProducts(string category, string searchText)
        {
            IEnumerable<Product> products = string.IsNullOrEmpty(category)
                ? _allProducts
                : _allProducts.Where(p => p.ProductCategory.Equals(category, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(searchText))
                products = products.Where(p => p.ProductName.Contains(searchText, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));

            return products.ToList();
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<ProductModifier> GetModifiers() => _modifiers;

        public void AddItemToCart(Product product, ProductSize size, int quantity, IEnumerable<ProductModifier> modifiers)
        {
            EnsurePending();
            if (quantity <= 0)
                throw new ArgumentException("Quantity must be greater than zero.");

            var existing = Order.OrderItems.FirstOrDefault(i => i.ProductSizeId == size.ProductSizeId);
            if (existing != null)
            {
                existing.Quantity += quantity;
                RecalculateItemPricing(existing);
            }
            else
            {
                var item = new OrderItem
                {
                    ProductId = product.ProductId,
                    ProductSizeId = size.ProductSizeId,
                    SizeNameSnapshot = size.SizeName,
                    BaseSizePrice = size.Price,
                    Quantity = quantity,
                    Product = product,
                    ProductSize = size,
                };

                foreach (var modifier in modifiers)
                {
                    item.OrderItemModifiers.Add(new OrderItemModifier
                    {
                        ProductModifierId = modifier.ProductModifierId,
                        ProductModifierNameSnapshot = modifier.Name,
                        UnitPrice = modifier.Price,
                        ProductModifier = modifier,
                    });
                }

                RecalculateItemPricing(item);
                Order.OrderItems.Add(item);
            }

            RecalculateOrderTotals();
        }

        public void UpdateCartItem(OrderItem item, ProductSize newSize, int newQuantity, IEnumerable<ProductModifier> newModifiers)
        {
            EnsurePending();
            if (newQuantity <= 0)
                throw new ArgumentException("Quantity must be greater than zero.");

            if (item.ProductSizeId == newSize.ProductSizeId)
            {
                item.Quantity = newQuantity;
                item.OrderItemModifiers.Clear();
                foreach (var modifier in newModifiers)
                {
                    item.OrderItemModifiers.Add(new OrderItemModifier
                    {
                        ProductModifierId = modifier.ProductModifierId,
                        ProductModifierNameSnapshot = modifier.Name,
                        UnitPrice = modifier.Price,
                        ProductModifier = modifier,
                    });
                }
                RecalculateItemPricing(item);
                RecalculateOrderTotals();
            }
            else
            {
                Order.OrderItems.Remove(item);
                AddItemToCart(item.Product!, newSize, newQuantity, newModifiers);
            }
        }

        public void RemoveItem(OrderItem item)
        {
            Order.OrderItems.Remove(item);
            RecalculateOrderTotals();
        }

        public IReadOnlyList<Campaign> GetEligiblePromos() =>
            _activeCampaigns.Where(c => CanRedeem(c, Customer, Order)).ToList();

        public void ApplyPromo(Campaign campaign)
        {
            EnsurePending();

            // Only one promo at a time — clear any existing before adding the new one.
            Order.CampaignRedeems.Clear();

            var discount = CalculatePromoDiscount(campaign, Order.SubTotal);
            Order.CampaignRedeems.Add(new CampaignRedeem
            {
                CampaignId = campaign.CampaignId,
                DiscountUsed = discount,
                RedeemDate = DateTime.UtcNow,
                Campaign = campaign,
            });
            RecalculateOrderTotals();
        }

        public void RemovePromo(Campaign campaign)
        {
            var redeem = Order.CampaignRedeems.FirstOrDefault(r => r.CampaignId == campaign.CampaignId);
            if (redeem is null) return;

            Order.CampaignRedeems.Remove(redeem);
            RecalculateOrderTotals();
        }

        public void ClearPromos()
        {
            Order.CampaignRedeems.Clear();
            RecalculateOrderTotals();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Toggles the PWD/Senior discount. Stacks on top of any applied promo using the
        /// standard PH method: 20% off the VAT-exempt amount of the post-promo net.
        /// Recalculates totals immediately so the Payables panel updates on the spot.
        /// </summary>
        public void SetPwdSeniorDiscount(bool applied)
        {
            EnsurePending();
            Order.HasPwdSeniorDiscount = applied;
            RecalculateOrderTotals();
        }

        public async Task CompleteSaleAsync()
        {
            EnsurePending();
            if (Order.OrderItems.Count == 0)
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Order cannot be empty.");

            var request = new CompleteOrderRequest
            {
                CustomerId = Order.CustomerId,
                CreatedBy = Order.CreatedBy,
                CampaignId = Order.CampaignRedeems.FirstOrDefault()?.CampaignId,
                HasPwdSeniorDiscount = Order.HasPwdSeniorDiscount,
                Lines = Order.OrderItems.Select(i => new CompleteOrderLineDto
                {
                    ProductId = i.ProductId,
                    ProductSizeId = i.ProductSizeId,
                    Quantity = i.Quantity,
                    ModifierIds = i.OrderItemModifiers.Select(m => m.ProductModifierId).ToList(),
                }).ToList(),
            };

            var response = await _orderApi.CompleteOrderAsync(request);

            CompletedOrderId = response.OrderId;
            CompletedPointsEarned = response.PointsEarned;
            CompletedCustomerName = response.CustomerName;

            Order.Status = "Completed";
            Order.OrderId = response.OrderId;
            Order.SubTotal = response.SubTotal;
            Order.TotalDiscount = response.TotalDiscount;
            Order.TotalAmount = response.TotalAmount;
            Order.PointsEarned = response.PointsEarned;
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // BUSINESS RULES
        // =====================================================================

        private void EnsurePending()
        {
            if (!Order.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                throw new InvalidOperationException($"Order cannot be modified in '{Order.Status}' state.");
        }

        private static void RecalculateItemPricing(OrderItem item)
        {
            item.UnitPrice = item.BaseSizePrice + item.OrderItemModifiers.Sum(m => m.UnitPrice);
            item.SubTotal = item.Quantity * item.UnitPrice;
        }

        private void RecalculateOrderTotals()
        {
            Order.SubTotal = Order.OrderItems.Sum(i => i.SubTotal);

            // --- Promo discount (single promo, applied to SubTotal) ---
            decimal promoDiscount = 0m;
            foreach (var redeem in Order.CampaignRedeems)
            {
                var campaign = redeem.Campaign ?? _activeCampaigns.FirstOrDefault(c => c.CampaignId == redeem.CampaignId);
                var discount = campaign != null ? CalculatePromoDiscount(campaign, Order.SubTotal) : redeem.DiscountUsed;
                redeem.DiscountUsed = discount;
                promoDiscount += discount;
            }
            Order.TotalDiscount = promoDiscount;

            // --- PWD/Senior (stacked, standard PH method) ---
            var afterPromo = Math.Max(0m, Order.SubTotal - Order.TotalDiscount);

            if (Order.HasPwdSeniorDiscount)
            {
                var netOfVat = afterPromo / 1.12m;
                var pwdDiscount = decimal.Round(netOfVat * 0.20m, 2);

                Order.PwdSeniorDiscountAmount = pwdDiscount;
                Order.TotalAmount = decimal.Round(netOfVat - pwdDiscount, 2);
            }
            else
            {
                Order.PwdSeniorDiscountAmount = 0m;
                Order.TotalAmount = afterPromo;
            }
        }

        private static bool IsCurrentlyActive(Campaign campaign)
        {
            var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
            return campaign.IsActive && now >= campaign.StartDate && now <= campaign.EndDate;
        }

        private static bool CanRedeem(Campaign campaign, Customer customer, Order order)
        {
            if (!IsCurrentlyActive(campaign)) return false;

            if (campaign.MinPurchaseAmount.HasValue && order.SubTotal < campaign.MinPurchaseAmount.Value)
                return false;

            if (!campaign.TargetTier.Equals(PromoCategories.AllTiers, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
    !PointsToTier(customer.PointsBalance).Equals(campaign.TargetTier, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return false;

            if (campaign.PromoCategory.Equals(PromoCategories.Birthday, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && !IsBirthdayToday(customer))
                return false;

            if (campaign.PromoCategory.Equals(PromoCategories.Inactive, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && !IsInactive(customer))
                return false;

            var previousRedemptions = campaign.CampaignRedeems.Count(r => r.Order != null && r.Order.CustomerId == customer.CustomerId);
            return previousRedemptions < campaign.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer;
        }

        private static string PointsToTier(int pointsBalance) => pointsBalance switch
        {
            >= 500 => "Gold",
            >= 200 => "Silver",
            _ => "Regular",
        };

        private static bool IsBirthdayToday(Customer customer) =>
            customer.Birthday?.Month == DateTime.UtcNow.Month && customer.Birthday?.Day == DateTime.UtcNow.Day;

        private static bool IsInactive(Customer customer) => (DateTime.UtcNow - customer.LastVisitDate).Days > ActivityStatus.InactiveThresholdDays;

        private static decimal CalculatePromoDiscount(Campaign campaign, decimal orderTotal)
        {
            if (orderTotal <= 0) return 0m;
            if (campaign.MinPurchaseAmount.HasValue && orderTotal < campaign.MinPurchaseAmount.Value) return 0m;

            decimal discount;
            if (campaign.DiscountType.Equals("Percentage", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            {
                discount = orderTotal * (campaign.DiscountValue / 100m);
                if (campaign.MaxDiscountAmount.HasValue && discount > campaign.MaxDiscountAmount.Value)
                    discount = campaign.MaxDiscountAmount.Value;
            }
            else
            {
                discount = campaign.DiscountValue;
            }

            return discount > orderTotal ? orderTotal : discount;
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Program.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api;
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein
{
    internal static class Program
    {
        [STAThread]
        static void Main()
        {
            ApplicationConfiguration.Initialize();

            // Prewarm the API (fire-and-forget, ignore failures).
            _ = Task.Run(async () =>
            {
                try
                {
                    using var http = new HttpClient
                    {
                        BaseAddress = new Uri(ApiConfig.BaseUrl),
                        Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10),
                    };
                    await http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/customers?page=1&pageSize=1");
                }
                catch { }
            });

            // Loop: show login, then main, until the user closes the login window.
            while (true)
            {
                using var login = new FrmLogin();
                if (login.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.OK)
                    return;   // user closed login — exit

                var main = new FrmMain();
                Application.Run(main);

                // If the user closed FrmMain via the X button, exit the app.
                // If they clicked Logout, loop back to the login screen.
                if (!main.ClosedViaLogout)
                    return;
            }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Services/ActivityStatus.cs =====
﻿namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Single source of truth for "is this customer inactive?" and for rendering
    /// human-readable relative times next to LastVisitDate.
    ///
    /// The 14-day threshold is used by TransactionController (promo eligibility: the
    /// "We Miss You" campaign targets customers inactive for more than 14 days) and by
    /// the Customers view (the Status line in the drawer). Both reference
    /// InactiveThresholdDays so the number can never drift between the two.
    /// </summary>
    public static class ActivityStatus
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// A customer is inactive once more than this many whole days have passed since
        /// their last visit.
        /// </summary>
        public const int InactiveThresholdDays = 14;

        /// <summary>
        /// True when lastVisitUtc is strictly more than InactiveThresholdDays days ago.
        /// </summary>
        public static bool IsInactive(DateTime lastVisitUtc) =>
            (DateTime.UtcNow - lastVisitUtc).TotalDays > InactiveThresholdDays;

        /// <summary>
        /// "Active" or "Inactive" as plain text — no badge, no color.
        /// </summary>
        public static string ToDisplayString(DateTime lastVisitUtc) =>
            IsInactive(lastVisitUtc) ? "Inactive" : "Active";

        /// <summary>
        /// Human-readable relative time, e.g. "just now", "5 minutes ago", "3 hours ago",
        /// "2 days ago", "6 weeks ago", "4 months ago", "over a year ago".
        ///
        /// Never returns an empty string. A default(DateTime) (year 0001) is treated as
        /// "never" rather than a nonsense negative duration.
        /// </summary>
        public static string FormatRelative(DateTime utcTimestamp)
        {
            if (utcTimestamp == default)
                return "never";

            var delta = DateTime.UtcNow - utcTimestamp;

            if (delta.TotalSeconds < 60)
                return "just now";

            if (delta.TotalMinutes < 60)
            {
                var m = (int)delta.TotalMinutes;
                return m == 1 ? "1 minute ago" : $"{m} minutes ago";
            }

            if (delta.TotalHours < 24)
            {
                var h = (int)delta.TotalHours;
                return h == 1 ? "1 hour ago" : $"{h} hours ago";
            }

            if (delta.TotalDays < 7)
            {
                var d = (int)delta.TotalDays;
                return d == 1 ? "1 day ago" : $"{d} days ago";
            }

            if (delta.TotalDays < 30)
            {
                var w = (int)(delta.TotalDays / 7);
                return w == 1 ? "1 week ago" : $"{w} weeks ago";
            }

            if (delta.TotalDays < 365)
            {
                var mo = (int)(delta.TotalDays / 30);
                return mo == 1 ? "1 month ago" : $"{mo} months ago";
            }

            var y = (int)(delta.TotalDays / 365);
            return y == 1 ? "over a year ago" : $"{y} years ago";
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Services/Api/ApiClientFactory.cs =====
﻿using System.Net.Http.Headers;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Central place for building HttpClients used by the Api* classes. Reads
    /// ApiConfig.BaseUrl for the base address and attaches the current auth token
    /// (if any) as a bearer header.
    ///
    /// Each call creates a fresh HttpClient. That's not ideal (sockets should be
    /// pooled), but it's what the existing Api* classes already do — this just
    /// centralizes the setup so adding the token is one change instead of many.
    /// </summary>
    public static class ApiClientFactory
    {
        public static HttpClient Create()
        {
            var client = new HttpClient
            {
                BaseAddress = new Uri(ApiConfig.BaseUrl),
                Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5),
            };

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ApiConfig.AuthToken))
            {
                client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Authorization =
                    new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", ApiConfig.AuthToken);
            }

            return client;
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Services/Api/ApiConfig.cs =====
﻿namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api
{
    public static class ApiConfig
    {
        public const string BaseUrl = "http://localhost:5035";
        public const int CompanyId = 1;

        /// <summary>
        /// The JWT for the currently logged-in employee. Set by FrmLogin after a
        /// successful authentication. Every Api* class reads this and attaches it as
        /// an "Authorization: Bearer &lt;token&gt;" header on outgoing requests.
        /// Null until the user logs in.
        /// </summary>
        public static string? AuthToken { get; set; }

        /// <summary>Clears the token on logout.</summary>
        public static void ClearAuthToken() => AuthToken = null;
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Services/Api/AuthApi.cs =====
﻿using System.Net.Http.Json;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api
{
    public class LoginResult
    {
        public bool Success { get; init; }
        public LoginResponse? Data { get; init; }
        public string? ErrorMessage { get; init; }
    }

    public class AuthApi
    {
        private readonly HttpClient _http;

        public AuthApi()
        {
            _http = new HttpClient { BaseAddress = new Uri(ApiConfig.BaseUrl) };
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// POSTs credentials to the tenant's login endpoint. NEVER throws for normal
        /// failure cases (wrong password, account locked, API unreachable) — those
        /// come back as a LoginResult with Success=false and an ErrorMessage.
        /// </summary>
        public async Task<LoginResult> LoginAsync(string username, string password)
        {
            var request = new LoginRequest
            {
                Username = username,
                Password = password,
            };

            HttpResponseMessage response;
            try
            {
                response = await _http.PostAsJsonAsync(
                    $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/auth/login", request);
            }
            catch (HttpRequestException ex)
            {
                return new LoginResult
                {
                    Success = false,
                    ErrorMessage = $"Cannot reach the server. Is the API running?\n\n{ex.Message}",
                };
            }
            catch (TaskCanceledException)
            {
                return new LoginResult
                {
                    Success = false,
                    ErrorMessage = "The request timed out. Please try again.",
                };
            }

            if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
            {
                var body = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();
                var message = TryExtractMessage(body) ?? $"Login failed ({(int)response.StatusCode}).";
                return new LoginResult { Success = false, ErrorMessage = message };
            }

            LoginResponse? data;
            try
            {
                data = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<LoginResponse>();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return new LoginResult
                {
                    Success = false,
                    ErrorMessage = $"Unexpected response from server: {ex.Message}",
                };
            }

            if (data is null)
                return new LoginResult { Success = false, ErrorMessage = "Login returned an empty response." };

            return new LoginResult { Success = true, Data = data };
        }

        private static string? TryExtractMessage(string body)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(body)) return null;

            const string marker = "\"message\":\"";
            var idx = body.IndexOf(marker, StringComparison.Ordinal);
            if (idx < 0) return null;

            var start = idx + marker.Length;
            var end = body.IndexOf('"', start);
            if (end < 0) return null;

            return body.Substring(start, end - start);
        }
    }
}

﻿using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;


namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api

    public class CampaignApi
    {

        public async Task<List<CampaignResponseDto>> GetActiveCampaignsAsync()
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/campaigns");
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<List<CampaignResponseDto>>();
            return result ?? new List<CampaignResponseDto>();
        }

        public async Task<CampaignResponseDto?> GetByIdAsync(int campaignId)
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/campaigns/{campaignId}");
            if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
                return null;
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            return await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<CampaignResponseDto>();
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/campaigns/manage
        // Returns every campaign regardless of status — the Manager's list.
        public async Task<List<CampaignResponseDto>> GetForManagementAsync()
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/campaigns/manage");
            await EnsureSuccess(response);

                $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/campaigns/{campaignId}/reject", body);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<CampaignResponseDto>();
            return result ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("API returned an empty campaign.");
        }
        private static async Task EnsureSuccess(HttpResponseMessage response)
        {
            if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode) return;
            var body = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(body)
                    ? $"API returned {(int)response.StatusCode}."
                    : body);
        }
    }

﻿using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api
{
    public class CustomerApi
    {
        private readonly HttpClient _http;

        public CustomerApi()
        {
            _http = ApiClientFactory.Create();
        }

        public async Task<PagedResponse<CustomerResponseDto>> GetCustomersAsync(
            string? search = null, int page = 1, int pageSize = 25, string? activeFilter = null)
        {
            var url = $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/customers?page={page}&pageSize={pageSize}";
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(search))
                url += $"&search={Uri.EscapeDataString(search)}";
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(activeFilter))
                url += $"&activeFilter={Uri.EscapeDataString(activeFilter)}";

            var response = await _http.GetAsync(url);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);

            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<PagedResponse<CustomerResponseDto>>();
            return result ?? new PagedResponse<CustomerResponseDto>();
        }

        public async Task<CustomerResponseDto?> GetCustomerAsync(int id)
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/customers/{id}");
            if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
                return null;
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            return await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<CustomerResponseDto>();
        }

        public async Task<CustomerResponseDto?> FindCustomerAsync(string query)
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync(
                $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/customers/find?query={Uri.EscapeDataString(query)}");
            if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
                return null;
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            return await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<CustomerResponseDto>();
        }

        public async Task<CustomerResponseDto> CreateCustomerAsync(CreateCustomerDto request)
        {
            var response = await _http.PostAsJsonAsync(
                $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/customers", request);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<CustomerResponseDto>();
            return result ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("API returned an empty customer.");
        }

        public async Task<CustomerResponseDto> UpdateCustomerAsync(int id, UpdateCustomerDto request)
        {
            var response = await _http.PutAsJsonAsync(
                $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/customers/{id}", request);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<CustomerResponseDto>();
            return result ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("API returned an empty customer.");
        }

        public async Task DeleteCustomerAsync(int id)
        {
            var response = await _http.DeleteAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/customers/{id}");
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
        }
        public async Task ReactivateCustomerAsync(int id)
        {
            var response = await _http.PostAsync(
                $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/customers/{id}/reactivate", null);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
        }
        public async Task<List<PointLedgerResponseDto>> GetLedgerAsync(int customerId)
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/customers/{customerId}/ledger");
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<List<PointLedgerResponseDto>>();
            return result ?? new List<PointLedgerResponseDto>();
        }

        public async Task<List<OrderHistoryDto>> GetOrderHistoryAsync(int customerId)
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/customers/{customerId}/orders");
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<List<OrderHistoryDto>>();
            return result ?? new List<OrderHistoryDto>();
        }

        private static async Task EnsureSuccess(HttpResponseMessage response)
        {
            if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode) return;

            var body = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();
            var message = TryExtractMessage(body) ?? $"API returned {(int)response.StatusCode}.";
            throw new InvalidOperationException(message);
        }

        private static string? TryExtractMessage(string body)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(body)) return null;

            const string marker = "\"message\":\"";
            var idx = body.IndexOf(marker, StringComparison.Ordinal);
            if (idx < 0) return null;

            var start = idx + marker.Length;
            var end = body.IndexOf('"', start);
            if (end < 0) return null;

            return body.Substring(start, end - start);
        }
    }

            var end = body.IndexOf('"', start);
            if (end < 0) return null;
            return body.Substring(start, end - start);
        }

﻿using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api
{
    public class FeedbackApi
    {
        private readonly HttpClient _http;

        public FeedbackApi()
        {
            _http = ApiClientFactory.Create();
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/feedback?search=...&page=...&pageSize=...&status=...
        // status is appended at the END of the signature so existing positional callers
        // (search, page, pageSize) keep binding to the same parameters.
        public async Task<PagedResponse<FeedbackResponseDto>> GetTicketsAsync(
            string? search = null, int page = 1, int pageSize = 25, string? status = null)
        {
            var url = $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/feedback?page={page}&pageSize={pageSize}";
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(search))
                url += $"&search={Uri.EscapeDataString(search)}";
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(status))
                url += $"&status={Uri.EscapeDataString(status)}";

            var response = await _http.GetAsync(url);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);

            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<PagedResponse<FeedbackResponseDto>>();
            return result ?? new PagedResponse<FeedbackResponseDto>();
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/feedback/{id}
        public async Task<FeedbackResponseDto?> GetTicketAsync(int ticketId)
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/feedback/{ticketId}");
            if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound)

            return await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<FeedbackResponseDto>();

            throw new InvalidOperationException(message);
        }

        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(body)) return null;

            const string marker = "\"message\":\"";
            var idx = body.IndexOf(marker, StringComparison.Ordinal);
            if (idx < 0) return null;

            var start = idx + marker.Length;
            var end = body.IndexOf('"', start);
            if (end < 0) return null;

            return body.Substring(start, end - start);

﻿using System.Net.Http.Json;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api
{
    public class OrderApi
    {
        private readonly HttpClient _http;

        public OrderApi()
        {
            _http = ApiClientFactory.Create();
        }

        // POST api/tenant/{companyId}/orders
        // Sends the completed cart. The server recomputes every total from the raw
        // product/size/modifier IDs and returns the canonical OrderResponseDto.
        public async Task<OrderResponseDto> CompleteOrderAsync(CompleteOrderRequest request)
        {
            var response = await _http.PostAsJsonAsync(
                $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/orders", request);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);

            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<OrderResponseDto>();
            return result ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("API returned an empty order.");
        }

        private static async Task EnsureSuccess(HttpResponseMessage response)
        {
            if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode) return;

            var body = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();
            var message = TryExtractMessage(body) ?? $"API returned {(int)response.StatusCode}.";
            throw new InvalidOperationException(message);
        }

        private static string? TryExtractMessage(string body)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(body)) return null;

            const string marker = "\"message\":\"";
            var idx = body.IndexOf(marker, StringComparison.Ordinal);
            if (idx < 0) return null;

            var start = idx + marker.Length;
            var end = body.IndexOf('"', start);
            if (end < 0) return null;

            return body.Substring(start, end - start);
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Services/Api/PointAdjustmentApi.cs =====
﻿using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api
{
    public class PointAdjustmentApi
    {
        private readonly HttpClient _http;

        public PointAdjustmentApi()
        {
            _http = ApiClientFactory.Create();
        }

        public async Task<List<PointAdjustmentResponseDto>> GetPendingAsync(int? customerId = null)
        {
            var url = $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/point-adjustments/pending";
            if (customerId.HasValue)
                url += $"?customerId={customerId.Value}";

            var response = await _http.GetAsync(url);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<List<PointAdjustmentResponseDto>>();
            return result ?? new List<PointAdjustmentResponseDto>();
        }

        public async Task<PointAdjustmentResponseDto> RequestAsync(
            int customerId, int pointsChange, string reason, int requestedBy)
        {
            var request = new CreatePointAdjustmentDto
            {
                CustomerId = customerId,
                PointsChange = pointsChange,
                Reason = reason,
                RequestedBy = requestedBy,
            };
            var response = await _http.PostAsJsonAsync(
                $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/point-adjustments", request);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<PointAdjustmentResponseDto>();
            return result ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("API returned an empty response.");
        }

        public async Task<PointAdjustmentResponseDto> ApproveAsync(int requestId, int decidedBy, string? notes = null)
        {
            var body = new DecidePointAdjustmentDto { DecidedBy = decidedBy, Notes = notes };
            var response = await _http.PostAsJsonAsync(
                $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/point-adjustments/{requestId}/approve", body);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<PointAdjustmentResponseDto>();
            return result ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("API returned an empty response.");
        }

        public async Task<PointAdjustmentResponseDto> RejectAsync(int requestId, int decidedBy, string reason)
        {
            var body = new DecidePointAdjustmentDto { DecidedBy = decidedBy, Notes = reason };
            var response = await _http.PostAsJsonAsync(
                $"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/point-adjustments/{requestId}/reject", body);
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<PointAdjustmentResponseDto>();
            return result ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("API returned an empty response.");
        }

        private static async Task EnsureSuccess(HttpResponseMessage response)
        {
            if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode) return;

            var body = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();
            var message = TryExtractMessage(body) ?? $"API returned {(int)response.StatusCode}.";
            throw new InvalidOperationException(message);
        }

        private static string? TryExtractMessage(string body)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(body)) return null;

            const string marker = "\"message\":\"";
            var idx = body.IndexOf(marker, StringComparison.Ordinal);
            if (idx < 0) return null;

            var start = idx + marker.Length;
            var end = body.IndexOf('"', start);
            if (end < 0) return null;

            return body.Substring(start, end - start);
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Services/Api/ProductApi.cs =====
﻿using System.Net.Http.Json;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api
{
    public class ProductApi
    {
        private readonly HttpClient _http;

        public ProductApi()
        {
            _http = ApiClientFactory.Create();
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/products
        public async Task<List<ProductResponseDto>> GetProductsAsync()
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/products");
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<List<ProductResponseDto>>();
            return result ?? new List<ProductResponseDto>();
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/products/full-catalog
        public async Task<FullCatalogResponseDto> GetFullCatalogAsync()
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/products/full-catalog");
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<FullCatalogResponseDto>();
            return result ?? new FullCatalogResponseDto();
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/products/{id}
        public async Task<ProductResponseDto?> GetProductAsync(int productId)
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/products/{productId}");
            if (response.StatusCode == System.Net.HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
                return null;
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            return await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<ProductResponseDto>();
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/products/categories
        public async Task<List<string>> GetCategoriesAsync()
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/products/categories");
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<List<string>>();
            return result ?? new List<string>();
        }

        // GET api/tenant/{companyId}/products/modifiers
        public async Task<List<ProductModifierResponseDto>> GetModifiersAsync()
        {
            var response = await _http.GetAsync($"api/tenant/{ApiConfig.CompanyId}/products/modifiers");
            await EnsureSuccess(response);
            var result = await response.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<List<ProductModifierResponseDto>>();
            return result ?? new List<ProductModifierResponseDto>();
        }

        private static async Task EnsureSuccess(HttpResponseMessage response)
        {
            if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode) return;
            var body = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();
            throw new InvalidOperationException(
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(body)
                    ? $"API returned {(int)response.StatusCode}."
                    : body);
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Services/Api/ProductCatalogCache.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Session-scoped cache of the product catalog. Fetched once from the API on first
    /// use, then held in memory for the rest of the app session.
    ///
    /// Uses a single "full-catalog" endpoint instead of three separate calls, so the
    /// client only pays one HTTP round-trip (and one tenant DB lookup) on load.
    /// </summary>
    public static class ProductCatalogCache
    {
        private static readonly SemaphoreSlim _lock = new(1, 1);

        private static FullCatalogResponseDto? _catalog;

        public static async Task<FullCatalogResponseDto> GetCatalogAsync()
        {
            if (_catalog is not null) return _catalog;

            await _lock.WaitAsync();
            try
            {
                if (_catalog is null)
                {
                    var api = new ProductApi();
                    _catalog = await api.GetFullCatalogAsync();
                }
                return _catalog;
            }
            finally { _lock.Release(); }
        }

        public static void Reset() => _catalog = null;
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Services/CampaignCache.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Session-scoped cache for active campaigns. Fetched once from the API on
    /// first use, then reused for the rest of the app session. Campaigns rarely
    /// change; refetching on every customer search is wasteful and slow.
    ///
    /// Call Reset() to force a refetch (e.g. after a manager creates a new campaign).
    /// </summary>
    public static class CampaignCache
    {
        private static readonly SemaphoreSlim _lock = new(1, 1);
        private static List<CampaignResponseDto>? _campaigns;

        public static async Task<List<CampaignResponseDto>> GetActiveCampaignsAsync()
        {
            if (_campaigns is not null) return _campaigns;

            await _lock.WaitAsync();
            try
            {
                if (_campaigns is null)
                {
                    var api = new CampaignApi();
                    _campaigns = await api.GetActiveCampaignsAsync();
                }
                return _campaigns;
            }
            finally { _lock.Release(); }
        }

        public static void Reset() => _campaigns = null;
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Session/EmployeeSession.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.Session
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Nothing like this existed anywhere in the codebase yet. Order.CreatedBy,
    /// Feedback.LoggedBy, and Campaign.MadeBy/ApprovedBy all need "who is currently
    /// logged in" — this is the single source of truth for that, set once by FrmLogin
    /// on a successful Credential.VerifyPassword(...), and read by every downstream
    /// ViewModel via the static Current instance for the lifetime of the app process.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class EmployeeSession
    {
        public static EmployeeSession? Current { get; private set; }

        public Employee Employee { get; }

        private EmployeeSession(Employee employee)
        {
            Employee = employee;
        }

        public static EmployeeSession Start(Employee employee)
        {
            Current = new EmployeeSession(employee);
            return Current;
        }

        public static void End() => Current = null;

        public int EmployeeId => Employee.EmployeeId;
        public string DisplayName => Employee.FullName;
    }
}


===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Theme/BrandPalette.cs =====
using System.Drawing;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.Theme
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Every color in the UI should come from this class or be a plain white/system neutral.
    /// The brand roasted-coffee brown (#572400) — and tints/tones/shades of it — is the only
    /// hue used for branding, primary actions, and content-area accents (see HeaderBackground,
    /// ButtonPrimaryFill). Large chrome surfaces (the sidebar, in particular) are explicitly
    /// allowed to lean on plain white/light-grey neutrals instead of brand tints for a lighter
    /// feel — see the Nav* tokens below. Status/tier indicators are plain text with no color
    /// coding at all for now (no blues/greens/reds/yellows anywhere).
    /// </summary>
    public static class BrandPalette
    {
        public static readonly Color Brand = ColorTranslator.FromHtml("#572400");

        // --- Shades (brand mixed toward black) : darker, for pressed states / headers ---
        public static readonly Color Shade10 = Shade(Brand, 0.10);
        public static readonly Color Shade25 = Shade(Brand, 0.25);
        public static readonly Color Shade45 = Shade(Brand, 0.45); // pressed button state

        // --- Tints (brand mixed toward white) : lighter, for surfaces / hover / disabled ---
        public static readonly Color Tint10 = Tint(Brand, 0.10);
        public static readonly Color Tint25 = Tint(Brand, 0.25); // hover state
        public static readonly Color Tint50 = Tint(Brand, 0.50);
        public static readonly Color Tint75 = Tint(Brand, 0.75); // subtle card background
        public static readonly Color Tint85 = Tint(Brand, 0.85); // sidebar chrome — a step darker than the page background
        public static readonly Color Tint90 = Tint(Brand, 0.90); // page background
        public static readonly Color Tint95 = Tint(Brand, 0.95); // near-white panel background

        // --- Tones (brand mixed toward mid-grey) : muted, for borders / disabled fills ---
        public static readonly Color Tone35 = Tone(Brand, 0.35);
        public static readonly Color Tone60 = Tone(Brand, 0.60); // disabled control fill
        public static readonly Color Tone80 = Tone(Brand, 0.80); // divider lines

        // --- Neutrals: white/black and brand-mixed greys only ---
        public static readonly Color White = Color.White;
        public static readonly Color Black = Color.Black;
        public static readonly Color NeutralText = Shade(Brand, 0.55);   // body text (brand-tinted near-black)
        public static readonly Color NeutralTextMuted = Tone(Brand, 0.45); // secondary text

        // --- Semantic roles, expressed via VALUE not hue ---
        public static Color SurfacePrimary => White;
        public static Color SurfaceSecondary => Tint95;
        public static Color SurfaceCard => Tint90;
        public static Color Border => Tone80;

        // Table headers: a light, brand-tinted band with dark text — not a full-brand fill.
        // The main color stays reserved for primary actions (buttons) so it doesn't compete
        // for attention across every grid in the app. Swapped with RowSelected below: the
        // header now takes the stronger of the two tints, so it stands out over a selected row.
        public static Color HeaderBackground => Tint50;
        public static Color HeaderText => NeutralText;

        // Selected row highlight — was the header's tint, now the lighter one.
        public static Color RowSelected => Tint90;

        public static Color ButtonPrimaryFill => Brand;
        public static Color ButtonPrimaryHover => Shade10;
        public static Color ButtonPrimaryPressed => Shade25;
        public static Color ButtonPrimaryText => White;

        public static Color ButtonSecondaryFill => Tint75;
        public static Color ButtonSecondaryHover => Tint50;
        public static Color ButtonSecondaryText => Shade(Brand, 0.30);

        // Plain neutral grey — deliberately NOT a tint/shade of the brand color, so a
        // disabled button reads as "off" rather than as a duller version of the same action.
        public static Color ButtonDisabledFill => Color.FromArgb(255, 210, 205, 198);
        public static Color ButtonDisabledText => Color.FromArgb(255, 135, 128, 120);

        // "Positive" state (points earned, resolved feedback, success) -> lightest fill, dark text
        public static Color StatusPositiveFill => Tint75;
        public static Color StatusPositiveText => Shade25;

        // "Attention" state (low stock, pending feedback, warnings) -> mid-tone, bolder text
        public static Color StatusAttentionFill => Tint50;
        public static Color StatusAttentionText => Shade45;

        // "Negative" state (dismissed, locked account, errors) -> darkest shade, high contrast text
        public static Color StatusNegativeFill => Shade25;
        public static Color StatusNegativeText => White;

        // --- Sidebar/nav chrome: a distinct step in tone from both the content background
        // (Tint95) and the table/card surfaces (White), so the sidebar reads as its own
        // region rather than blending into whatever's next to it. ---
        public static Color NavBackground => Tint85;
        public static Color NavFooterBackground => Tint75;
        public static Color NavText => NeutralTextMuted;
        public static Color NavActiveBackground => White;
        public static Color NavActiveText => Brand;
        public static Color NavHoverBackground => Color.White;

        // Unfilled star rating — Border (Tone80) reads too faint against white; Tone35 keeps
        // the same neutral hue family but with enough contrast to actually see the outline.
        public static Color RatingInactive => Tone35;

        // --- Top bar: thin brand strip above the content/sidebar (icon mark + tab name) ---
        public static Color TopBarBackground => White;
        public static Color TopBarBorder => Border;

        public static Font BrandFontRegular(float size) => TryCreateFont("Bahnschrift SemiLight", size, FontStyle.Regular);
        public static Font BrandFontBold(float size) => TryCreateFont("Bahnschrift SemiBold", size, FontStyle.Bold);

        private static Font TryCreateFont(string family, float size, FontStyle style)
        {
            try
            {
                return new Font(family, size, style);
            }
            catch
            {
                // Falls back gracefully if the custom font isn't installed on a given machine.
                return new Font(FontFamily.GenericSansSerif, size, style);
            }
        }

        private static Color Tint(Color c, double amount) => Mix(c, Color.White, amount);
        private static Color Shade(Color c, double amount) => Mix(c, Color.Black, amount);

        // "Tone" = mix toward a neutral mid-grey derived from the brand itself, for muted/disabled elements.
        private static Color Tone(Color c, double amount)
        {
            var grey = Mix(c, Color.Black, 0.5);
            grey = Mix(grey, Color.White, 0.5);
            return Mix(c, grey, amount);
        }

        private static Color Mix(Color a, Color b, double amount)
        {
            amount = Math.Clamp(amount, 0.0, 1.0);
            int r = (int)Math.Round(a.R + (b.R - a.R) * amount);
            int g = (int)Math.Round(a.G + (b.G - a.G) * amount);
            int bl = (int)Math.Round(a.B + (b.B - a.B) * amount);
            return Color.FromArgb(255, r, g, bl);
        }
    }
}


===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Theme/ButtonStyler.cs =====
namespace CRM_Morcaffein.Theme
{
    /// <summary>
    /// FlatStyle.Flat buttons don't repaint their fill when Enabled flips false — they just
    /// grey the text and keep whatever BackColor was set, so a disabled primary button still
    /// looks like a brand-colored, seemingly-clickable button. Call TrackEnabledState once
    /// (after the button's "active" colors are set) on any button whose Enabled is toggled at
    /// runtime, and it'll swap to the neutral disabled palette automatically.
    /// </summary>
    public static class ButtonStyler
    {
        public static void TrackEnabledState(Button button)
        {
            var activeBackColor = button.BackColor;
            var activeForeColor = button.ForeColor;

            void Apply()
            {
                button.BackColor = button.Enabled ? activeBackColor : BrandPalette.ButtonDisabledFill;
                button.ForeColor = button.Enabled ? activeForeColor : BrandPalette.ButtonDisabledText;
            }

            button.EnabledChanged += (s, e) => Apply();
            Apply(); // the button may already be disabled at hook-up time (e.g. designer default)
        }
    }
}


===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/Controls/ApprovalCardControl.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// One horizontal card in the Admin's Approvals queue. Paints itself entirely in
    /// OnPaint, no child controls, same convention as ProductCardControl and
    /// CampaignCardControl.
    ///
    /// Layout (two lines, full row width, ~72px tall):
    ///   Line 1: [ Promo name (bold) ]  [ Category (muted) ]  [ Discount (brand, bold) ]
    ///   Line 2: [ Date range · by Creator (muted small) ]              [ Status pill ]
    ///
    /// The status pill is drawn flush right on line 2, using the same fill/text scheme
    /// as CampaignCardControl so the two views read consistently.
    ///
    /// Click anywhere on the card to open its detail in the drawer.
    /// </summary>
    public class ApprovalCardControl : Panel
    {
        public CampaignResponseDto Campaign { get; }

        public event EventHandler? CardClicked;

        // ---- Card metrics ----
        private const int CardPadding = 16;
        private const int Line1Top = 14;
        private const int Line1Height = 24;
        private const int Line2Top = 40;
        private const int Line2Height = 20;
        private const int PillHeight = 22;
        private const int PillPaddingX = 12;

        // ---- Shared paint resources (static so all cards share one instance) ----
        private static readonly Font NameFont = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(11f);
        private static readonly Font CategoryFont = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f);
        private static readonly Font DiscountFont = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(11f);
        private static readonly Font MetaFont = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f);
        private static readonly Font StatusFont = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9f);

        private static readonly SolidBrush NameBrush = new(BrandPalette.NeutralText);
        private static readonly SolidBrush CategoryBrush = new(BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted);
        private static readonly SolidBrush DiscountBrush = new(BrandPalette.Brand);
        private static readonly SolidBrush MetaBrush = new(BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted);

        private static readonly StringFormat EllipsisFormat = new()
        {
            Trimming = StringTrimming.EllipsisCharacter,
            FormatFlags = StringFormatFlags.NoWrap,
        };

        public ApprovalCardControl(CampaignResponseDto campaign)
        {
            Campaign = campaign;

            Height = 72;
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 8);
            BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary;
            Cursor = Cursors.Hand;

            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint
                   | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint
                   | ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer
                   | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);

            Click += (s, e) => CardClicked?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);

            MouseEnter += (s, e) => { BackColor = BrandPalette.Tint95; Invalidate(); };
            MouseLeave += (s, e) => { BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary; Invalidate(); };
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            var g = e.Graphics;
            g.TextRenderingHint = System.Drawing.Text.TextRenderingHint.ClearTypeGridFit;

            // Border
            using var borderPen = new Pen(BrandPalette.Border);
            g.DrawRectangle(borderPen, 0, 0, Width - 1, Height - 1);

            var usableWidth = Width - CardPadding * 2;

            // ---- Line 1 ----
            // Left to right: name, category, discount. Positioned by measuring each so
            // they can't collide.
            var nameSize = g.MeasureString(Campaign.PromoName, NameFont);
            var nameWidth = (int)Math.Ceiling(nameSize.Width);
            var nameRect = new RectangleF(CardPadding, Line1Top, nameWidth, Line1Height);
            g.DrawString(Campaign.PromoName, NameFont, NameBrush, nameRect, EllipsisFormat);

            var categoryX = CardPadding + nameWidth + 16;
            var categorySize = g.MeasureString(Campaign.PromoCategory, CategoryFont);
            var categoryWidth = (int)Math.Ceiling(categorySize.Width);
            var categoryRect = new RectangleF(categoryX, Line1Top + 4, categoryWidth, Line1Height);
            g.DrawString(Campaign.PromoCategory, CategoryFont, CategoryBrush, categoryRect, EllipsisFormat);

            var discountX = categoryX + categoryWidth + 16;
            var discountText = FormatDiscount(Campaign.DiscountType, Campaign.DiscountValue);
            var discountRect = new RectangleF(
                discountX, Line1Top,
                Math.Max(0, Width - CardPadding - discountX),
                Line1Height);
            g.DrawString(discountText, DiscountFont, DiscountBrush, discountRect, EllipsisFormat);

            // ---- Line 2 ----
            // Date range + creator on the left, status pill flush right.
            var metaText = $"{Campaign.StartDate:MMM d, yyyy} – {Campaign.EndDate:MMM d, yyyy}";
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Campaign.CreatedByName) && Campaign.CreatedByName != "—")
                metaText += $"  ·  by {Campaign.CreatedByName}";

            var metaRect = new RectangleF(
                CardPadding, Line2Top,
                usableWidth - 160,   // reserve room for the pill on the right
                Line2Height);
            g.DrawString(metaText, MetaFont, MetaBrush, metaRect, EllipsisFormat);

            DrawStatusPill(g);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Renders the status pill flush right on line 2, matching the color scheme used
        /// by CampaignCardControl: Active = light brand-tinted fill with dark text;
        /// everything else = dark warm-grey fill with the lightest brand tint as text.
        /// </summary>
        private void DrawStatusPill(Graphics g)
        {
            var label = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Campaign.Status) ? "—" : Campaign.Status;
            var isActive = label.Equals("Active", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            var fillColor = isActive ? BrandPalette.Tint75 : BrandPalette.Tone35;
            var textColor = isActive ? BrandPalette.Shade25 : BrandPalette.Tint95;

            var textSize = g.MeasureString(label, StatusFont);
            var pillWidth = (int)Math.Ceiling(textSize.Width) + PillPaddingX * 2;
            var pillX = Width - CardPadding - pillWidth;
            var pillY = Line2Top - 2;   // slight upward nudge so the pill centers on line 2

            var pillRect = new Rectangle(pillX, pillY, pillWidth, PillHeight);

            using var fillBrush = new SolidBrush(fillColor);
            using var textBrush = new SolidBrush(textColor);

            // Rounded pill via the same helper pattern as CampaignCardControl.
            using var path = RoundedRect(pillRect, PillHeight / 2);

            var savedMode = g.SmoothingMode;
            g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            g.FillPath(fillBrush, path);
            g.SmoothingMode = savedMode;

            var textRect = new RectangleF(
                pillRect.X,
                pillRect.Y,
                pillRect.Width,
                pillRect.Height);

            var centered = new StringFormat
            {
                Alignment = StringAlignment.Center,
                LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center,
            };
            g.DrawString(label, StatusFont, textBrush, textRect, centered);
        }

        private static System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath RoundedRect(Rectangle rect, int radius)
        {
            var path = new System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath();
            var d = radius * 2;

            path.AddArc(rect.X, rect.Y, d, d, 180, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.Right - d, rect.Y, d, d, 270, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.Right - d, rect.Bottom - d, d, d, 0, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.X, rect.Bottom - d, d, d, 90, 90);
            path.CloseFigure();
            return path;
        }

        private static string FormatDiscount(string type, decimal value) =>
            type.Equals("Percentage", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                ? $"{value:0.##}% off"
                : $"₱{value:0.##} off";
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/Controls/ApprovalsView.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Session;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// The Admin's Approvals tab. Lists pending Manager-submitted campaigns as horizontal
    /// cards on the right; clicking one opens its detail in the left drawer, where the
    /// Admin can Approve or Reject it.
    ///
    /// Campaigns come from GET /campaigns/pending — the same endpoint the API exposes
    /// specifically for this surface. Approve flips them Active; Reject moves them to
    /// Rejected with a required reason.
    ///
    /// Admin-only. FrmMain wires this in place of the Approvals placeholder.
    /// </summary>
    public class ApprovalsView : UserControl
    {
        private readonly CampaignController _controller;

        // ---- Header ----
        private Panel _headerPanel = null!;
        private Label _titleLabel = null!;
        private Label _countLabel = null!;

        // ---- Cards area ----
        private Panel _cardsScrollHost = null!;
        private FlowLayoutPanel _cardsFlow = null!;
        private Label _emptyStateLabel = null!;

        // ---- Drawer ----
        private Panel _drawerPanel = null!;
        private Label _drawerEmptyLabel = null!;
        private Panel _drawerContentPanel = null!;
        private Panel _detailBoxPanel = null!;
        private Panel _decisionPanel = null!;

        // Drawer content controls
        private Label _campaignNameLabel = null!;
        private Label _campaignMetaLabel = null!;
        private Label _detailCategoryLabel = null!;
        private Label _detailDiscountLabel = null!;
        private Label _detailTierLabel = null!;
        private Label _detailMinPurchaseLabel = null!;
        private Label _detailMaxDiscountLabel = null!;
        private Label _detailMaxRedemptionsLabel = null!;
        private Label _detailStartLabel = null!;
        private Label _detailEndLabel = null!;
        private Label _detailCreatorLabel = null!;
        private Label _detailRequestedAtLabel = null!;

        private TextBox _decisionNotesTxtBx = null!;
        private Label _decisionErrorLabel = null!;
        private Button _approveBtn = null!;
        private Button _rejectBtn = null!;

        private CampaignResponseDto? _selectedCampaign;

        // ---- Card metrics (kept in one place so the layout math can't drift) ----
        private const int CardHeight = 72;
        private const int CardGap = 8;

        // ---- Drawer metrics ----
        private const int DrawerWidth = 420;
        private const int DetailBoxWidth = 340;
        private const int DetailBoxHeight = 400;
        private const int DecisionPanelWidth = 340;
        private const int DecisionPanelHeight = 200;
        private const int DrawerInsetX = 18;
        private const int DetailRowSpacing = 28;

        public ApprovalsView()
        {
            _controller = new CampaignController();
            BuildLayout();
            Load += async (s, e) => await ReloadAsync();
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // LAYOUT
        // =====================================================================

        private void BuildLayout()
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceSecondary;

            var bodyPanel = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceSecondary,
            };

            // ---------- RIGHT: card with header + list of approval cards ----------
