            var cardOuter = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceSecondary,
                Padding = new Padding(24),
            };

            var card = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
            };

            _headerPanel = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                Height = 76,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
            };

            _titleLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Pending Campaign Approvals",
                Location = new Point(20, 18),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(13f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            _countLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(20, 44),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            var headerDivider = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Bottom,
                Height = 1,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.Border,
            };

            _headerPanel.Controls.Add(_titleLabel);
            _headerPanel.Controls.Add(_countLabel);
            _headerPanel.Controls.Add(headerDivider);

            // ---------- Cards area ----------
            _cardsScrollHost = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                AutoScroll = true,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                Padding = new Padding(16, 12, 16, 12),
            };

            _cardsFlow = new FlowLayoutPanel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                FlowDirection = FlowDirection.TopDown,
                WrapContents = false,
                AutoSize = false,               // we control width and height explicitly
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                Padding = new Padding(0),
            };

            _emptyStateLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "No pending approvals. Campaigns submitted by Managers appear here.",
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleCenter,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                Visible = false,
            };

            _cardsScrollHost.Controls.Add(_cardsFlow);
            _cardsScrollHost.Controls.Add(_emptyStateLabel);
            _cardsScrollHost.Resize += (s, e) => SyncCardsFlowWidth();

            card.Controls.Add(_cardsScrollHost);
            card.Controls.Add(_headerPanel);

            cardOuter.Controls.Add(card);

            // ---------- LEFT: drawer ----------
            _drawerPanel = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Left,
                Width = DrawerWidth,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceCard,
            };

            _drawerEmptyLabel = new Label
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleCenter,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                Text = "Select a campaign to review.",
            };

            _drawerContentPanel = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                Visible = false,
                AutoScroll = true,
            };

            var drawerFlow = new FlowLayoutPanel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                FlowDirection = FlowDirection.TopDown,
                WrapContents = false,
                AutoSize = true,
                AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink,
                Padding = new Padding(20),
                Width = DrawerWidth,
            };

            // ---- Campaign Details header (sits above the box, distinct from it) ----
            var detailHeaderLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Campaign Details",
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(10f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
                Margin = new Padding(4, 0, 0, 8),
            };
            drawerFlow.Controls.Add(detailHeaderLabel);

            // ---- Detail box ----
            _detailBoxPanel = BuildDetailBox();

            drawerFlow.Controls.Add(_detailBoxPanel);

            // ---- Requested-at line ----
            _detailRequestedAtLabel = new Label
            {
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
                Margin = new Padding(4, 0, 0, 14),
            };
            drawerFlow.Controls.Add(_detailRequestedAtLabel);

            // ---- Decision panel ----
            _decisionPanel = BuildDecisionPanel();
            drawerFlow.Controls.Add(_decisionPanel);

            _drawerContentPanel.Controls.Add(drawerFlow);
            _drawerPanel.Controls.Add(_drawerEmptyLabel);
            _drawerPanel.Controls.Add(_drawerContentPanel);

            bodyPanel.Controls.Add(cardOuter);
            bodyPanel.Controls.Add(_drawerPanel);

            Controls.Add(bodyPanel);
        }

        private Panel BuildDetailBox()
        {
            var box = new Panel
            {
                Size = new Size(DetailBoxWidth, DetailBoxHeight),
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
                Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 16),
            };

            // Header is placed OUTSIDE this box (see BuildLayout's drawer flow) so the
            // box itself reads as one self-contained region without a nested header.
            _campaignNameLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(DrawerInsetX, 18),
                MaximumSize = new Size(DetailBoxWidth - DrawerInsetX * 2, 0),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(14f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            _campaignMetaLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(DrawerInsetX, 48),
                MaximumSize = new Size(DetailBoxWidth - DrawerInsetX * 2, 0),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.Brand,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            // Field rows, each spaced DetailRowSpacing apart, starting below the meta line.
            var fieldTop = 84;
            _detailCategoryLabel = MakeDetailLabel(DrawerInsetX, fieldTop);
            _detailDiscountLabel = MakeDetailLabel(DrawerInsetX, fieldTop + DetailRowSpacing * 1);
            _detailTierLabel = MakeDetailLabel(DrawerInsetX, fieldTop + DetailRowSpacing * 2);
            _detailMinPurchaseLabel = MakeDetailLabel(DrawerInsetX, fieldTop + DetailRowSpacing * 3);
            _detailMaxDiscountLabel = MakeDetailLabel(DrawerInsetX, fieldTop + DetailRowSpacing * 4);
            _detailMaxRedemptionsLabel = MakeDetailLabel(DrawerInsetX, fieldTop + DetailRowSpacing * 5);
            _detailStartLabel = MakeDetailLabel(DrawerInsetX, fieldTop + DetailRowSpacing * 6);
            _detailEndLabel = MakeDetailLabel(DrawerInsetX, fieldTop + DetailRowSpacing * 7);
            _detailCreatorLabel = MakeDetailLabel(DrawerInsetX, fieldTop + DetailRowSpacing * 8);

            box.Controls.Add(_campaignNameLabel);
            box.Controls.Add(_campaignMetaLabel);
            box.Controls.Add(_detailCategoryLabel);
            box.Controls.Add(_detailDiscountLabel);
            box.Controls.Add(_detailTierLabel);
            box.Controls.Add(_detailMinPurchaseLabel);
            box.Controls.Add(_detailMaxDiscountLabel);
            box.Controls.Add(_detailMaxRedemptionsLabel);
            box.Controls.Add(_detailStartLabel);
            box.Controls.Add(_detailEndLabel);
            box.Controls.Add(_detailCreatorLabel);

            return box;
        }

        private Panel BuildDecisionPanel()
        {
            var panel = new Panel
            {
                Size = new Size(DecisionPanelWidth, DecisionPanelHeight),
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
                Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 16),
            };

            const int inset = 14;

            var header = new Label
            {
                Text = "Decision",
                Location = new Point(inset, 12),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            var hint = new Label
            {
                Text = "Notes optional on approve; required on reject.",
                Location = new Point(inset, 34),
                Size = new Size(DecisionPanelWidth - inset * 2, 20),
                AutoSize = false,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft,
            };

            _decisionNotesTxtBx = new TextBox
            {
                Location = new Point(inset, 62),
                Size = new Size(DecisionPanelWidth - inset * 2, 60),
                Multiline = true,
                ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Vertical,
                PlaceholderText = "Notes / rejection reason",
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
            };
            _decisionNotesTxtBx.TextChanged += (s, e) => UpdateRejectEnabled();

            _decisionErrorLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(inset, 128),
                Size = new Size(DecisionPanelWidth - inset * 2, 20),
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
                Visible = false,
            };

            var buttonWidth = (DecisionPanelWidth - inset * 2 - 8) / 2;   // two buttons, 8px gap

            _approveBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Approve",
                Location = new Point(inset, 152),
                Size = new Size(buttonWidth, 32),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _approveBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _approveBtn.Click += ApproveBtn_Click;

            _rejectBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Reject",
                Location = new Point(inset + buttonWidth + 8, 152),
                Size = new Size(buttonWidth, 32),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _rejectBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _rejectBtn.Click += RejectBtn_Click;

            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_approveBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_rejectBtn);

            panel.Controls.Add(_decisionErrorLabel);
            panel.Controls.Add(_approveBtn);
            panel.Controls.Add(_rejectBtn);
            panel.Controls.Add(_decisionNotesTxtBx);
            panel.Controls.Add(hint);
            panel.Controls.Add(header);

            return panel;
        }

        private static Label MakeDetailLabel(int x, int y) => new()
        {
            Location = new Point(x, y),
            AutoSize = true,
            Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
            ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
            BackColor = Color.Transparent,
        };

        private void SyncCardsFlowWidth()
        {
            if (_cardsScrollHost is null || _cardsFlow is null)
                return;

            var hostWidth = _cardsScrollHost.ClientSize.Width;
            if (hostWidth <= 0)
                return;

            // Reserve for the vertical scrollbar if it appears.
            var usableWidth = Math.Max(200, hostWidth
                - _cardsScrollHost.Padding.Horizontal
                - SystemInformation.VerticalScrollBarWidth);

            _cardsFlow.Width = usableWidth;

            foreach (var c in _cardsFlow.Controls.OfType<ApprovalCardControl>())
            {
                c.Width = usableWidth;
                c.Height = CardHeight;
            }

            _cardsFlow.PerformLayout();
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // DATA
        // =====================================================================

        private async Task ReloadAsync()
        {
            List<CampaignResponseDto> pending;
            try
            {
                pending = await _controller.GetPendingAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, "Error loading pending approvals");
                return;
            }

            // Clear selection and drawer
            _selectedCampaign = null;
            RefreshDrawer();

            // The width we should give each card. Computed once, used both when creating
            // the cards and when resizing the flow panel afterwards — using the flow panel's
            // own Width here would give 0 on first load, which made the cards invisible.
            var cardWidth = Math.Max(200,
                _cardsScrollHost.ClientSize.Width
                - _cardsScrollHost.Padding.Horizontal
                - SystemInformation.VerticalScrollBarWidth);

            _cardsFlow.SuspendLayout();
            try
            {
                _cardsFlow.Controls.Clear();
                _cardsFlow.Width = cardWidth;

                if (pending.Count == 0)
                {
                    _emptyStateLabel.Visible = true;
                    _cardsFlow.Visible = false;
                    _cardsFlow.Height = 0;
                }
                else
                {
                    _emptyStateLabel.Visible = false;
                    _cardsFlow.Visible = true;

                    foreach (var campaign in pending)
                    {
                        var card = new ApprovalCardControl(campaign)
                        {
                            Width = cardWidth,
                        };
                        card.CardClicked += (s, e) => SelectCampaign(campaign);
                        _cardsFlow.Controls.Add(card);
                    }

                    _cardsFlow.Height = pending.Count * (CardHeight + CardGap) + 4;
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                _cardsFlow.ResumeLayout(performLayout: true);
            }

            _countLabel.Text = pending.Count == 1
                ? "1 campaign awaiting approval"
                : $"{pending.Count} campaigns awaiting approval";
        }

        private void SelectCampaign(CampaignResponseDto campaign)
        {
            _selectedCampaign = campaign;
            RefreshDrawer();
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // DRAWER
        // =====================================================================

        private void RefreshDrawer()
        {
            var campaign = _selectedCampaign;
            if (campaign is null)
            {
                _drawerEmptyLabel.Visible = true;
                _drawerContentPanel.Visible = false;
                return;
            }

            _drawerEmptyLabel.Visible = false;
            _drawerContentPanel.Visible = true;

            _campaignNameLabel.Text = campaign.PromoName;
            _campaignMetaLabel.Text = FormatDiscount(campaign.DiscountType, campaign.DiscountValue);

            _detailCategoryLabel.Text = $"Category: {campaign.PromoCategory}";
            _detailDiscountLabel.Text = $"Discount: {FormatDiscount(campaign.DiscountType, campaign.DiscountValue)}";
            _detailTierLabel.Text = $"Target tier: {campaign.TargetTier}";
            _detailMinPurchaseLabel.Text = $"Min purchase: {(campaign.MinPurchaseAmount.HasValue ? $"₱{campaign.MinPurchaseAmount.Value:0.00}" : "—")}";
            _detailMaxDiscountLabel.Text = $"Max discount: {(campaign.MaxDiscountAmount.HasValue ? $"₱{campaign.MaxDiscountAmount.Value:0.00}" : "—")}";
            _detailMaxRedemptionsLabel.Text = $"Max redemptions: {campaign.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer} per customer";
            _detailStartLabel.Text = $"Start: {campaign.StartDate:MMMM d, yyyy}";
            _detailEndLabel.Text = $"End: {campaign.EndDate:MMMM d, yyyy}";
            _detailCreatorLabel.Text = $"Requested by: {(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(campaign.CreatedByName) ? "—" : campaign.CreatedByName)}";

            _detailRequestedAtLabel.Text = campaign.DecidedAt.HasValue
                ? $"Decision recorded {campaign.DecidedAt.Value:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm}"
                : "Awaiting decision";

            // Reset decision panel
            _decisionNotesTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;
            _decisionErrorLabel.Text = string.Empty;
            _decisionErrorLabel.Visible = false;

            var isPending = campaign.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            _approveBtn.Enabled = isPending;
            _rejectBtn.Enabled = isPending && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_decisionNotesTxtBx.Text);
        }

        private void UpdateRejectEnabled()
        {
            var isPending = _selectedCampaign?.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true;
            _rejectBtn.Enabled = isPending && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_decisionNotesTxtBx.Text);
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // ACTIONS
        // =====================================================================

        private async void ApproveBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedCampaign is null) return;

            var campaign = _selectedCampaign;
            var notes = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_decisionNotesTxtBx.Text)
                ? null
                : _decisionNotesTxtBx.Text.Trim();

            var confirm = MessageBox.Show(
                FindForm(),
                $"Approve \"{campaign.PromoName}\"? It will go live immediately.",
                "Approve Campaign",
                MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                MessageBoxIcon.Question);

            if (confirm != DialogResult.Yes) return;

            try
            {
                _approveBtn.Enabled = false;
                _rejectBtn.Enabled = false;

                var adminId = EmployeeSession.Current?.EmployeeId
                    ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No employee session.");

                await _controller.ApproveAsync(campaign.CampaignId, adminId, notes);

                Toast.Show(this, $"Approved \"{campaign.PromoName}\".", ToastKind.Success);

                await ReloadAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _decisionErrorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
                _decisionErrorLabel.Visible = true;
            }
            finally
            {
                _approveBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }

        private async void RejectBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedCampaign is null) return;

            var notes = _decisionNotesTxtBx.Text.Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(notes))
            {
                _decisionErrorLabel.Text = "A rejection reason is required.";
                _decisionErrorLabel.Visible = true;
                return;
            }

            var campaign = _selectedCampaign;

            var confirm = MessageBox.Show(
                FindForm(),
                $"Reject \"{campaign.PromoName}\"? The Manager will see your reason and can edit and resubmit.",
                "Reject Campaign",
                MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                MessageBoxIcon.Warning);

            if (confirm != DialogResult.Yes) return;

            try
            {
                _approveBtn.Enabled = false;
                _rejectBtn.Enabled = false;

                var adminId = EmployeeSession.Current?.EmployeeId
                    ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No employee session.");

                await _controller.RejectAsync(campaign.CampaignId, adminId, notes);

                Toast.Show(this, $"Rejected \"{campaign.PromoName}\".", ToastKind.Info);

                await ReloadAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _decisionErrorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
                _decisionErrorLabel.Visible = true;
            }
            finally
            {
                _approveBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }

        private static string FormatDiscount(string type, decimal value) =>
            type.Equals("Percentage", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                ? $"{value:0.##}% off"
                : $"₱{value:0.##} off";
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/Controls/CampaignCardControl.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// One campaign tile on the Manager's Campaign tab. Paints itself entirely in
    /// OnPaint with no child controls (same convention as ProductCardControl — cheaper
    /// to build, no layout flicker when many cards are created at once).
    ///
    /// Layout:
    ///   [ Campaign name (bold, 2 lines max) ]
    ///   [ Category label (muted small)      ]
    ///   [ Discount summary (brand color)    ]
    ///   [ Date range (muted small)          ]
    ///   [ Status pill (bottom)              ]
    ///
    /// Click anywhere on the card to open the detail modal — the whole tile is the
    /// hit target, there are no interactive sub-regions.
    /// </summary>
    public class CampaignCardControl : Panel
    {
        public CampaignResponseDto Campaign { get; }

        public event EventHandler? CardClicked;

        // ---- Card metrics ----
        private const int CardPadding = 12;
        private const int NameHeight = 40;     // room for up to two lines
        private const int CategoryHeight = 20;
        private const int DiscountHeight = 28;
        private const int DateRangeHeight = 20;
        private const int StatusHeight = 24;

        // ---- Shared paint resources (static so all cards share one instance) ----
        private static readonly Font NameFont = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(11f);
        private static readonly Font CategoryFont = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f);
        private static readonly Font DiscountFont = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(13f);
        private static readonly Font DateFont = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f);
        private static readonly Font StatusFont = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9f);

        private static readonly SolidBrush NameBrush = new(BrandPalette.NeutralText);
        private static readonly SolidBrush CategoryBrush = new(BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted);
        private static readonly SolidBrush DiscountBrush = new(BrandPalette.Brand);
        private static readonly SolidBrush DateBrush = new(BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted);

        private static readonly StringFormat WrapEllipsisFormat = new()
        {
            Trimming = StringTrimming.EllipsisCharacter,
            FormatFlags = StringFormatFlags.LineLimit,
        };

        public CampaignCardControl(CampaignResponseDto campaign)
        {
            Campaign = campaign;

            Size = new Size(260, 200);
            Margin = new Padding(8);
            BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary;
            Cursor = Cursors.Hand;

            SetStyle(ControlStyles.UserPaint
                   | ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint
                   | ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer
                   | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);

            Click += (s, e) => CardClicked?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);

            MouseEnter += (s, e) => { BackColor = BrandPalette.Tint90; Invalidate(); };
            MouseLeave += (s, e) => { BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary; Invalidate(); };
        }

        protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            var g = e.Graphics;
            g.TextRenderingHint = System.Drawing.Text.TextRenderingHint.ClearTypeGridFit;

            // Border
            using var borderPen = new Pen(BrandPalette.Border);
            g.DrawRectangle(borderPen, 0, 0, Width - 1, Height - 1);

            int y = CardPadding;

            // ---- Campaign name (up to two lines) ----
            var nameRect = new RectangleF(
                CardPadding, y,
                Width - CardPadding * 2,
                NameHeight);
            g.DrawString(Campaign.PromoName, NameFont, NameBrush, nameRect, WrapEllipsisFormat);
            y += NameHeight;

            // ---- Category ----
            var categoryRect = new RectangleF(
                CardPadding, y,
                Width - CardPadding * 2,
                CategoryHeight);
            g.DrawString(Campaign.PromoCategory, CategoryFont, CategoryBrush, categoryRect, WrapEllipsisFormat);
            y += CategoryHeight + 4;

            // ---- Discount summary ----
            var discountText = FormatDiscount(Campaign.DiscountType, Campaign.DiscountValue);
            var discountRect = new RectangleF(
                CardPadding, y,
                Width - CardPadding * 2,
                DiscountHeight);
            g.DrawString(discountText, DiscountFont, DiscountBrush, discountRect, WrapEllipsisFormat);
            y += DiscountHeight + 2;

            // ---- Date range ----
            var dateText = $"{Campaign.StartDate:MMM d, yyyy} – {Campaign.EndDate:MMM d, yyyy}";
            var dateRect = new RectangleF(
                CardPadding, y,
                Width - CardPadding * 2,
                DateRangeHeight);
            g.DrawString(dateText, DateFont, DateBrush, dateRect, WrapEllipsisFormat);

            // ---- Status pill, bottom of the card ----
            DrawStatusPill(g);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Renders the campaign's status as a filled pill at the bottom-left of the card.
        /// Active = filled brand tint, Inactive/Pending/Rejected = muted neutral.
        /// </summary>
        private void DrawStatusPill(Graphics g)
        {
            var label = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Campaign.Status) ? "—" : Campaign.Status;

            var isActive = label.Equals("Active", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            var fillColor = isActive ? BrandPalette.Tint75 : BrandPalette.Tone35;
            var textColor = isActive ? BrandPalette.Shade25 : BrandPalette.Tint95;

            // Measure the text so the pill hugs its contents.
            var textSize = g.MeasureString(label, StatusFont);

            var pillPaddingX = 10;
            var pillWidth = (int)Math.Ceiling(textSize.Width) + pillPaddingX * 2;
            var pillRect = new Rectangle(
                CardPadding,
                Height - CardPadding - StatusHeight,
                pillWidth,
                StatusHeight);

            using var fillBrush = new SolidBrush(fillColor);
            using var textBrush = new SolidBrush(textColor);

            // Rounded-ish pill via a small corner radius approximation.
            var radius = StatusHeight / 2;
            using var path = RoundedRect(pillRect, radius);
            g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
            g.FillPath(fillBrush, path);

            var textRect = new RectangleF(
                pillRect.X,
                pillRect.Y,
                pillRect.Width,
                pillRect.Height);
            var centered = new StringFormat
            {
                Alignment = StringAlignment.Center,
                LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center,
            };
            g.DrawString(label, StatusFont, textBrush, textRect, centered);
        }

        private static System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath RoundedRect(Rectangle rect, int radius)
        {
            var path = new System.Drawing.Drawing2D.GraphicsPath();
            var d = radius * 2;

            path.AddArc(rect.X, rect.Y, d, d, 180, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.Right - d, rect.Y, d, d, 270, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.Right - d, rect.Bottom - d, d, d, 0, 90);
            path.AddArc(rect.X, rect.Bottom - d, d, d, 90, 90);
            path.CloseFigure();
            return path;
        }

        private static string FormatDiscount(string discountType, decimal discountValue)
        {
            if (discountType.Equals("Percentage", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                return $"{discountValue:0.##}% off";

            return $"₱{discountValue:0.##} off";
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/Controls/CampaignManagementView.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Session;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// The Manager's Campaign tab. A grid of campaign cards — no drawer, no table.
    /// Clicking a card opens the detail modal (FrmCampaignDetail), which handles editing
    /// and the activate/deactivate actions.
    ///
    /// This view is Manager-only. Baristas never see the tab (FrmMain doesn't include it
    /// for them). Admin also doesn't see it — their Campaign oversight goes through the
    /// Approvals tab, not here.
    /// </summary>
    public class CampaignManagementView : UserControl
    {
        private const int CampaignColumnCount = 4;
        private const int CardWidth = 260;
        private const int CardHeight = 200;
        private const int CardMargin = 8;
        private const int RowHeight = CardHeight + CardMargin * 2;   // 216

        private readonly CampaignController _controller;

        // Header
        private Panel _cardHeaderPanel = null!;
        private TextBox _searchTxtBx = null!;
        private Button _newCampaignBtn = null!;

        // Card grid (mirrors TransactionView's drinks area)
        private Panel _cardsScrollHost = null!;
        private TableLayoutPanel _cardsGrid = null!;
        private Label _emptyStateLabel = null!;

        // Debounce for the search box — avoids one API call per keystroke.
        private System.Windows.Forms.Timer _searchDebounce = null!;

        // In-memory list of everything fetched; search filters it client-side.
        private List<CampaignResponseDto> _allCampaigns = new();

        // Filter signature so we don't rebuild the grid when nothing changed.
        private string _lastFilterSignature = string.Empty;

        public CampaignManagementView()
        {
            _controller = new CampaignController();
            BuildLayout();
            Load += async (s, e) => await ReloadAsync();
        }

        protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
        {
            if (disposing)
                _searchDebounce?.Dispose();
            base.Dispose(disposing);
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // LAYOUT
        // =====================================================================

        private void BuildLayout()
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceSecondary;

            var cardOuterPanel = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceSecondary,
                Padding = new Padding(24),
            };

            var tableCardPanel = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
            };

            // ---- Header: search + New Campaign ----
            _cardHeaderPanel = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                Height = 60,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
            };

            _searchTxtBx = new TextBox
            {
                Location = new Point(16, 16),
                Size = new Size(300, 28),
                PlaceholderText = "Search by name or category",
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
            };

            // Debounce: rebuild the grid only after the user pauses typing.
            _searchDebounce = new System.Windows.Forms.Timer { Interval = 200 };
            _searchDebounce.Tick += (s, e) =>
            {
                _searchDebounce.Stop();
                RefreshCardsPanel();
            };

            _searchTxtBx.TextChanged += (s, e) =>
            {
                _searchDebounce.Stop();
                _searchDebounce.Start();
            };

            _newCampaignBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "+ New Campaign",
                Size = new Size(170, 32),
                Location = new Point(16, 14),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _newCampaignBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _newCampaignBtn.Click += (s, e) => OpenCreateModal();

            var headerDivider = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Bottom,
                Height = 1,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.Border,
            };

            _cardHeaderPanel.Controls.Add(_newCampaignBtn);
            _cardHeaderPanel.Controls.Add(_searchTxtBx);
            _cardHeaderPanel.Controls.Add(headerDivider);

            // Reposition the button to hug the right edge whenever the header resizes.
            _cardHeaderPanel.Resize += (s, e) =>
            {
                _newCampaignBtn.Left = _cardHeaderPanel.ClientSize.Width - _newCampaignBtn.Width - 16;
                _newCampaignBtn.Top = 14;
            };

            // ---- Card grid ----
            // Same wrapper-Panel-with-AutoScroll + absolutely-positioned TableLayoutPanel
            // pattern as the drinks area in TransactionView. The wrapper scrolls; the grid
            // is Dock=None and its Width/Height are set explicitly.
            _cardsScrollHost = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                AutoScroll = true,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
            };

            _cardsGrid = new TableLayoutPanel
            {
                Location = new Point(0, 0),
                ColumnCount = CampaignColumnCount,
                RowCount = 0,
                AutoScroll = false,
                Padding = new Padding(16),
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                GrowStyle = TableLayoutPanelGrowStyle.AddRows,
            };

            for (int i = 0; i < CampaignColumnCount; i++)
                _cardsGrid.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100f / CampaignColumnCount));

            _emptyStateLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "No campaigns yet. Click \"+ New Campaign\" to create one.",
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleCenter,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
            };

            _cardsScrollHost.Controls.Add(_cardsGrid);
            _cardsScrollHost.Resize += (s, e) => SyncCardsGridWidth();

            tableCardPanel.Controls.Add(_cardsScrollHost);
            tableCardPanel.Controls.Add(_cardHeaderPanel);

            cardOuterPanel.Controls.Add(tableCardPanel);
            Controls.Add(cardOuterPanel);
        }

        private void SyncCardsGridWidth()
        {
            if (_cardsScrollHost is null || _cardsGrid is null)
                return;

            var hostWidth = _cardsScrollHost.ClientSize.Width;
            if (hostWidth <= 0)
                return;

            _cardsGrid.Width = hostWidth;
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // DATA LOADING
        // =====================================================================

        private async Task ReloadAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                _allCampaigns = await _controller.GetAllAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, "Error loading campaigns");
                return;
            }

            // Force a rebuild regardless of the filter signature — the underlying data
            // changed, so even the same filter should produce a fresh render.
            _lastFilterSignature = string.Empty;
            RefreshCardsPanel();
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // CARD GRID
        // =====================================================================

        private void RefreshCardsPanel()
        {
            var searchTerm = _searchTxtBx.Text?.Trim() ?? string.Empty;
            var signature = $"campaigns|{searchTerm}";

            if (signature == _lastFilterSignature && _cardsGrid.Controls.Count > 0)
                return;

            _lastFilterSignature = signature;

            _cardsGrid.SuspendLayout();
            try
            {
                _cardsGrid.Controls.Clear();
                _cardsGrid.RowStyles.Clear();
                _cardsGrid.RowCount = 0;

                // Filter: name or category contains the search term (case-insensitive).
                var filtered = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(searchTerm)
                    ? _allCampaigns
                    : _allCampaigns.Where(c =>
                        c.PromoName.Contains(searchTerm, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                        c.PromoCategory.Contains(searchTerm, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                      .ToList();

                if (filtered.Count == 0)
                {
                    _cardsGrid.RowCount = 1;
                    _cardsGrid.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100f));
                    _cardsGrid.Controls.Add(_emptyStateLabel, 0, 0);
                    _cardsGrid.SetColumnSpan(_emptyStateLabel, CampaignColumnCount);

                    SyncCardsGridWidth();
                    _cardsGrid.Height = Math.Max(
                        _cardsScrollHost.ClientSize.Height,
                        _cardsGrid.Padding.Top + _cardsGrid.Padding.Bottom + 60);
                    return;
                }

                var rows = (filtered.Count + CampaignColumnCount - 1) / CampaignColumnCount;

                for (int r = 0; r < rows; r++)
                    _cardsGrid.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, RowHeight));

                // Spacer row — absorbs leftover vertical space so the last product row
                // doesn't stretch (same fix as TransactionView).
                _cardsGrid.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 100f));
                _cardsGrid.RowCount = rows + 1;

                for (int i = 0; i < filtered.Count; i++)
                {
                    var campaign = filtered[i];
                    var card = new CampaignCardControl(campaign)
                    {
                        Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                        Margin = new Padding(CardMargin),
                    };
                    card.CardClicked += (s, e) => OpenDetailModal(campaign);

                    var row = i / CampaignColumnCount;
                    var col = i % CampaignColumnCount;
                    _cardsGrid.Controls.Add(card, col, row);
                }

                SyncCardsGridWidth();
                var gridHeight = rows * RowHeight
                    + _cardsGrid.Padding.Top + _cardsGrid.Padding.Bottom;
                _cardsGrid.Height = Math.Max(gridHeight, _cardsScrollHost.ClientSize.Height);
            }
            finally
            {
                _cardsGrid.ResumeLayout(performLayout: true);
                _cardsScrollHost.PerformLayout();
            }
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // MODAL HOOKS
        // =====================================================================

        private async void OpenCreateModal()
        {
            using var form = FrmCampaignDetail.ForCreate(_controller);

            if (form.ShowDialog(FindForm()) == DialogResult.OK)
            {
                Toast.Show(this, "New campaign created.", ToastKind.Success);
                await ReloadAsync();
            }
        }

        private async void OpenDetailModal(CampaignResponseDto campaign)
        {
            using var form = FrmCampaignDetail.ForView(_controller, campaign);

            if (form.ShowDialog(FindForm()) == DialogResult.OK)
            {
                Toast.Show(this, $"Updated \"{campaign.PromoName}\".", ToastKind.Success);
                await ReloadAsync();
            }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/Controls/CartLineControl.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// One row in the Order box: "Drink Name (Size) +Mods … ₱SubTotal [- 2x +] ✕" — a compact
    /// inline quantity stepper instead of a data-grid row, matching a modern POS's order list
    /// rather than the boxed-table convention the rest of the app uses.
    ///
    /// Absolute-positioned children recomputed on every OnResize. Deliberately NOT Dock-based
    /// (a Dock.Fill name label at full row height wraps before AutoEllipsis kicks in) and NOT
    /// TableLayoutPanel (absolute columns get clipped when the host is narrower than expected).
    ///
    /// The ✕ is a Label, not a Button — WinForms FlatStyle buttons enforce a minimum width
    /// larger than the 28px we want, so a Button ✕ visually overlapped the + stepper button.
    /// Labels have no such floor.
    /// </summary>
    public class CartLineControl : Panel
    {
        public OrderItem Item { get; }

        public event EventHandler? QuantityIncreased;
        public event EventHandler? QuantityDecreased;
        public event EventHandler? RemoveClicked;

        private const int RowHeight = 44;
        private const int NameHeight = 22;
        private const int StepperButtonSize = 30;
        private const int QtyLabelWidth = 34;
        private const int StepperClusterWidth = StepperButtonSize + QtyLabelWidth + StepperButtonSize; // 94
        private const int GapBeforeRemove = 10;
        private const int RemoveWidth = 28;
        private const int PriceWidth = 76;
        private const int RightClusterWidth = StepperClusterWidth + GapBeforeRemove + RemoveWidth;     // 132

        private readonly Label _nameLabel = null!;
        private readonly Label _priceLabel = null!;
        private readonly Panel _rightCluster = null!;
        private readonly FlowLayoutPanel _stepper = null!;
        private readonly Label _removeLabel = null!;

        public CartLineControl(OrderItem item)
        {
            Item = item;

            Height = RowHeight;
            Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 3);
            BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary;

            var modifiersSuffix = item.OrderItemModifiers.Count == 0
                ? string.Empty
                : $" +{string.Join(",", item.OrderItemModifiers.Select(m => m.ProductModifierNameSnapshot))}";

            _nameLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = $"{item.Product?.ProductName ?? "—"} ({item.SizeNameSnapshot}){modifiersSuffix}",
                AutoSize = false,
                Height = NameHeight,
                AutoEllipsis = true,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
            };

            _priceLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = $"₱{item.SubTotal:0.00}",
                AutoSize = false,
                Width = PriceWidth,
                Height = RowHeight,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleRight,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
            };

            _rightCluster = new Panel
            {
                Width = RightClusterWidth,
                Height = RowHeight,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
            };

            _stepper = new FlowLayoutPanel
            {
                FlowDirection = FlowDirection.LeftToRight,
                WrapContents = false,
                Width = StepperClusterWidth,
                Height = RowHeight,
                Padding = new Padding(0, 7, 0, 7),
            };

            var minusBtn = MakeStepperButton("-");
            minusBtn.Click += (s, e) => QuantityDecreased?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);

            var qtyLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = $"{item.Quantity}x",
                Width = QtyLabelWidth,
                Height = StepperButtonSize,
                Margin = new Padding(0),            // ← same reason
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleCenter,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(10f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
            };

            var plusBtn = MakeStepperButton("+");
            plusBtn.Click += (s, e) => QuantityIncreased?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);

            _stepper.Controls.Add(minusBtn);
            _stepper.Controls.Add(qtyLabel);
            _stepper.Controls.Add(plusBtn);

            _removeLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "✕",
                Width = RemoveWidth,
                Height = RowHeight,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleCenter,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(12f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _removeLabel.Click += (s, e) => RemoveClicked?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);

            Controls.Add(_nameLabel);
            Controls.Add(_priceLabel);
            Controls.Add(_rightCluster);
            _rightCluster.Controls.Add(_stepper);
            _rightCluster.Controls.Add(_removeLabel);
        }

        protected override void OnResize(EventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnResize(e);
            LayoutChildren();
        }

        protected override void OnHandleCreated(EventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnHandleCreated(e);
            LayoutChildren();
        }

        private void LayoutChildren()
        {
            // OnResize fires from the base Panel constructor (via the Height assignment at the
            // top of our own constructor) BEFORE the child fields are assigned. Bail until
            // everything's wired up — real layout runs from OnHandleCreated and every resize.
            if (_nameLabel is null || _priceLabel is null || _rightCluster is null ||
                _stepper is null || _removeLabel is null)
                return;

            var width = ClientSize.Width;
            if (width <= 0)
                return;

            _rightCluster.Location = new Point(width - RightClusterWidth, 0);
            _removeLabel.Location = new Point(RightClusterWidth - RemoveWidth, 0);
            _stepper.Location = new Point(0, 0);
            _priceLabel.Location = new Point(_rightCluster.Left - PriceWidth, 0);

            var nameWidth = Math.Max(0, _priceLabel.Left - 4);
            _nameLabel.Location = new Point(0, (RowHeight - NameHeight) / 2);
            _nameLabel.Width = nameWidth;
        }

        private static Button MakeStepperButton(string text) => new()
        {
            Text = text,
            Width = StepperButtonSize,
            Height = StepperButtonSize,
            Margin = new Padding(0),            // ← FlowLayoutPanel default margin (3px each side) was
                                                // squeezing the trailing + button out of the 94px stepper
            FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
            BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
            ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
            Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(10f),
            Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
        };
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/Controls/CustomerManagementView.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Session;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// The Customers tab. Searchable grid + profile drawer. Point-adjustment UI is
    /// gated by EmployeeSession role — the API enforces the same rules server-side.
    /// </summary>
    public partial class CustomerManagementView : UserControl
    {
        private readonly CustomerController _controller;
        private readonly BindingSource _customersBindingSource = new();
        private PaginationControl pagination = null!;

        // Status filter dropdown — built in code, inserted into cardHeaderPanel below the
        // search row. Mirrors the Feedback status filter and the TransactionView category
        // dropdown. Default is "Active"; switching it changes the API query.
        private Panel _activeFilterRow = null!;
        private ComboBox _activeFilterCombo = null!;

        private const string ActiveFilterActiveOption = "Active";
        private const string ActiveFilterInactiveOption = "Inactive";
        private const string ActiveFilterAllOption = "All";

        // Additive drawer elements — built in code so the designer file stays untouched.
        // _statusValueLabel lives INSIDE profileBoxPanel (added to its Controls, not to
        // drawerFlow) so it reads as part of the profile block. _editCustomerBtn and
        // _customerEditPanel are inserted into drawerFlow right after profileBoxPanel.
        private Label _statusValueLabel = null!;
        private Button _editCustomerBtn = null!;
        private Button _deactivateCustomerBtn = null!;

        private Panel _customerEditPanel = null!;
        private TextBox _editFirstNameTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox _editLastNameTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox _editContactTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox _editEmailTxtBx = null!;
        private DateTimePicker _editBirthdayPicker = null!;
        private CheckBox _editHasBirthdayChk = null!;
        private Label _editErrorLabel = null!;
        private Button _editSaveBtn = null!;
        private Button _editCancelBtn = null!;
        private bool _isEditingCustomer;

        private CustomerResponseDto? _selectedCustomer;
        private PointAdjustmentResponseDto? _selectedRequest;

        public CustomerManagementView()
        {
            _controller = new CustomerController();
            InitializeComponent();

            ConfigureCustomersGridColumns();
            ConfigureOrderHistoryGridColumns();
            ConfigureLedgerGridColumns();
            ConfigureAdjustmentRequestsGridColumns();

            pagination.PageChanged += async (s, page) => await ReloadCustomersAsync(page);

            customersGrid.DataSource = _customersBindingSource;
            customersGrid.SelectionChanged += CustomersGrid_SelectionChanged;
            customersGrid.CellFormatting += CustomersGrid_CellFormatting;

            searchTxtBx.TextChanged += (s, e) => ReloadCustomers();
            newCustomerBtn.Click += (s, e) => OpenOnboardingModal();

            cardHeaderPanel.Resize += (s, e) => PositionActionButton();
            PositionActionButton();

            BuildActiveFilterRow();

            // Only Baristas register new customers. Managers act on existing ones
            // (edit, approve point adjustments) and don't originate records. The API
            // enforces this too — CreateCustomer is reachable by any authenticated
            // employee today, so this is a UI-level restriction only for now.
            newCustomerBtn.Visible =
                EmployeeSession.Current?.Employee?.Role?.Equals("Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true;

            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(submitRequestBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(approveBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(rejectBtn);

            adjustmentRequestsGrid.SelectionChanged += AdjustmentRequestsGrid_SelectionChanged;

            requestAmountNud.ValueChanged += (s, e) => UpdateSubmitRequestEnabled();
            requestReasonTxtBx.TextChanged += (s, e) => UpdateSubmitRequestEnabled();
            submitRequestBtn.Click += SubmitRequestBtn_Click;

            rejectionNotesTxtBx.TextChanged += (s, e) => UpdateApprovalControlsEnabled();
            approveBtn.Click += ApproveBtn_Click;
            rejectBtn.Click += RejectBtn_Click;

            Load += async (s, e) => await ReloadCustomersAsync();

            BuildDrawerAdditions();
        }

        // Barista files requests; nobody else does.
        private bool CanRequestAdjustment =>
            EmployeeSession.Current?.Employee?.Role?.Equals("Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true;

        // Manager decides. Not Admin, not SuperAdmin, not Barista.
        private bool CanApprove =>
            EmployeeSession.Current?.Employee?.Role?.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true;

        // Manager edits customer info inline. Barista cannot edit. Admin is not present in this view at all.

        private bool CanEditCustomer => CanApprove;
        private void PositionActionButton()
        {
            newCustomerBtn.Left = cardHeaderPanel.ClientSize.Width - newCustomerBtn.Width - 16;
            newCustomerBtn.Top = 14;
        }
        /// <summary>
        /// Builds the "Filter by Status:" row and inserts it into the card header, below
        /// the search box. Same shape as the Feedback status filter: label, then dropdown,
        /// positions computed from the label's measured width. Default is Active.
        ///
        /// The card header was 60px tall for search + button; this needs another ~40px
        /// below the search row, so the header grows.
        /// </summary>
        private void BuildActiveFilterRow()
        {
            // Grow the header so the new row fits below the search box.
            // Search box lives at y=16..44; filter row starts at y=52.
            cardHeaderPanel.Height = 92;

            _activeFilterRow = new Panel
            {
                Location = new Point(16, 52),
                Size = new Size(360, 30),
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
            };

            var label = new Label
            {
                Text = "Filter by Status:",
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            _activeFilterCombo = new ComboBox
            {
                Size = new Size(180, 26),
                DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
            };

            _activeFilterCombo.Items.Add(ActiveFilterActiveOption);
            _activeFilterCombo.Items.Add(ActiveFilterInactiveOption);
            _activeFilterCombo.Items.Add(ActiveFilterAllOption);

            // Default to Active — matches the API's default and keeps Baristas from ever
            // seeing deactivated customers.
            _activeFilterCombo.SelectedItem = ActiveFilterActiveOption;

            // Position: label first, combo after, measured so they can't collide.
            var labelWidth = TextRenderer.MeasureText(label.Text, label.Font).Width;
            label.Left = 0;
            label.Top = (30 - label.Height) / 2;

            _activeFilterCombo.Left = labelWidth + 12;
            _activeFilterCombo.Top = (30 - _activeFilterCombo.Height) / 2;

            _activeFilterCombo.SelectedIndexChanged += async (s, e) =>
            {
                // Filter change is always a fresh page-1 query — same as a search change.
                await ReloadCustomersAsync();
            };

            _activeFilterRow.Controls.Add(_activeFilterCombo);
            _activeFilterRow.Controls.Add(label);

            cardHeaderPanel.Controls.Add(_activeFilterRow);
            _activeFilterRow.BringToFront();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// The API value for the currently-selected filter:
        ///   "Active"   -> "active"
        ///   "Inactive" -> "inactive"
        ///   "All"      -> "all"
        /// Null (never happens with the fixed list, but defensive) -> "active".
        /// </summary>
        private string SelectedActiveFilter =>
            (_activeFilterCombo?.SelectedItem as string) switch
            {
                ActiveFilterInactiveOption => "inactive",
                ActiveFilterAllOption => "all",
                _ => "active",
            };

        // =====================================================================
        // DRAWER ADDITIONS (Status line + Edit Customer + inline edit panel)
        // =====================================================================

        /// <summary>
        /// Builds the three runtime-only drawer elements and places them:
        ///   - _statusValueLabel -> inside profileBoxPanel, as a fifth absolutely-positioned
        ///     line under contactLabel. Grows profileBoxPanel by 26px to fit it.
        ///   - _editCustomerBtn  -> inserted into drawerFlow at index 1 (right after
        ///     profileBoxPanel, before the "Order History" header).
        ///   - _customerEditPanel -> inserted into drawerFlow at index 2 (right below the
        ///     edit button). Hidden by default.
        ///
        /// Deliberately does NOT touch the designer file. Everything here is additive.
        /// </summary>
        private void BuildDrawerAdditions()
        {
            // ---- Status line, inside the profile box ----
            profileBoxPanel.Height += 26;   // make room for the fifth line

            _statusValueLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(14, 110),          // just below contactLabel at y=86
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
                Margin = new Padding(0),
            };
            profileBoxPanel.Controls.Add(_statusValueLabel);

            // ---- Edit Customer button, inserted right after the profile box ----
            _editCustomerBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Edit Customer",
                Size = new Size(340, 34),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
                Visible = false,
                Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 12),
            };
            _editCustomerBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _editCustomerBtn.Click += (s, e) => EnterCustomerEditState();

            // ---- Deactivate Customer button, stacked directly below Edit ----
            _deactivateCustomerBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Deactivate Customer",
                Size = new Size(340, 34),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
                Visible = false,
                Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 12),
            };
            _deactivateCustomerBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _deactivateCustomerBtn.Click += DeactivateCustomerBtn_Click;

            // ---- Inline edit panel, inserted below the two buttons ----
            _customerEditPanel = BuildCustomerEditPanel();
            _customerEditPanel.Visible = false;

            // Insert into drawerFlow. profileBoxPanel is index 0; we want
            //   [profileBoxPanel, editCustomerBtn, deactivateCustomerBtn, customerEditPanel, orderHistoryHeader, ...]
            drawerFlow.Controls.Add(_editCustomerBtn);
            drawerFlow.Controls.Add(_deactivateCustomerBtn);
            drawerFlow.Controls.Add(_customerEditPanel);
            drawerFlow.Controls.SetChildIndex(_editCustomerBtn, 1);
            drawerFlow.Controls.SetChildIndex(_deactivateCustomerBtn, 2);
            drawerFlow.Controls.SetChildIndex(_customerEditPanel, 3);
        }

        private Panel BuildCustomerEditPanel()
        {
            const int rowHeight = 56;
            const int labelHeight = 18;
            const int fieldHeight = 28;
            const int fieldWidth = 300;

            var panel = new Panel
            {
                Width = 340,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceCard,
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
                Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 16),
            };

            int y = 14;   // inset from the panel's border

            void AddLabel(string text)
            {
                var label = new Label
                {
                    Text = text,
                    Location = new Point(14, y),
                    AutoSize = true,
                    Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                    ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                    BackColor = Color.Transparent,
                };
                panel.Controls.Add(label);
            }

            TextBox MakeRow(string labelText, bool digitsOnly = false)
            {
                AddLabel(labelText);
                var txt = new TextBox
                {
                    Location = new Point(14, y + labelHeight),
                    Size = new Size(fieldWidth, fieldHeight),
                    Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                    BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
                };
                if (digitsOnly)
                {
                    txt.KeyPress += (s, e) =>
                    {
                        if (!char.IsControl(e.KeyChar) && !char.IsDigit(e.KeyChar))
                            e.Handled = true;
                    };
                }
                panel.Controls.Add(txt);
                y += rowHeight;
                return txt;
            }

            _editFirstNameTxtBx = MakeRow("First name *");
            _editLastNameTxtBx = MakeRow("Last name *");
            _editContactTxtBx = MakeRow("Contact number * (10-11 digits)", digitsOnly: true);
            _editEmailTxtBx = MakeRow("Email (optional)");

            // Birthday row — label, then checkbox on its own line, then the picker below it.
            // Each element gets its own y step so nothing overlaps.
            AddLabel("Birthday");
            y += labelHeight + 4;                       // label baseline + small gap

            _editHasBirthdayChk = new CheckBox
            {
                Text = "Set birthday",
                Location = new Point(14, y),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };
            panel.Controls.Add(_editHasBirthdayChk);
            y += 26;                                    // checkbox row height

            _editBirthdayPicker = new DateTimePicker
            {
                Location = new Point(14, y),
                Size = new Size(fieldWidth, fieldHeight),
                Format = DateTimePickerFormat.Short,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Enabled = false,
            };
            _editHasBirthdayChk.CheckedChanged += (s, e) =>
                _editBirthdayPicker.Enabled = _editHasBirthdayChk.Checked;
            panel.Controls.Add(_editBirthdayPicker);
            y += fieldHeight + 12;                      // picker height + gap before the error label

            _editErrorLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(14, y),
                Size = new Size(fieldWidth, 24),
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };
            panel.Controls.Add(_editErrorLabel);
            y += 28;

            _editSaveBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Save Changes",
                Location = new Point(14, y),
                Size = new Size(150, 32),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _editSaveBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _editSaveBtn.Click += CustomerEditSaveBtn_Click;

            _editCancelBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Cancel",
                Location = new Point(172, y),
                Size = new Size(150, 32),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _editCancelBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _editCancelBtn.Click += (s, e) => ExitCustomerEditState();

            panel.Controls.Add(_editSaveBtn);
            panel.Controls.Add(_editCancelBtn);

            panel.Height = y + 32 + 14;   // + button height + bottom inset
            return panel;
        }

        private void EnterCustomerEditState()
        {
            if (_selectedCustomer is null) return;

            _editFirstNameTxtBx.Text = _selectedCustomer.FirstName;
            _editLastNameTxtBx.Text = _selectedCustomer.LastName;
            _editContactTxtBx.Text = _selectedCustomer.ContactNumber;
            _editEmailTxtBx.Text = _selectedCustomer.Email ?? string.Empty;

            if (_selectedCustomer.Birthday.HasValue)
            {
                _editHasBirthdayChk.Checked = true;
                _editBirthdayPicker.Value = _selectedCustomer.Birthday.Value;
                _editBirthdayPicker.Enabled = true;
            }
            else
            {
                _editHasBirthdayChk.Checked = false;
                _editBirthdayPicker.Enabled = false;
            }

            _editErrorLabel.Text = string.Empty;
            _editCustomerBtn.Visible = false;
            _deactivateCustomerBtn.Visible = false;
            _customerEditPanel.Visible = true;
            _isEditingCustomer = true;
        }

        private void ExitCustomerEditState()
        {
            _customerEditPanel.Visible = false;
            _editCustomerBtn.Visible = CanEditCustomer;
            _deactivateCustomerBtn.Visible = CanEditCustomer;
            _isEditingCustomer = false;
        }

        private async void DeactivateCustomerBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedCustomer is null) return;

            var customer = _selectedCustomer;
            var reactivating = !customer.IsActive;

            var confirmMessage = reactivating
                ? $"Reactivate {customer.FullName}? They will appear in the list."
                : $"Deactivate {customer.FullName}? They will no longer appear in the list, ";

            var confirmTitle = reactivating ? "Reactivate Customer" : "Deactivate Customer";

            var confirm = MessageBox.Show(
                FindForm(),
                confirmMessage,
                confirmTitle,
                MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                MessageBoxIcon.Question);

            if (confirm != DialogResult.Yes) return;

            try
            {
                _deactivateCustomerBtn.Enabled = false;

                if (reactivating)
                {
                    await _controller.ReactivateCustomerAsync(customer.CustomerId);
                    Toast.Show(this, $"Reactivated {customer.FullName}.", ToastKind.Success);
                }
                else
                {
                    await _controller.DeactivateCustomerAsync(customer.CustomerId);
                    Toast.Show(this, $"Deactivated {customer.FullName}.", ToastKind.Success);
                }

                // Reload. If we're in the Active filter and just deactivated, the customer
                // drops out of the grid. If we're in the Inactive filter and just reactivated,
                // the same. If we're in All, the row stays but its state flips.
                await ReloadCustomersAsync();

                // Try to keep the customer selected if they're still visible under the
                // current filter. If they filtered themselves out, selection clears and the
                // drawer reverts to the empty state.
                SelectCustomerRowById(customer.CustomerId);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, "Operation failed");
            }
            finally
            {
                _deactivateCustomerBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }


        private async void CustomerEditSaveBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedCustomer is null) return;

            _editErrorLabel.Text = string.Empty;

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_editFirstNameTxtBx.Text) ||
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_editLastNameTxtBx.Text))
            {
                _editErrorLabel.Text = "First and last name are required.";
                return;
            }

            var cleanedPhone = new string(_editContactTxtBx.Text.Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
            if (cleanedPhone.Length < 10 || cleanedPhone.Length > 11)
            {
                _editErrorLabel.Text = "Contact number must be 10-11 digits.";
                return;
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_editEmailTxtBx.Text) &&
                (!_editEmailTxtBx.Text.Contains('@') || !_editEmailTxtBx.Text.Contains('.')))
            {
                _editErrorLabel.Text = "Invalid email format.";
                return;
            }

            try
            {
                _editSaveBtn.Enabled = false;

                var birthday = _editHasBirthdayChk.Checked
                    ? _editBirthdayPicker.Value.Date
                    : (DateTime?)null;

                await _controller.UpdateCustomerAsync(
                    _selectedCustomer.CustomerId,
                    _editFirstNameTxtBx.Text,
                    _editLastNameTxtBx.Text,
                    _editContactTxtBx.Text,
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_editEmailTxtBx.Text) ? null : _editEmailTxtBx.Text,
                    birthday);

                ExitCustomerEditState();

                Toast.Show(this, $"Saved changes to {_selectedCustomer.FullName}.", ToastKind.Success);

                var affectedCustomerId = _selectedCustomer.CustomerId;   // local
                await ReloadCustomersAsync();
                SelectCustomerRowById(affectedCustomerId);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _editErrorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                _editSaveBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }

        private async Task ReloadCustomersAsync(int? page = null)
        {
            var targetPage = page ?? 1;

            if (page is null)
                pagination.ResetToFirstPage();

            PagedResponse<CustomerResponseDto> response;
            try
            {
                response = await _controller.SearchAsync(
                    searchTxtBx.Text, targetPage, 25, SelectedActiveFilter);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, "Error loading customers");
                return;
            }

            _customersBindingSource.DataSource = response.Items;

            pagination.UpdatePageInfo(response.TotalCount, response.Page, response.PageSize);

            customersGrid.ClearSelection();
            try { customersGrid.CurrentCell = null; } catch { /* not always allowed; ignore */ }

            // Page changes (and search changes) reset the drawer.
            _selectedCustomer = null;
            await RefreshDrawerAsync();
        }

        private async void ReloadCustomers()
        {
            try { await ReloadCustomersAsync(); }
            catch (Exception ex) { MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, "Error"); }
        }
        /// <summary>
        /// Mutes rows for inactive customers with a neutral grey treatment (not brand-tinted)
        /// so they visibly read as "off" against the white grid. Same treatment as inactive
        /// employees in Manage Users.
        /// </summary>
        private void CustomersGrid_CellFormatting(object? sender, DataGridViewCellFormattingEventArgs e)
        {
            if (customersGrid.Rows[e.RowIndex].DataBoundItem is not CustomerResponseDto customer)
                return;

            if (!customer.IsActive)
            {
                var greyText = Color.FromArgb(255, 130, 130, 130);
                var greyBackground = Color.FromArgb(255, 240, 240, 240);

                e.CellStyle.ForeColor = greyText;
                e.CellStyle.BackColor = greyBackground;
                e.CellStyle.SelectionForeColor = greyText;
                e.CellStyle.SelectionBackColor = greyBackground;
            }
        }

        private async void CustomersGrid_SelectionChanged(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _selectedCustomer = customersGrid.CurrentRow?.DataBoundItem as CustomerResponseDto;
            await RefreshDrawerAsync();
        }

        private void AdjustmentRequestsGrid_SelectionChanged(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _selectedRequest = adjustmentRequestsGrid.CurrentRow?.DataBoundItem as PointAdjustmentResponseDto;
            UpdateApprovalControlsEnabled();
        }

        private void UpdateSubmitRequestEnabled()
        {
            submitRequestBtn.Enabled = CanRequestAdjustment &&
                requestAmountNud.Value != 0 &&
                !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(requestReasonTxtBx.Text);
        }

        private void UpdateApprovalControlsEnabled()
        {
            approvalHintLabel.Text = _selectedRequest is { Status: "Pending" }
                ? $"Selected: {(_selectedRequest.PointsChange > 0 ? "+" : string.Empty)}{_selectedRequest.PointsChange} pts — {_selectedRequest.Reason}"
                : "Select a pending request above.";

            var canActOnSelection = CanApprove &&
                _selectedRequest is { Status: "Pending" } &&
                _selectedRequest.RequestedBy != EmployeeSession.Current?.EmployeeId;

            approveBtn.Enabled = canActOnSelection;
            rejectBtn.Enabled = canActOnSelection && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(rejectionNotesTxtBx.Text);
        }

        private async void SubmitRequestBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedCustomer is null) return;

            try
            {
                await _controller.RequestAdjustmentAsync(
                    _selectedCustomer.CustomerId, (int)requestAmountNud.Value,
                    requestReasonTxtBx.Text, EmployeeSession.Current!.EmployeeId);

                requestStatusLabel.Text = "Submitted — waiting on admin approval.";
                requestAmountNud.Value = 0;
                requestReasonTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;
                await RefreshDrawerAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                requestStatusLabel.Text = ex.Message;
            }
        }

        private async void ApproveBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedRequest is null || _selectedCustomer is null) return;

            try
            {
                await _controller.ApproveAdjustmentAsync(
                    _selectedRequest.PointAdjustmentRequestId,
                    EmployeeSession.Current!.EmployeeId);

                approvalStatusLabel.Text = "Approved — points applied.";
                rejectionNotesTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;

                var affectedCustomerId = _selectedCustomer.CustomerId;
                var approvedPoints = _selectedRequest.PointsChange;
                var approvedCustomerName = _selectedCustomer.FullName;

                await ReloadCustomersAsync();
                SelectCustomerRowById(affectedCustomerId);

                var sign = approvedPoints > 0 ? "+" : string.Empty;
                Toast.Show(this, $"Approved {sign}{approvedPoints} pts for {approvedCustomerName}.", ToastKind.Success);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                approvalStatusLabel.Text = ex.Message;
            }
        }

        private async void RejectBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedRequest is null) return;

            try
            {
