                await _controller.RejectAdjustmentAsync(
                    _selectedRequest.PointAdjustmentRequestId,
                    EmployeeSession.Current!.EmployeeId,
                    rejectionNotesTxtBx.Text);

                approvalStatusLabel.Text = "Rejected.";
                rejectionNotesTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;

                var rejectedPoints = _selectedRequest.PointsChange;
                var rejectedCustomerName = _selectedCustomer?.FullName ?? "customer";

                await RefreshDrawerAsync();

                var sign = rejectedPoints > 0 ? "+" : string.Empty;
                Toast.Show(this, $"Rejected {sign}{rejectedPoints} pts for {rejectedCustomerName}.", ToastKind.Info);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                approvalStatusLabel.Text = ex.Message;
            }
        }

        private async void OpenOnboardingModal()
        {
            using var form = new FrmCustomerOnboarding(_controller);

            if (form.ShowDialog(FindForm()) == DialogResult.OK && form.CreatedCustomer != null)
            {
                await ReloadCustomersAsync();
                SelectCustomerRowById(form.CreatedCustomer.CustomerId);
            }
        }

        private async Task RefreshDrawerAsync()
        {
            var customer = _selectedCustomer;
            if (customer is null)
            {
                // Clear any lingering edit state so the next selection starts fresh.
                if (_isEditingCustomer)
                    ExitCustomerEditState();

                drawerEmptyLabel.Visible = true;
                drawerContentPanel.Visible = false;
                return;
            }

            drawerEmptyLabel.Visible = false;
            drawerContentPanel.Visible = true;

            // When the customer is inactive, the drawer becomes read-only: profile and
            // history remain visible, but every interactive element is hidden except the
            // Reactivate button. Matches how Manage Users locks down a deactivated employee.
            var customerIsActive = customer.IsActive;

            requestSectionPanel.Visible = CanRequestAdjustment && customerIsActive;
            approvalSectionPanel.Visible = CanApprove && customerIsActive;

            // If the user was mid-edit and the drawer is being repopulated (row change,
            // page change, save), abandon the edit silently rather than carrying stale
            // text into a different customer.
            if (_isEditingCustomer)
                ExitCustomerEditState();

            // Edit is only available on an active customer.
            _editCustomerBtn.Visible = CanEditCustomer && customerIsActive;

            // Deactivate/Reactivate stays visible for Managers regardless of state —
            // for an inactive customer it's the one actionable button.
            _deactivateCustomerBtn.Visible = CanEditCustomer;

            _deactivateCustomerBtn.Text = customerIsActive
                ? "Deactivate Customer"
                : "Reactivate Customer";
            _deactivateCustomerBtn.ForeColor = customerIsActive
                ? BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill
                : BrandPalette.NeutralText;

            customerNameLabel.Text = customer.FullName;
            tierBadgeLabel.Text = $"{customer.Tier} Tier";
            pointsBalanceLabel.Text = $"{customer.PointsBalance:N0} pts";
            contactLabel.Text = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(customer.Email)
                ? customer.ContactNumber
                : $"{customer.ContactNumber}   •   {customer.Email}";

            // Status line — plain text, computed from the 14-day threshold in ActivityStatus.
            _statusValueLabel.Text =
                $"Status: {ActivityStatus.ToDisplayString(customer.LastVisitDate)}   •   " +
                $"Last visit: {ActivityStatus.FormatRelative(customer.LastVisitDate)}";

            try
            {
                var orders = await _controller.GetOrderHistoryAsync(customer.CustomerId);
                orderHistoryGrid.DataSource = null;
                orderHistoryGrid.DataSource = orders;

                var ledger = await _controller.GetLedgerAsync(customer.CustomerId);
                ledgerGrid.DataSource = null;
                ledgerGrid.DataSource = ledger;

                var pending = await _controller.GetPendingAdjustmentsAsync(customer.CustomerId);

                // Baristas see only their own pending requests — they filed them, and
                // they can't act on anyone else's. Managers see every pending request for
                // the customer because they're the ones deciding.
                if (EmployeeSession.Current?.Employee?.Role?.Equals("Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true)
                {
                    var me = EmployeeSession.Current?.EmployeeId;
                    pending = pending.Where(r => r.RequestedBy == me).ToList();
                }

                _selectedRequest = null;
                adjustmentRequestsGrid.DataSource = null;
                adjustmentRequestsGrid.DataSource = pending;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, "Error loading drawer");
            }

            requestAmountNud.Value = 0;
            requestReasonTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;
            requestStatusLabel.Text = string.Empty;
            UpdateSubmitRequestEnabled();

            rejectionNotesTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;
            approvalStatusLabel.Text = string.Empty;
            approvalHintLabel.Text = "Select a pending request above.";
            approveBtn.Enabled = false;
            rejectBtn.Enabled = false;
        }

        private void SelectCustomerRowById(int customerId)
        {
            foreach (DataGridViewRow row in customersGrid.Rows)
            {
                if (row.DataBoundItem is CustomerResponseDto c && c.CustomerId == customerId)
                {
                    customersGrid.ClearSelection();
                    row.Selected = true;
                    customersGrid.CurrentCell = row.Cells[0];
                    _selectedCustomer = c;
                    _ = RefreshDrawerAsync();
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        private void ConfigureCustomersGridColumns()
        {
            customersGrid.Columns.Clear();
            customersGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.CustomerId),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.CustomerId),
                HeaderText = "ID",
                Width = 60,
            });
            customersGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.FullName),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.FullName),
                HeaderText = "Name",
                Width = 180,
                AutoSizeMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnMode.Fill,
            });
            customersGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.ContactNumber),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.ContactNumber),
                HeaderText = "Contact",
                Width = 120,
            });
            customersGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.PointsBalance),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.PointsBalance),
                HeaderText = "Points",
                Width = 80,
            });
            customersGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.Tier),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.Tier),
                HeaderText = "Tier",
                Width = 90,
            });
            customersGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.LastVisitDate),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.LastVisitDate),
                HeaderText = "Last Visit",
                Width = 110,
                DefaultCellStyle = new DataGridViewCellStyle { Format = "yyyy-MM-dd" },
            });
        }

        private void ConfigureOrderHistoryGridColumns()
        {

            orderHistoryGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(OrderHistoryDto.OrderId),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(OrderHistoryDto.OrderId),
                HeaderText = "Order #",
                Width = 60,
            });
            orderHistoryGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(OrderHistoryDto.OrderDate),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(OrderHistoryDto.OrderDate),
                HeaderText = "Date",
                Width = 90,
                DefaultCellStyle = new DataGridViewCellStyle { Format = "yyyy-MM-dd" },
            });
            orderHistoryGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(OrderHistoryDto.TotalAmount),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(OrderHistoryDto.TotalAmount),
                HeaderText = "Total",
                Width = 90,
                AutoSizeMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnMode.Fill,
                DefaultCellStyle = new DataGridViewCellStyle { Format = "₱#,##0.00", Alignment = DataGridViewContentAlignment.MiddleRight },
            });
        }

        private void ConfigureLedgerGridColumns()
        {
            ledgerGrid.Columns.Clear();

            {
                Name = nameof(PointLedgerResponseDto.Timestamp),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(PointLedgerResponseDto.Timestamp),
                HeaderText = "Date",
                Width = 90,
                DefaultCellStyle = new DataGridViewCellStyle { Format = "yyyy-MM-dd" },
            });
            ledgerGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(PointLedgerResponseDto.Type),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(PointLedgerResponseDto.Type),
                HeaderText = "Type",
                Width = 80,
            });
            ledgerGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(PointLedgerResponseDto.PointsChange),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(PointLedgerResponseDto.PointsChange),
                HeaderText = "Pts",
                Width = 60,
            });
            ledgerGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(PointLedgerResponseDto.Reason),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(PointLedgerResponseDto.Reason),
                HeaderText = "Reason",
                Width = 100,
                AutoSizeMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnMode.Fill,
            });
        }

        private void ConfigureAdjustmentRequestsGridColumns()
        {
            adjustmentRequestsGrid.Columns.Clear();
            adjustmentRequestsGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(PointAdjustmentResponseDto.RequestedAt),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(PointAdjustmentResponseDto.RequestedAt),
                HeaderText = "Date",
                Width = 110,
                DefaultCellStyle = new DataGridViewCellStyle { Format = "yyyy-MM-dd" },
            });
            adjustmentRequestsGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(PointAdjustmentResponseDto.PointsChange),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(PointAdjustmentResponseDto.PointsChange),
                HeaderText = "Pts",
                Width = 70,
            });
            adjustmentRequestsGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(PointAdjustmentResponseDto.Status),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(PointAdjustmentResponseDto.Status),
                HeaderText = "Status",
                Width = 90,
                AutoSizeMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnMode.Fill,
            });
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/Controls/FeedbackManagementView.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Session;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// The Feedback tab: ticket grid + read-only drawer. Staff log and view; deciding
    /// (resolve/dismiss/award) is a manager concern on a future surface.
    ///
    /// Staff can also CANCEL their own pending tickets (soft-delete, only the employee
    /// who logged it). Cancelling hides the ticket from the grid.
    ///
    /// API-backed: all ticket data comes from the API. No mock.
    /// </summary>
    public partial class FeedbackManagementView : UserControl
    {
        private readonly FeedbackController _controller;
        private readonly BindingSource _ticketsBindingSource = new();

        private FeedbackResponseDto? _selectedTicket;
        private Button _cancelTicketBtn = null!;

        // Status filter dropdown — built in code, inserted into cardHeaderPanel below the
        // existing search row. Mirrors the TransactionView "Sort by Category" pattern.
        private Panel _statusFilterRow = null!;
        private ComboBox _statusFilterCombo = null!;

        private const string StatusAllOption = "All Statuses";
        private static readonly string[] StatusOptions = { "Pending", "Resolved", "Cancelled" };

        private Panel _decisionPanel = null!;
        private NumericUpDown _bonusPointsNud = null!;
        private TextBox _decisionNotesTxtBx = null!;
        private Button _resolveBtn = null!;
        private Label _decisionErrorLabel = null!;

        private const int MaxFeedbackBonusPoints = 50;

        public FeedbackManagementView()
        {
            _controller = new FeedbackController();
            InitializeComponent();

            ConfigureTicketsGridColumns();

            pagination.PageChanged += async (s, page) => await ReloadTicketsAsync(page);

            ticketsGrid.DataSource = _ticketsBindingSource;
            ticketsGrid.SelectionChanged += TicketsGrid_SelectionChanged;

            searchTxtBx.TextChanged += (s, e) => ReloadTickets();
            logFeedbackBtn.Click += (s, e) => OpenLogFeedbackModal();

            cardHeaderPanel.Resize += (s, e) => PositionActionButton();
            PositionActionButton();

            BuildDecisionPanel();
            BuildCancelButton();
            BuildStatusFilterRow();

            // Only Baristas log feedback. Managers decide on it. The API enforces this
            // server-side too (FeedbackController.LogFeedback rejects non-Baristas).
            logFeedbackBtn.Visible =
                EmployeeSession.Current?.Employee?.Role?.Equals("Barista", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true;

            Load += async (s, e) => await ReloadTicketsAsync();
        }
        /// <summary>
        /// Builds the Manager-only decision panel and inserts it into drawerFlow. Visible
        /// only when the current employee is a Manager AND the selected ticket is Pending;
        /// RefreshDrawer toggles visibility.
        ///
        /// One button — "Resolve Ticket". BonusPoints == 0 means "resolved, no gesture"
        /// (the dismiss case); > 0 awards that many points to the customer. Notes optional.
        /// </summary>
        private void BuildDecisionPanel()
        {
            _decisionPanel = new Panel
            {
                Size = new Size(420, 200),
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceCard,
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
                Visible = false,
                Margin = new Padding(0, 16, 0, 16),
            };

            const int inset = 14;

            var header = new Label
            {
                Text = "Resolve Ticket",
                Location = new Point(inset, 12),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            var hint = new Label
            {
                Text = "0 points = resolved without a gesture. Up to 50 for a recovery.",
                Location = new Point(inset, 34),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            var pointsLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Bonus points",
                Location = new Point(inset, 60),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            _bonusPointsNud = new NumericUpDown
            {
                Location = new Point(inset, 80),
                Size = new Size(100, 26),
                Minimum = 0,
                Maximum = MaxFeedbackBonusPoints,
                Value = 0,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                TextAlign = HorizontalAlignment.Center,
            };

            _decisionNotesTxtBx = new TextBox
            {
                Location = new Point(inset, 112),
                Size = new Size(392, 26),
                PlaceholderText = "Notes (optional)",
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
            };

            _resolveBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Resolve Ticket",
                Location = new Point(inset, 146),
                Size = new Size(392, 32),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _resolveBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _resolveBtn.Click += ResolveBtn_Click;

            _decisionErrorLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(inset, 182),
                Size = new Size(392, 20),
                AutoSize = false,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
                Visible = false,
            };

            _decisionPanel.Controls.Add(_decisionErrorLabel);
            _decisionPanel.Controls.Add(_resolveBtn);
            _decisionPanel.Controls.Add(_decisionNotesTxtBx);
            _decisionPanel.Controls.Add(_bonusPointsNud);
            _decisionPanel.Controls.Add(pointsLabel);
            _decisionPanel.Controls.Add(hint);
            _decisionPanel.Controls.Add(header);

            drawerFlow.Controls.Add(_decisionPanel);
        }


        /// <summary>
        /// Adds the "Cancel Ticket" button to the drawer's bottom. Built here in code
        /// rather than the Designer so the drawer layout doesn't need to be touched.
        /// </summary>
        private void BuildCancelButton()
        {
            _cancelTicketBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Cancel Ticket",
                Width = 420,               // matches the width of the review box in the flow
                Height = 36,
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
                Visible = false,
                Margin = new Padding(0, 16, 0, 0),   // gap above the button
            };
            _cancelTicketBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _cancelTicketBtn.Click += CancelTicketBtn_Click;

            // Add to the drawer's flow panel so it appears right after the review content,
            // near the ticket details — not pinned to the bottom of the whole drawer.
            drawerFlow.Controls.Add(_cancelTicketBtn);
        }

        private void PositionActionButton()
        {
            logFeedbackBtn.Left = cardHeaderPanel.ClientSize.Width - logFeedbackBtn.Width - 16;
            logFeedbackBtn.Top = 14;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Builds the status filter row and inserts it into the card header, below the
        /// existing search box. Mirrors the TransactionView "Sort by Category" pattern:
        /// a small label, then a dropdown. The card header was 60px tall for search + button;
        /// this needs another ~40px below the search row, so we grow the header.
        ///
        /// Changing the selection re-queries the API with the new status filter applied.
        /// Default selection is "Pending" — resolved tickets are hidden until the user
        /// explicitly picks another option.
        /// </summary>
        private void BuildStatusFilterRow()
        {
            // Grow the header so the new row has space below the search box.
            // Search box lives at y=16..44; filter row starts at y=52.
            cardHeaderPanel.Height = 92;

            _statusFilterRow = new Panel
            {
                Location = new Point(16, 52),
                Size = new Size(360, 30),
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
            };

            var label = new Label
            {
                Text = "Filter by Status:",
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            _statusFilterCombo = new ComboBox
            {
                Size = new Size(180, 26),
                DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
            };

            // "All Statuses" first, then the explicit statuses. Same shape as the
            // TransactionView category dropdown.
            _statusFilterCombo.Items.Add(StatusAllOption);
            foreach (var s in StatusOptions)
                _statusFilterCombo.Items.Add(s);

            // Default to Pending — the pending queue is the Manager's working set.
            _statusFilterCombo.SelectedItem = "Pending";

            // Position: label first, combo after, computed from the label's measured width
            // so they can't collide at any DPI.
            var labelWidth = TextRenderer.MeasureText(label.Text, label.Font).Width;
            label.Left = 0;
            label.Top = (30 - label.Height) / 2;

            _statusFilterCombo.Left = labelWidth + 12;
            _statusFilterCombo.Top = (30 - _statusFilterCombo.Height) / 2;

            _statusFilterCombo.SelectedIndexChanged += async (s, e) =>
            {
                // A filter change is always a fresh page-1 query — same as a search change.
                await ReloadTicketsAsync();
            };

            _statusFilterRow.Controls.Add(_statusFilterCombo);
            _statusFilterRow.Controls.Add(label);

            cardHeaderPanel.Controls.Add(_statusFilterRow);
            _statusFilterRow.BringToFront();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// The currently-selected status filter, or null when "All Statuses" is picked.
        /// Null means "no status filter" — the API returns every status.
        /// </summary>
        private string? SelectedStatusFilter =>
            _statusFilterCombo?.SelectedItem is string s && s != StatusAllOption
                ? s
                : null;

        private async Task ReloadTicketsAsync(int? page = null)
        {
            var targetPage = page ?? 1;

            // A null page (search change or initial load) always resets to page 1.
            if (page is null)
                pagination.ResetToFirstPage();

            PagedResponse<FeedbackResponseDto> response;
            try
            {
                response = await _controller.GetTicketsAsync(
                    searchTxtBx.Text, targetPage, 25, SelectedStatusFilter);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, "Error loading tickets");
                return;
            }

            _ticketsBindingSource.DataSource = response.Items;

            pagination.UpdatePageInfo(response.TotalCount, response.Page, response.PageSize);

            ticketsGrid.ClearSelection();
            try { ticketsGrid.CurrentCell = null; } catch { /* not always allowed; ignore */ }

            // Page changes (and search changes) reset the drawer.
            _selectedTicket = null;
            RefreshDrawer();
        }

        private async void ReloadTickets()
        {
            try { await ReloadTicketsAsync(); }
            catch (Exception ex) { MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, "Error loading tickets"); }
        }

        private void TicketsGrid_SelectionChanged(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _selectedTicket = ticketsGrid.CurrentRow?.DataBoundItem as FeedbackResponseDto;
            RefreshDrawer();
        }

        private async void OpenLogFeedbackModal()
        {
            using var form = new FrmLogFeedback(_controller, EmployeeSession.Current!.EmployeeId);

            if (form.ShowDialog(FindForm()) == DialogResult.OK && form.CreatedFeedback != null)
            {
                await ReloadTicketsAsync();
                SelectTicketRowById(form.CreatedFeedback.TicketId);
            }
        }

        private void RefreshDrawer()
        {
            var ticket = _selectedTicket;
            if (ticket is null)
            {
                drawerEmptyLabel.Visible = true;
                drawerContentPanel.Visible = false;
                _cancelTicketBtn.Visible = false;
                _decisionPanel.Visible = false;
                return;
            }

            drawerEmptyLabel.Visible = false;
            drawerContentPanel.Visible = true;

            ticketCustomerLabel.Text = ticket.CustomerName;
            ticketRatingLabel.Text = new string('★', ticket.Rating) + new string('☆', 5 - ticket.Rating);
            ticketCategoryLabel.Text = $"Category: {ticket.Category}";
            ticketStatusLabel.Text = $"Status: {ticket.Status}";
            ticketDateLabel.Text = $"Logged {ticket.DateLogged:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm} by {ticket.LoggedByName}";
            ticketNotesLabel.Text = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(ticket.StaffNotes) ? "(no notes)" : ticket.StaffNotes;

            // Cancel button is visible only when:
            //   - ticket status is Pending (not already resolved/dismissed/cancelled)
            //   - the currently logged-in employee is the one who logged the ticket
            var currentEmployeeId = EmployeeSession.Current?.EmployeeId;
            _cancelTicketBtn.Visible =
                ticket.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                ticket.LoggedBy == currentEmployeeId;

            // Decision panel is Manager-only and only for Pending tickets.
            var isManager = EmployeeSession.Current?.Employee?.Role?
                .Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true;

            _decisionPanel.Visible =
                isManager &&
                ticket.Status.Equals("Pending", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            // Reset panel state on every drawer refresh.
            _bonusPointsNud.Value = 0;
            _decisionNotesTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;
            _decisionErrorLabel.Text = string.Empty;
            _decisionErrorLabel.Visible = false;
        }
        private async void ResolveBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedTicket is null) return;

            _decisionErrorLabel.Text = string.Empty;
            _decisionErrorLabel.Visible = false;

            var bonus = (int)_bonusPointsNud.Value;
            var notes = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_decisionNotesTxtBx.Text)
                ? null
                : _decisionNotesTxtBx.Text.Trim();

            try
            {
                _resolveBtn.Enabled = false;

                var employeeId = EmployeeSession.Current?.EmployeeId
                    ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No employee session.");

                var ticketId = _selectedTicket.TicketId;
                var customerName = _selectedTicket.CustomerName;

                await _controller.ResolveAsync(ticketId, employeeId, bonus, notes);

                // Compose the toast message based on whether a gesture was awarded.
                var message = bonus > 0
                    ? $"Resolved ticket #{ticketId} for {customerName} — +{bonus} pts."
                    : $"Resolved ticket #{ticketId} for {customerName}.";

                Toast.Show(this, message, ToastKind.Success);

                await ReloadTicketsAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _decisionErrorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
                _decisionErrorLabel.Visible = true;
            }
            finally
            {
                _resolveBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }

        private async void CancelTicketBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedTicket is null) return;

            var confirm = MessageBox.Show(
                FindForm(),
                $"Are you sure you want to cancel ticket #{_selectedTicket.TicketId}?",
                "Cancel Ticket",
                MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                MessageBoxIcon.Warning);

            if (confirm != DialogResult.Yes)
                return;

            try
            {
                var employeeId = EmployeeSession.Current?.EmployeeId
                    ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No employee session.");

                var ticketId = _selectedTicket.TicketId;
                await _controller.CancelTicketAsync(ticketId, employeeId);

                MessageBox.Show(FindForm(),
                    $"Ticket #{ticketId} has been cancelled.",
                    "Ticket Cancelled",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                    MessageBoxIcon.Information);

                await ReloadTicketsAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, "Cancel failed");
            }
        }

        private void SelectTicketRowById(int ticketId)
        {
            foreach (DataGridViewRow row in ticketsGrid.Rows)
            {
                if (row.DataBoundItem is FeedbackResponseDto t && t.TicketId == ticketId)
                {
                    ticketsGrid.ClearSelection();
                    row.Selected = true;
                    ticketsGrid.CurrentCell = row.Cells[0];
                    _selectedTicket = t;
                    RefreshDrawer();
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        private void ConfigureTicketsGridColumns()
        {
            ticketsGrid.Columns.Clear();
            ticketsGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.TicketId),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.TicketId),
                HeaderText = "Ticket #",
                Width = 70,
            });
            ticketsGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.CustomerName),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.CustomerName),
                HeaderText = "Customer",
                Width = 160,
                AutoSizeMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnMode.Fill,
            });
            ticketsGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.Rating),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.Rating),
                HeaderText = "Rating",
                Width = 60,
            });
            ticketsGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.Category),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.Category),
                HeaderText = "Category",
                Width = 90,
            });
            ticketsGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.Status),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.Status),
                HeaderText = "Status",
                Width = 90,
            });
            ticketsGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.DateLogged),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(FeedbackResponseDto.DateLogged),
                HeaderText = "Logged",
                Width = 130,
                DefaultCellStyle = new DataGridViewCellStyle { Format = "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm" },
            });
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/Controls/ManageUsersView.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Session;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Admin tab: manage employee accounts. Searchable grid + left drawer for the
    /// selected employee (details + Edit / Reset Password / Deactivate actions).
    ///
    /// Admin role is NOT shown in the role dropdown — only Barista and Manager. Admins
    /// cannot create other Admins, edit Admins, or deactivate Admins (server enforces
    /// the same rules).
    /// </summary>
    public class ManageUsersView : UserControl
    {
        private readonly EmployeeController _controller;
        private readonly BindingSource _employeesBindingSource = new();

        private EmployeeResponseDto? _selectedEmployee;
        private int _page = 1;
        private const int PageSize = 25;
        private string _lastSearchSignature = string.Empty;

        // Layout
        private Panel cardHeaderPanel = null!;
        private TextBox searchTxtBx = null!;
        private Button newEmployeeBtn = null!;
        private DataGridView employeesGrid = null!;
        private Panel drawerPanel = null!;
        private Label drawerEmptyLabel = null!;
        private Panel drawerContentPanel = null!;
        private FlowLayoutPanel drawerFlow = null!;

        private Panel combinedBox = null!;
        private Label nameLabel = null!;
        private Label roleLabel = null!;
        private Label contactLabel = null!;
        private Label lastLoginLabel = null!;
        private Label statusLabel = null!;
        private Button editBtn = null!;
        private Button resetPasswordBtn = null!;
        private Button deactivateBtn = null!;
        private PaginationControl pagination = null!;

        // Inline edit state — the drawer's combinedBox swaps between a "view" layout
        // (profile + three buttons) and an "edit" layout (profile header + editable fields
        // + Save/Cancel). These fields hold the editable rows.
        private Panel editPanel = null!;
        private TextBox editFirstNameTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox editLastNameTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox editContactTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox editEmailTxtBx = null!;
        private ComboBox editRoleCombo = null!;
        private TextBox editUsernameTxtBx = null!;
        private Label editErrorLabel = null!;
        private Button editSaveBtn = null!;
        private Button editCancelBtn = null!;

        private Panel resetPanel = null!;
        private TextBox resetNewPasswordTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox resetConfirmPasswordTxtBx = null!;
        private Label resetErrorLabel = null!;
        private Button resetSaveBtn = null!;
        private Button resetCancelBtn = null!;
        private DrawerMode _mode = DrawerMode.View;

        private enum DrawerMode
        {
            View,
            Edit,
            Reset,
        }


        public ManageUsersView()
        {
            _controller = new EmployeeController();
            BuildLayout();
            ConfigureGridColumns();
            Load += async (s, e) => await ReloadAsync();
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // LAYOUT (mirrors CustomerManagementView's card + drawer)
        // =====================================================================

        private void BuildLayout()
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceSecondary;

            var bodyPanel = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceSecondary };

            // ---------- LEFT DRAWER ----------
            drawerPanel = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Left,
                Width = 380,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceCard,
            };

            drawerEmptyLabel = new Label
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleCenter,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                Text = "Select an employee to view details.",
            };

            drawerContentPanel = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, Visible = false, AutoScroll = true };

            drawerFlow = new FlowLayoutPanel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                FlowDirection = FlowDirection.TopDown,
                WrapContents = false,
                AutoSize = true,
                AutoSizeMode = AutoSizeMode.GrowAndShrink,
                Padding = new Padding(20),
                Width = 380,
            };

            // ---------- SINGLE COMBINED BOX: profile + divider + actions ----------
            combinedBox = new Panel
            {
                Size = new Size(340, 340),
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceCard,
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
                Margin = new Padding(0),
            };

            // Profile labels — fixed positions inside the box
            nameLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(16, 16),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(13f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
            };
            roleLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(16, 46),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
            };
            contactLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(16, 72),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
            };
            // usernameLabel is no longer shown in the view state (the grid already has a
            // Username column, and the drawer shouldn't duplicate it). The field still
            // exists on the class so older references compile — it's just never added to
            // the box and never populated. If you'd rather fully delete the field, remove
            // its declaration at the top and the `usernameLabel` line in RefreshDrawer.
            lastLoginLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(16, 98),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
            };
            statusLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(16, 124),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
            };

            // Divider line between profile info and buttons/edit fields
            var dividerLine = new Panel
            {
                Location = new Point(16, 156),
                Size = new Size(308, 1),
                BackColor = BrandPalette.Border,
            };

            // Action buttons (view state) — shown below the divider.
            editBtn = MakeActionButton("Edit Information", 176);
            editBtn.Click += (s, e) => EnterEditState();

            resetPasswordBtn = MakeActionButton("Reset Password", 218);
            resetPasswordBtn.Click += (s, e) => EnterResetState();

            deactivateBtn = MakeActionButton("Deactivate Employee", 260);
            deactivateBtn.ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill;
            deactivateBtn.Click += (s, e) => DeactivateSelected();

            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(editBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(resetPasswordBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(deactivateBtn);

            // Inline edit + reset panels (edit/reset states) — occupy the same vertical
            // slot as the three action buttons. Both hidden by default.
            editPanel = BuildEditPanel();
            editPanel.Visible = false;

            resetPanel = BuildResetPanel();
            resetPanel.Visible = false;

            combinedBox.Controls.Add(resetPanel);
            combinedBox.Controls.Add(editPanel);

            combinedBox.Controls.Add(editPanel);
            combinedBox.Controls.Add(deactivateBtn);
            combinedBox.Controls.Add(resetPasswordBtn);
            combinedBox.Controls.Add(editBtn);
            combinedBox.Controls.Add(dividerLine);
            combinedBox.Controls.Add(statusLabel);
            combinedBox.Controls.Add(lastLoginLabel);
            combinedBox.Controls.Add(contactLabel);
            combinedBox.Controls.Add(roleLabel);
            combinedBox.Controls.Add(nameLabel);

            drawerFlow.Controls.Add(combinedBox);
            drawerContentPanel.Controls.Add(drawerFlow);

            drawerPanel.Controls.Add(drawerEmptyLabel);
            drawerPanel.Controls.Add(drawerContentPanel);

            // ---------- RIGHT SIDE: card with grid ----------
            var cardOuter = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceSecondary,
                Padding = new Padding(24),
            };

            var card = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
            };

            cardHeaderPanel = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 60, BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary };

            searchTxtBx = new TextBox
            {
                Location = new Point(16, 16),
                Size = new Size(300, 28),
                PlaceholderText = "Search by name, contact, username, or ID",
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
            };
            searchTxtBx.TextChanged += async (s, e) => await ReloadAsync();

            newEmployeeBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "+ New Employee",
                Size = new Size(150, 32),
                Location = new Point(16, 14),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            newEmployeeBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            newEmployeeBtn.Click += (s, e) => OpenCreateModal();

            var divider = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = 1, BackColor = BrandPalette.Border };

            cardHeaderPanel.Controls.Add(newEmployeeBtn);
            cardHeaderPanel.Controls.Add(searchTxtBx);
            cardHeaderPanel.Controls.Add(divider);
            cardHeaderPanel.Resize += (s, e) =>
            {
                newEmployeeBtn.Left = cardHeaderPanel.ClientSize.Width - newEmployeeBtn.Width - 16;
                newEmployeeBtn.Top = 14;
            };

            pagination = new PaginationControl();
            pagination.PageChanged += async (s, page) =>
            {
                _page = page;
                await ReloadAsync(keepPage: true);
            };

            employeesGrid = new DataGridView { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None };
            ConfigureGrid(employeesGrid);
            employeesGrid.CellFormatting += EmployeesGrid_CellFormatting;
            employeesGrid.SelectionChanged += async (s, e) =>
            {
                _selectedEmployee = employeesGrid.CurrentRow?.DataBoundItem as EmployeeResponseDto;

                // If the user was editing or resetting and clicks a different row, abandon
                // silently rather than carrying stale input into a different employee.
                if (_mode != DrawerMode.View)
                    ExitToViewState();

                RefreshDrawer();
            };

            card.Controls.Add(employeesGrid);
            card.Controls.Add(pagination);
            card.Controls.Add(cardHeaderPanel);
            cardOuter.Controls.Add(card);

            bodyPanel.Controls.Add(cardOuter);
            bodyPanel.Controls.Add(drawerPanel);
            Controls.Add(bodyPanel);

            // Initial position; also triggered on resize.
            newEmployeeBtn.Left = 300;
        }

        private void EmployeesGrid_CellFormatting(object? sender, DataGridViewCellFormattingEventArgs e)
        {
            if (employeesGrid.Rows[e.RowIndex].DataBoundItem is not EmployeeResponseDto emp)
                return;

            // Render the IsActive column as a readable label instead of the raw bool.
            if (employeesGrid.Columns[e.ColumnIndex].Name == nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.IsActive))
            {
                e.Value = emp.IsActive ? "Active" : "Inactive";
                e.FormattingApplied = true;
            }

            // Mute the entire row when the employee is deactivated. Neutral greys (not
            // brand-tinted) so the muted state reads clearly against the white grid.
            if (!emp.IsActive)
            {
                var greyText = Color.FromArgb(255, 130, 130, 130);
                var greyBackground = Color.FromArgb(255, 240, 240, 240);

                e.CellStyle.ForeColor = greyText;
                e.CellStyle.BackColor = greyBackground;
                e.CellStyle.SelectionForeColor = greyText;
                e.CellStyle.SelectionBackColor = greyBackground;
            }
        }
        /// <summary>
        /// The inline edit form. Laid out with absolute positions inside a Panel whose
        /// height is the sum of its content. Sits inside combinedBox at y=176 (where the
        /// three action buttons also live), so entering edit state hides those buttons and
        /// shows this panel.
        ///
        /// Fields are prefilled in EnterEditState() from the currently selected employee.
        /// </summary>
        private Panel BuildEditPanel()
        {
            const int rowHeight = 56;
            const int labelHeight = 18;
            const int fieldHeight = 28;
            const int fieldWidth = 308;

            var panel = new Panel
            {
                Location = new Point(14, 176),
                Width = 312,
                Height = 6 * rowHeight + 80,   // 6 rows + Save/Cancel + error label space
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceCard,
            };

            int y = 0;

            TextBox MakeRow(string labelText, bool digitsOnly = false)
            {
                var label = new Label
                {
                    Text = labelText,
                    Location = new Point(0, y),
                    AutoSize = true,
                    Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                    ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                };
                var txt = new TextBox
                {
                    Location = new Point(0, y + labelHeight),
                    Size = new Size(fieldWidth, fieldHeight),
                    Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                    BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
                };
                if (digitsOnly)
                {
                    txt.KeyPress += (s, e) =>
                    {
                        if (!char.IsControl(e.KeyChar) && !char.IsDigit(e.KeyChar))
                            e.Handled = true;
                    };
                }
                panel.Controls.Add(txt);
                panel.Controls.Add(label);
                y += rowHeight;
                return txt;
            }

            editFirstNameTxtBx = MakeRow("First name *");
            editLastNameTxtBx = MakeRow("Last name *");
            editContactTxtBx = MakeRow("Contact number * (10-11 digits)", digitsOnly: true);
            editEmailTxtBx = MakeRow("Email (optional)");

            // Role row — label + combo.
            var roleLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Role *",
                Location = new Point(0, y),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
            };
            editRoleCombo = new ComboBox
            {
                Location = new Point(0, y + labelHeight),
                Size = new Size(fieldWidth, fieldHeight),
                DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
            };
            editRoleCombo.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "Barista", "Manager" });
            panel.Controls.Add(editRoleCombo);
            panel.Controls.Add(roleLabel);
            y += rowHeight;

            editUsernameTxtBx = MakeRow("Username * (min 3 chars)");

            // Error label — sits between the last field and the buttons.
            editErrorLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(0, y),
                Size = new Size(fieldWidth, 24),
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft,
            };
            panel.Controls.Add(editErrorLabel);
            y += 28;

            // Save / Cancel row.
            editSaveBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Save Changes",
                Location = new Point(0, y),
                Size = new Size(150, 32),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            editSaveBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            editSaveBtn.Click += EditSaveBtn_Click;

            editCancelBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Cancel",
                Location = new Point(158, y),
                Size = new Size(150, 32),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            editCancelBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            editCancelBtn.Click += (s, e) => ExitToViewState();

            panel.Controls.Add(editSaveBtn);
            panel.Controls.Add(editCancelBtn);

            // The panel's actual height is now known — set it so combinedBox can grow to fit.
            panel.Height = y + 32;
            return panel;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Switches combinedBox from "view" state to "edit" state:
        ///   - Hides the three action buttons
        ///   - Shows the edit panel, prefilled from the selected employee
        ///   - Grows combinedBox to fit the edit panel
        /// </summary>
        private void EnterEditState()
        {
            if (_selectedEmployee is null) return;

            var emp = _selectedEmployee;

            editFirstNameTxtBx.Text = emp.FirstName;
            editLastNameTxtBx.Text = emp.LastName;
            editContactTxtBx.Text = emp.ContactNumber;
            editEmailTxtBx.Text = emp.Email ?? string.Empty;
            editUsernameTxtBx.Text = emp.Username ?? string.Empty;

            var roleIndex = editRoleCombo.Items.IndexOf(emp.Role);
            editRoleCombo.SelectedIndex = roleIndex >= 0 ? roleIndex : 0;

            editErrorLabel.Text = string.Empty;

            editBtn.Visible = false;
            resetPasswordBtn.Visible = false;
            deactivateBtn.Visible = false;
            editPanel.Visible = true;
            resetPanel.Visible = false;

            // Grow the box: edit panel top is y=176 inside combinedBox, plus panel height,
            // plus a 14px bottom inset.
            combinedBox.Height = 176 + editPanel.Height + 14;

            _mode = DrawerMode.Edit;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Reverts combinedBox to "view" state. Called on Cancel and after a successful Save.
        /// </summary>
        /// <summary>
        /// Reverts combinedBox to "view" state. Called on Cancel and after a successful
        /// Save from either the edit panel or the reset panel.
        /// </summary>
        private void ExitToViewState()
        {
            editPanel.Visible = false;
            resetPanel.Visible = false;
            editBtn.Visible = true;
            resetPasswordBtn.Visible = true;
            deactivateBtn.Visible = true;

            combinedBox.Height = 340;
            _mode = DrawerMode.View;
        }

        private async void EditSaveBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedEmployee is null) return;

            editErrorLabel.Text = string.Empty;

            // Client-side validation — mirrors the server rules.
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(editFirstNameTxtBx.Text) ||
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(editLastNameTxtBx.Text))
            {
                editErrorLabel.Text = "First and last name are required.";
                return;
            }

            var cleanedPhone = new string(editContactTxtBx.Text.Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
            if (cleanedPhone.Length < 10 || cleanedPhone.Length > 11)
            {
                editErrorLabel.Text = "Contact number must be 10-11 digits.";
                return;
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(editEmailTxtBx.Text) &&
                (!editEmailTxtBx.Text.Contains('@') || !editEmailTxtBx.Text.Contains('.')))
            {
                editErrorLabel.Text = "Invalid email format.";
                return;
            }

            var newUsername = editUsernameTxtBx.Text.Trim();
            if (newUsername.Length < 3)
            {
                editErrorLabel.Text = "Username must be at least 3 characters.";
                return;
            }

            try
            {
                editSaveBtn.Enabled = false;

                await _controller.UpdateAsync(
                    _selectedEmployee.EmployeeId,
                    editFirstNameTxtBx.Text,
                    editLastNameTxtBx.Text,
                    editContactTxtBx.Text,
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(editEmailTxtBx.Text) ? null : editEmailTxtBx.Text,
                    editRoleCombo.SelectedItem as string ?? "Barista",
                    newUsername);

                ExitToViewState();
                Toast.Show(this, $"Saved changes to {_selectedEmployee.FullName}.", ToastKind.Success);
                await ReloadAsync(keepPage: true);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                editErrorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                editSaveBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }
        /// <summary>
        /// The inline reset-password form. Two password fields + Save/Cancel, prefilled
        /// empty. Sits at the same y-offset as the edit panel and the three action buttons,
        /// so entering reset state hides those and shows this panel instead.
        /// </summary>
        private Panel BuildResetPanel()
        {
            const int rowHeight = 56;
            const int labelHeight = 18;
            const int fieldHeight = 28;
            const int fieldWidth = 308;

            var panel = new Panel
            {
                Location = new Point(14, 176),
                Width = 312,
                Height = 2 * rowHeight + 80,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfaceCard,
            };

            int y = 0;

            // Both helpers add their control to the panel — a local function that only
            // *constructs* a Label and returns it would orphan the label with no parent,
            // which is why they weren't rendering.
            void AddLabel(string text, int yPos)
            {
                var label = new Label
                {
                    Text = text,
                    Location = new Point(0, yPos),
                    AutoSize = true,
                    Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                    ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                };
                panel.Controls.Add(label);
            }

            TextBox MakePwdBox()
            {
                var txt = new TextBox
                {
                    Location = new Point(0, y + labelHeight),
                    Size = new Size(fieldWidth, fieldHeight),
                    Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                    BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
                    UseSystemPasswordChar = true,
                };
                panel.Controls.Add(txt);
                y += rowHeight;
                return txt;
            }

            AddLabel("New password * (min 4 chars)", y);
            resetNewPasswordTxtBx = MakePwdBox();

            AddLabel("Confirm new password *", y);
            resetConfirmPasswordTxtBx = MakePwdBox();

            // Error label
            resetErrorLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(0, y),
                Size = new Size(fieldWidth, 24),
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft,
            };
            panel.Controls.Add(resetErrorLabel);
            y += 28;

            // Save / Cancel row
            resetSaveBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Reset Password",
                Location = new Point(0, y),
                Size = new Size(150, 32),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            resetSaveBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            resetSaveBtn.Click += ResetSaveBtn_Click;

            resetCancelBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Cancel",
                Location = new Point(158, y),
                Size = new Size(150, 32),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            resetCancelBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            resetCancelBtn.Click += (s, e) => ExitToViewState();

            panel.Controls.Add(resetSaveBtn);
            panel.Controls.Add(resetCancelBtn);

            panel.Height = y + 32;
            return panel;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Enters reset-password state: hides the three buttons, shows the reset panel,
        /// grows the box.
        /// </summary>
        private void EnterResetState()
        {
            if (_selectedEmployee is null) return;

            resetNewPasswordTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;
            resetConfirmPasswordTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;
            resetErrorLabel.Text = string.Empty;

            editPanel.Visible = false;
            resetPanel.Visible = true;
            editBtn.Visible = false;
            resetPasswordBtn.Visible = false;
            deactivateBtn.Visible = false;

            combinedBox.Height = 176 + resetPanel.Height + 14;
            _mode = DrawerMode.Reset;
        }

        private async void ResetSaveBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedEmployee is null) return;

            resetErrorLabel.Text = string.Empty;

            var pwd = resetNewPasswordTxtBx.Text;
            var confirm = resetConfirmPasswordTxtBx.Text;

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pwd) || pwd.Length < 4)
            {
                resetErrorLabel.Text = "Password must be at least 4 characters.";
                return;
            }
            if (pwd != confirm)
            {
                resetErrorLabel.Text = "Passwords do not match.";
                return;
            }

            try
            {
                resetSaveBtn.Enabled = false;
                await _controller.ResetPasswordAsync(_selectedEmployee.EmployeeId, pwd);

                ExitToViewState();
                Toast.Show(this, $"Password reset for {_selectedEmployee.FullName}.", ToastKind.Success);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                resetErrorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                resetSaveBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }

        private static Button MakeActionButton(string text, int y)
        {
            var btn = new Button
            {
                Text = text,
                Location = new Point(14, y),
                Size = new Size(312, 32),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft,
                Padding = new Padding(12, 0, 0, 0),
            };
            btn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            return btn;
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // GRID
        // =====================================================================

        private void ConfigureGridColumns()
        {
            employeesGrid.Columns.Clear();
            employeesGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.EmployeeId),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.EmployeeId),
                HeaderText = "ID",
                Width = 60,
            });
            employeesGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.FullName),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.FullName),
                HeaderText = "Name",
                Width = 180,
                AutoSizeMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnMode.Fill,
            });
            employeesGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.Role),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.Role),
                HeaderText = "Role",
                Width = 90,
            });
            employeesGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.ContactNumber),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.ContactNumber),
                HeaderText = "Contact",
                Width = 120,
            });
            employeesGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.Username),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.Username),
                HeaderText = "Username",
                Width = 140,
            });
            employeesGrid.Columns.Add(new DataGridViewTextBoxColumn
            {
                Name = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.IsActive),
                DataPropertyName = nameof(EmployeeResponseDto.IsActive),
                HeaderText = "Active",
                Width = 60,
            });
        }

        private static void ConfigureGrid(DataGridView grid)
        {
            grid.BackgroundColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary;
            grid.AllowUserToAddRows = false;
            grid.AllowUserToDeleteRows = false;
            grid.AllowUserToResizeRows = false;
            grid.ReadOnly = true;
            grid.RowHeadersVisible = false;
            grid.MultiSelect = false;
            grid.SelectionMode = DataGridViewSelectionMode.FullRowSelect;
            grid.AutoGenerateColumns = false;
            grid.EnableHeadersVisualStyles = false;
            grid.GridColor = BrandPalette.Border;
            grid.Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f);
            grid.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = BrandPalette.HeaderBackground;
            grid.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = BrandPalette.HeaderText;
            grid.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f);
            grid.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = BrandPalette.HeaderBackground;
            grid.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = BrandPalette.HeaderText;
            grid.ColumnHeadersHeight = 36;
            grid.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = BrandPalette.RowSelected;
            grid.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText;
            grid.DefaultCellStyle.BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary;
            grid.DefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText;
            grid.RowTemplate.Height = 30;
            grid.ScrollBars = ScrollBars.Vertical;
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // LOADING
        // =====================================================================

        private async Task ReloadAsync(bool keepPage = false)
        {
            if (!keepPage)
            {
                _page = 1;
                pagination.ResetToFirstPage();
            }

            PagedResponse<EmployeeResponseDto> response;
            try
            {
                response = await _controller.SearchAsync(searchTxtBx.Text, _page, PageSize);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, "Error loading employees");
                return;
            }

            _employeesBindingSource.DataSource = response.Items;
            employeesGrid.DataSource = _employeesBindingSource;
            pagination.UpdatePageInfo(response.TotalCount, response.Page, response.PageSize);

            employeesGrid.ClearSelection();
            try { employeesGrid.CurrentCell = null; } catch { }
            _selectedEmployee = null;
            RefreshDrawer();
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // DRAWER
        // =====================================================================

        private void RefreshDrawer()
        {
            var emp = _selectedEmployee;
            if (emp is null)
            {
                drawerEmptyLabel.Visible = true;
                drawerContentPanel.Visible = false;
                return;
            }

            drawerEmptyLabel.Visible = false;
            drawerContentPanel.Visible = true;

            nameLabel.Text = emp.FullName;
            roleLabel.Text = $"{emp.Role}";
            contactLabel.Text = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(emp.ContactNumber) ? "—" : emp.ContactNumber;
            lastLoginLabel.Text = emp.LastLoginDate.HasValue
                ? $"Last login: {emp.LastLoginDate.Value:yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm}"
                : "Never logged in";
            statusLabel.Text = emp.IsActive ? "Active" : "Inactive";
            statusLabel.ForeColor = emp.IsActive ? BrandPalette.StatusPositiveText : BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill;

            // Two independent reasons to lock down an employee:
            //   1. The row is an Admin/SuperAdmin — the server blocks all edits to those.
            //   2. The employee is deactivated — only the reactivate action is allowed.
            //
            // For an Admin row: everything is disabled, including the deactivate/reactivate
            // toggle, since the server blocks even that.
            // For a non-Admin inactive row: Edit and Reset Password are disabled, but the
            // toggle stays enabled so the Manager can reactivate.
            var isAdminRow = emp.Role.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                 emp.Role.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            var isInactive = !emp.IsActive;

            editBtn.Enabled = !isAdminRow && !isInactive;
            resetPasswordBtn.Enabled = !isAdminRow && !isInactive;
            deactivateBtn.Enabled = !isAdminRow;

            deactivateBtn.Text = isInactive ? "Reactivate Employee" : "Deactivate Employee";
            deactivateBtn.ForeColor = isInactive
                ? BrandPalette.NeutralText
                : BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill;
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // ACTIONS
        // =====================================================================

        private void OpenCreateModal()
        {
            using var form = new FrmCreateEmployee(_controller);
            if (form.ShowDialog(FindForm()) == DialogResult.OK)
            {
                _ = ReloadAsync();
            }
        }   

        private async void DeactivateSelected()
        {
            if (_selectedEmployee is null) return;

            var emp = _selectedEmployee;
            var activating = !emp.IsActive;

            var confirm = MessageBox.Show(FindForm(),
                activating
                    ? $"Reactivate {emp.FullName}? They will be able to log in again."
                    : $"Deactivate {emp.FullName}? They will no longer be able to log in.",
                activating ? "Reactivate Employee" : "Deactivate Employee",
                MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                MessageBoxIcon.Question);

            if (confirm != DialogResult.Yes) return;

            try
            {
                if (activating)
                    await _controller.ReactivateAsync(emp.EmployeeId);
                else
                    await _controller.DeactivateAsync(emp.EmployeeId);

                await ReloadAsync(keepPage: true);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), ex.Message, activating ? "Reactivate failed" : "Deactivate failed");
            }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/Controls/PaginationControl.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Reusable pagination bar: a small "◀ Page X of Y ▶" control.
    /// Host grids wire it up via the PageChanged event and update
    /// UpdatePageInfo(totalCount, currentPage, pageSize) after each fetch.
    /// </summary>
    public class PaginationControl : UserControl
    {
        private Button _prevBtn = null!;
        private Button _nextBtn = null!;
        private Label _pageInfoLabel = null!;

        private int _currentPage = 1;
        private int _totalPages = 1;

        /// <summary>Raised when the user clicks Prev or Next. Carries the new page number.</summary>
        public event EventHandler<int>? PageChanged;

        public int CurrentPage => _currentPage;

        public PaginationControl()
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Bottom;
            Height = 36;
            BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary;
            Padding = new Padding(8, 4, 8, 4);

            _prevBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "◀",
                Width = 36,
                Height = 28,
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
                Enabled = false,
            };
            _prevBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _prevBtn.Click += (s, e) => GoTo(_currentPage - 1);

            _nextBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "▶",
                Width = 36,
                Height = 28,
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
                Enabled = false,
            };
            _nextBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _nextBtn.Click += (s, e) => GoTo(_currentPage + 1);

            _pageInfoLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Page 1 of 1",
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleCenter,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
            };

            Controls.Add(_pageInfoLabel);
            Controls.Add(_prevBtn);
            Controls.Add(_nextBtn);

            // Left-docked Prev button, right-docked Next button, fill label between.
            _prevBtn.Dock = DockStyle.Left;
            _nextBtn.Dock = DockStyle.Right;

            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_prevBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_nextBtn);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Called by the host after each fetch. Updates the page text and button states.
        /// </summary>
        public void UpdatePageInfo(int totalCount, int currentPage, int pageSize)
        {
            _currentPage = currentPage;
            _totalPages = pageSize <= 0 ? 1 : Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Ceiling((double)totalCount / pageSize));

            _pageInfoLabel.Text = $"Page {_currentPage} of {_totalPages}";
            _prevBtn.Enabled = _currentPage > 1;
            _nextBtn.Enabled = _currentPage < _totalPages;
        }

        /// <summary>Resets the pager to page 1 (useful when the search filter changes).</summary>
        public void ResetToFirstPage()
        {
            _currentPage = 1;
            _pageInfoLabel.Text = "Page 1 of 1";
            _prevBtn.Enabled = false;
            _nextBtn.Enabled = false;
        }

        private void GoTo(int page)
        {
            if (page < 1 || page > _totalPages) return;
            if (page == _currentPage) return;

            PageChanged?.Invoke(this, page);
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/Controls/PlaceholderView.cs =====

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls
