        private Panel BuildActionRow()
        {
            var row = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Bottom,
                Height = 52,
                BackColor = Color.Transparent,
            };

            // Create primary button (Create or Save)
            _primaryBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Create Campaign",
                Size = new Size(160, 36),
                Location = new Point(0, 8),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _primaryBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _primaryBtn.Click += PrimaryBtn_Click;

            // Edit (View mode)
            _editBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Edit",
                Size = new Size(100, 36),
                Location = new Point(0, 8),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _editBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _editBtn.Click += (s, e) => SwitchToEdit();

            // Activate (View mode)
            _activateBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Activate",
                Size = new Size(110, 36),
                Location = new Point(110, 8),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _activateBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _activateBtn.Click += (s, e) => ToggleActivation(activate: true);

            // Deactivate (View mode)
            _deactivateBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Deactivate",
                Size = new Size(110, 36),
                Location = new Point(230, 8),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _deactivateBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _deactivateBtn.Click += (s, e) => ToggleActivation(activate: false);

            // Cancel (Create/Edit mode)
            _cancelBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Cancel",
                Size = new Size(100, 36),
                Location = new Point(0, 8),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _cancelBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _cancelBtn.Click += CancelBtn_Click;

            // Close (View mode)
            _closeBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Close",
                Size = new Size(100, 36),
                Location = new Point(0, 8),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _closeBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _closeBtn.Click += (s, e) => { DialogResult = ChangedSomething ? DialogResult.OK : DialogResult.Cancel; Close(); };
            
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_closeBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_cancelBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_deactivateBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_activateBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_editBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_primaryBtn);

            row.Controls.Add(_closeBtn);
            row.Controls.Add(_cancelBtn);
            row.Controls.Add(_deactivateBtn);
            row.Controls.Add(_activateBtn);
            row.Controls.Add(_editBtn);
            row.Controls.Add(_primaryBtn);

            return row;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Places the visible action buttons left-to-right at fixed offsets inside the
        /// row. Deliberately does NOT depend on the row's ClientSize — this runs during
        /// the form's Load event, before docking has settled, and earlier attempts to
        /// compute positions from ClientSize pushed buttons off the right edge (or behind
        /// each other) because the row reported a stale or zero width at that moment.
        ///
        /// Layout: buttons are laid out in visual order from x=16 onward, rightward.
        /// Whatever fits, fits; the row's Width is generous enough for every combination
        /// of visible buttons, which is why fixed offsets are safe here.
        ///
        /// Visual order (left → right):
        ///   Close / Cancel  |  Deactivate  |  Activate  |  Edit  |  Create/Save
        /// Destructive (Deactivate) sits well away from the confirm action (Create/Save)
        /// to avoid mis-clicks.
        /// </summary>
        private void PositionActionButtons(Panel row)
        {
            const int leftInset = 16;
            const int gap = 8;
            const int topY = 8;

            var ordered = new[]
 {
                _closeBtn,
                _cancelBtn,
                _editBtn,
                _activateBtn,
                _deactivateBtn,
                _primaryBtn,
            };

            var x = leftInset;

            foreach (var btn in ordered)
            {
                if (!btn.Visible) continue;

                btn.Left = x;
                btn.Top = topY;
                x += btn.Width + gap;
            }
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // MODE
        // =====================================================================

        private void ApplyMode()
        {
            // Reset visibility for all mode-dependent controls
            _primaryBtn.Visible = false;
            _editBtn.Visible = false;
            _activateBtn.Visible = false;
            _deactivateBtn.Visible = false;
            _cancelBtn.Visible = false;
            _closeBtn.Visible = false;
            _viewPanel.Visible = false;
            _formPanel.Visible = false;
            _errorLabel.Text = string.Empty;

            switch (_mode)
            {
                case Mode.Create:
                    Text = "New Campaign";
                    _formPanel.Visible = true;
                    _primaryBtn.Text = "Create Campaign";
                    _primaryBtn.Visible = true;
                    _cancelBtn.Visible = true;
                    break;

                case Mode.Edit:
                    Text = "Edit Campaign";
                    _formPanel.Visible = true;
                    _primaryBtn.Text = "Save Changes";
                    _primaryBtn.Visible = true;
                    _cancelBtn.Visible = true;
                    break;

                case Mode.View:
                    Text = "Campaign Details";
                    _viewPanel.Visible = true;
                    RefreshViewPanel();
                    _editBtn.Visible = true;
                    _closeBtn.Visible = true;

                    // Only one of Activate / Deactivate is ever visible, based on status.
                    var isActive = _existing?.Status?.Equals("Active", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true;
                    _activateBtn.Visible = !isActive;
                    _deactivateBtn.Visible = isActive;
                    break;
            }

            // Reflow button positions after visibility changes.
            PositionActionButtons(_actionRow);
        }

        private void SwitchToEdit()
        {
            _mode = Mode.Edit;
            PopulateFromExisting();
            ApplyMode();
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // DATA BINDING
        // =====================================================================

        private void PopulateEmptyForm()
        {
            _nameTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;
            _categoryCombo.SelectedIndex = 0;
            _discountTypeCombo.SelectedIndex = 0;
            _discountValueNud.Value = 10m;
            _targetTierCombo.SelectedIndex = 0;
            _hasMinPurchaseChk.Checked = false;
            _minPurchaseNud.Value = 0m;
            _minPurchaseNud.Enabled = false;
            _hasMaxDiscountChk.Checked = false;
            _maxDiscountNud.Value = 0m;
            _maxDiscountNud.Enabled = false;
            _maxRedemptionsNud.Value = 1m;
            _startDatePicker.Value = DateTime.Today;
            _endDatePicker.Value = DateTime.Today.AddMonths(1);
        }

        private void PopulateFromExisting()
        {
            if (_existing is null) return;

            _nameTxtBx.Text = _existing.PromoName;

            var catIdx = _categoryCombo.Items.IndexOf(_existing.PromoCategory);
            _categoryCombo.SelectedIndex = catIdx >= 0 ? catIdx : 0;

            var typeIdx = _discountTypeCombo.Items.IndexOf(_existing.DiscountType);
            _discountTypeCombo.SelectedIndex = typeIdx >= 0 ? typeIdx : 0;

            _discountValueNud.Value = _existing.DiscountValue;

            var tierIdx = _targetTierCombo.Items.IndexOf(_existing.TargetTier);
            _targetTierCombo.SelectedIndex = tierIdx >= 0 ? tierIdx : 0;

            if (_existing.MinPurchaseAmount.HasValue)
            {
                _hasMinPurchaseChk.Checked = true;
                _minPurchaseNud.Value = _existing.MinPurchaseAmount.Value;
                _minPurchaseNud.Enabled = true;
            }
            else
            {
                _hasMinPurchaseChk.Checked = false;
                _minPurchaseNud.Value = 0m;
                _minPurchaseNud.Enabled = false;
            }

            if (_existing.MaxDiscountAmount.HasValue)
            {
                _hasMaxDiscountChk.Checked = true;
                _maxDiscountNud.Value = _existing.MaxDiscountAmount.Value;
                _maxDiscountNud.Enabled = true;
            }
            else
            {
                _hasMaxDiscountChk.Checked = false;
                _maxDiscountNud.Value = 0m;
                _maxDiscountNud.Enabled = false;
            }

            _maxRedemptionsNud.Value = _existing.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer;
            _startDatePicker.Value = _existing.StartDate;
            _endDatePicker.Value = _existing.EndDate;
        }

        private void RefreshViewPanel()
        {
            if (_existing is null) return;

            _viewPanel.Controls.Clear();

            const int labelX = 18;
            const int valueX = 180;
            int y = 14;

            void AddRow(string label, string value, Font? valueFont = null, Color? valueColor = null)
            {
                _viewPanel.Controls.Add(new Label
                {
                    Text = label,
                    Location = new Point(labelX, y),
                    AutoSize = true,
                    Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                    ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
                });

                _viewPanel.Controls.Add(new Label
                {
                    Text = value,
                    Location = new Point(valueX, y),
                    AutoSize = true,
                    Font = valueFont ?? BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                    ForeColor = valueColor ?? BrandPalette.NeutralText,
                });

                y += 26;
            }

            AddRow("Promo name", _existing.PromoName, BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(11f));
            AddRow("Category", _existing.PromoCategory);
            AddRow("Discount", FormatDiscount(_existing.DiscountType, _existing.DiscountValue),
                BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(11f), BrandPalette.Brand);
            AddRow("Target tier", _existing.TargetTier);
            AddRow("Min purchase", _existing.MinPurchaseAmount.HasValue ? $"₱{_existing.MinPurchaseAmount.Value:0.00}" : "—");
            AddRow("Max discount", _existing.MaxDiscountAmount.HasValue ? $"₱{_existing.MaxDiscountAmount.Value:0.00}" : "—");
            AddRow("Max redemptions", $"{_existing.MaxRedemptionsPerCustomer} per customer");
            AddRow("Start", _existing.StartDate.ToString("MMMM d, yyyy"));
            AddRow("End", _existing.EndDate.ToString("MMMM d, yyyy"));

            var statusColor = _existing.Status?.Equals("Active", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) == true
                ? BrandPalette.StatusPositiveText
                : BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted;
            AddRow("Status", _existing.Status ?? "—", BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f), statusColor);
        }

        private static string FormatDiscount(string type, decimal value) =>
            type.Equals("Percentage", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                ? $"{value:0.##}% off"
                : $"₱{value:0.##} off";

        // =====================================================================
        // ACTIONS
        // =====================================================================

        private async void PrimaryBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _errorLabel.Text = string.Empty;

            // Client-side validation before hitting the API (matches server rules).
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_nameTxtBx.Text))
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "Promo name is required.";
                return;
            }

            if (_discountTypeCombo.SelectedItem is not string discountType)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "Discount type is required.";
                return;
            }

            var discountValue = _discountValueNud.Value;
            if (discountValue <= 0)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "Discount value must be greater than zero.";
                return;
            }

            if (discountType.Equals("Percentage", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) && discountValue > 100)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "Percentage discount cannot exceed 100.";
                return;
            }

            if (_endDatePicker.Value <= _startDatePicker.Value)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "End date must be after start date.";
                return;
            }

            var employeeId = EmployeeSession.Current?.EmployeeId
                ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No employee session.");

            try
            {
                _primaryBtn.Enabled = false;

                var minPurchase = _hasMinPurchaseChk.Checked ? _minPurchaseNud.Value : (decimal?)null;
                var maxDiscount = _hasMaxDiscountChk.Checked ? _maxDiscountNud.Value : (decimal?)null;

                if (_mode == Mode.Create)
                {
                    await _controller.CreateAsync(
                        _nameTxtBx.Text, _categoryCombo.SelectedItem as string ?? PromoCategories.Seasonal,
                        discountType, discountValue,
                        _targetTierCombo.SelectedItem as string ?? "All",
                        minPurchase, maxDiscount,
                        (int)_maxRedemptionsNud.Value,
                        _startDatePicker.Value.Date, _endDatePicker.Value.Date,
                        employeeId);

                    ChangedSomething = true;
                    DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                    Close();
                }
                else // Mode.Edit
                {
                    if (_existing is null) return;

                    await _controller.UpdateAsync(
                        _existing.CampaignId,
                        _nameTxtBx.Text, _categoryCombo.SelectedItem as string ?? PromoCategories.Seasonal,
                        discountType, discountValue,
                        _targetTierCombo.SelectedItem as string ?? "All",
                        minPurchase, maxDiscount,
                        (int)_maxRedemptionsNud.Value,
                        _startDatePicker.Value.Date, _endDatePicker.Value.Date,
                        employeeId);

                    ChangedSomething = true;
                    DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                    Close();
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                _primaryBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }

        private void CancelBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            // Cancel from Create: close without saving.
            // Cancel from Edit: go back to View (not close) — user was looking at a campaign.
            if (_mode == Mode.Edit && _existing is not null)
            {
                _mode = Mode.View;
                ApplyMode();
            }
            else
            {
                DialogResult = ChangedSomething ? DialogResult.OK : DialogResult.Cancel;
                Close();
            }
        }

        private async void ToggleActivation(bool activate)
        {
            if (_existing is null) return;

            var verb = activate ? "activate" : "deactivate";
            var confirm = MessageBox.Show(this,
                $"Are you sure you want to {verb} \"{_existing.PromoName}\"?",
                activate ? "Activate Campaign" : "Deactivate Campaign",
                MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Question);

            if (confirm != DialogResult.Yes) return;

            try
            {
                _activateBtn.Enabled = false;
                _deactivateBtn.Enabled = false;

                var employeeId = EmployeeSession.Current?.EmployeeId
                    ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("No employee session.");

                if (activate)
                    await _controller.ActivateAsync(_existing.CampaignId, employeeId);
                else
                    await _controller.DeactivateAsync(_existing.CampaignId, employeeId);

                ChangedSomething = true;

                // Close the modal — the caller will reload the grid, so the card
                // reflects the new status. Keeping the modal open with a stale view
                // is more confusing than just closing.
                DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                Close();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
                _activateBtn.Enabled = true;
                _deactivateBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/FrmCreateEmployee.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Modal for creating a new employee + their login credential in one step.
    /// Admin can only assign Barista or Manager roles — the server enforces the same
    /// rule, but we also omit "Admin" from the dropdown here.
    /// </summary>
    public class FrmCreateEmployee : Form
    {
        private readonly EmployeeController _controller;

        private TextBox _firstNameTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox _lastNameTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox _contactTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox _emailTxtBx = null!;
        private ComboBox _roleCombo = null!;
        private TextBox _usernameTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox _passwordTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox _confirmTxtBx = null!;
        private Label _errorLabel = null!;

        public FrmCreateEmployee(EmployeeController controller)
        {
            _controller = controller;

            Text = "New Employee";
            FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
            MaximizeBox = false;
            MinimizeBox = false;
            ClientSize = new Size(500, 560);
            BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary;
            Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f);
            Padding = new Padding(24);

            BuildLayout();
        }

        private void BuildLayout()
        {
            var titleLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Create Employee",
                Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                Height = 30,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(14f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
            };

            var subtitleLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Fill in the employee's details and assign them a login.",
                Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                Height = 40,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
            };

            // Two-column row for first/last name
            var nameRow = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 68 };
            _firstNameTxtBx = MakeField(0, 0, 220);
            _lastNameTxtBx = MakeField(244, 0, 220);

            _contactTxtBx = MakeField(0, 0, 464);
            _contactTxtBx.KeyPress += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (!char.IsControl(e.KeyChar) && !char.IsDigit(e.KeyChar))
                    e.Handled = true;
            };
            var contactPanel = WrapField("Contact number * (10-11 digits)", _contactTxtBx, 68);

            _emailTxtBx = MakeField(0, 0, 464);
            var emailPanel = WrapField("Email (optional)", _emailTxtBx, 68);

            // Role row
            var rolePanel = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 68 };
            var roleLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Role *",
                Location = new Point(0, 0),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
            };
            _roleCombo = new ComboBox
            {
                Location = new Point(0, 20),
                Size = new Size(464, 28),
                DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
            };
            _roleCombo.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "Barista", "Manager" });
            _roleCombo.SelectedIndex = 0;
            rolePanel.Controls.Add(_roleCombo);
            rolePanel.Controls.Add(roleLabel);

            _usernameTxtBx = MakeField(0, 0, 464);
            var usernamePanel = WrapField("Username * (min 3 chars)", _usernameTxtBx, 68);

            _passwordTxtBx = MakeField(0, 0, 464);
            _passwordTxtBx.UseSystemPasswordChar = true;
            var passwordPanel = WrapField("Password * (min 4 chars)", _passwordTxtBx, 68);

            _confirmTxtBx = MakeField(0, 0, 464);
            _confirmTxtBx.UseSystemPasswordChar = true;
            var confirmPanel = WrapField("Confirm password *", _confirmTxtBx, 68);

            _errorLabel = new Label
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                Height = 30,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill,
            };

            var btnRow = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = 44 };

            var cancelBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Cancel",
                Width = 120,
                Height = 36,
                Dock = DockStyle.Right,
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            cancelBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            cancelBtn.Click += (s, e) => { DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel; Close(); };

            var createBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Create Employee",
                Width = 160,
                Height = 36,
                Dock = DockStyle.Right,
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            createBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            createBtn.Click += CreateBtn_Click;

            btnRow.Controls.Add(cancelBtn);
            btnRow.Controls.Add(createBtn);

            // Build top-down. Order matters with Dock.Top: first added is bottom-most.
            // We want visual order: title, subtitle, first/last row, contact, email, role, username, password, confirm, error.
            var stack = new List<Control>
            {
                _errorLabel,
                confirmPanel,
                passwordPanel,
                usernamePanel,
                rolePanel,
                emailPanel,
                contactPanel,
                nameRow,
                subtitleLabel,
                titleLabel,
            };
            foreach (var c in stack)
                Controls.Add(c);
            Controls.Add(btnRow);

            // Tab order
            int tab = 0;
            _firstNameTxtBx.TabIndex = tab++;
            _lastNameTxtBx.TabIndex = tab++;
            _contactTxtBx.TabIndex = tab++;
            _emailTxtBx.TabIndex = tab++;
            _roleCombo.TabIndex = tab++;
            _usernameTxtBx.TabIndex = tab++;
            _passwordTxtBx.TabIndex = tab++;
            _confirmTxtBx.TabIndex = tab++;
            createBtn.TabIndex = tab++;
            cancelBtn.TabIndex = tab++;

            AcceptButton = createBtn;
            CancelButton = cancelBtn;
        }

        private static TextBox MakeField(int x, int y, int width)
        {
            return new TextBox
            {
                Location = new Point(x, y + 20),
                Size = new Size(width, 28),
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
            };
        }

        private static Panel WrapField(string labelText, TextBox field, int height)
        {
            var panel = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = height };
            var label = new Label
            {
                Text = labelText,
                Location = new Point(0, 0),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
            };
            panel.Controls.Add(field);
            panel.Controls.Add(label);
            return panel;
        }

        private async void CreateBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _errorLabel.Text = string.Empty;

            // Client-side validation matching server rules
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_firstNameTxtBx.Text) ||
                string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_lastNameTxtBx.Text))
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "First and last name are required.";
                return;
            }

            var cleanedPhone = new string(_contactTxtBx.Text.Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
            if (cleanedPhone.Length < 10 || cleanedPhone.Length > 11)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "Contact number must be 10-11 digits.";
                return;
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_emailTxtBx.Text) &&
                (!_emailTxtBx.Text.Contains('@') || !_emailTxtBx.Text.Contains('.')))
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "Invalid email format.";
                return;
            }

            if (_usernameTxtBx.Text.Trim().Length < 3)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "Username must be at least 3 characters.";
                return;
            }

            if (_passwordTxtBx.Text.Length < 4)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "Password must be at least 4 characters.";
                return;
            }

            if (_passwordTxtBx.Text != _confirmTxtBx.Text)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = "Passwords do not match.";
                return;
            }

            try
            {
                await _controller.CreateAsync(
                    _firstNameTxtBx.Text, _lastNameTxtBx.Text,
                    _contactTxtBx.Text,
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_emailTxtBx.Text) ? null : _emailTxtBx.Text,
                    _roleCombo.SelectedItem as string ?? "Barista",
                    _usernameTxtBx.Text,
                    _passwordTxtBx.Text);

                DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                Close();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                _errorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
            }
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/FrmCustomerOnboarding.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Modal registration form, two steps. Caller reads CreatedCustomer after ShowDialog()
    /// returns OK. MVC: no business logic here — validation is done on the API.
    /// </summary>
    public partial class FrmCustomerOnboarding : Form
    {
        private readonly CustomerController _controller;
        private DateTime? _birthday;

        public CustomerResponseDto? CreatedCustomer { get; private set; }

        public FrmCustomerOnboarding(CustomerController controller)
        {
            _controller = controller;
            InitializeComponent();

            // Explicit tab order — visual top-to-bottom, left-to-right.
            // Step 1 order: first name -> last name -> phone -> email -> birthday -> Cancel -> Next.
            // Step 2 order: Back -> Confirm (Next and Cancel are hidden on step 2).
            firstNameTxtBx.TabIndex = 0;
            lastNameTxtBx.TabIndex = 1;
            phoneTxtBx.TabIndex = 2;
            emailTxtBx.TabIndex = 3;
            hasBirthdayChk.TabIndex = 4;
            birthdayPicker.TabIndex = 5;
            nextBtn.TabIndex = 6;
            cancelBtn.TabIndex = 7;
            backBtn.TabIndex = 8;
            confirmBtn.TabIndex = 9;

            hasBirthdayChk.CheckedChanged += (s, e) =>
            {
                birthdayPicker.Enabled = hasBirthdayChk.Checked;
                _birthday = hasBirthdayChk.Checked ? birthdayPicker.Value.Date : null;
            };
            birthdayPicker.ValueChanged += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (hasBirthdayChk.Checked)
                    _birthday = birthdayPicker.Value.Date;
            };
            phoneTxtBx.KeyPress += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (!char.IsControl(e.KeyChar) && !char.IsDigit(e.KeyChar))
                    e.Handled = true;
            };

            nextBtn.Click += NextBtn_Click;
            backBtn.Click += (s, e) => ShowDetailsStep();
            confirmBtn.Click += ConfirmBtn_ClickAsync;

            ShowDetailsStep();
        }

        private bool CanSave() =>
            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(firstNameTxtBx.Text) &&
            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(lastNameTxtBx.Text) &&
            !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(phoneTxtBx.Text);

        private void NextBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (!CanSave())
            {
                errorLabel.Text = "Please fill in first name, last name, and phone number.";
                return;
            }

            // Phone: 10-11 digits only.
            var phone = new string(phoneTxtBx.Text.Where(char.IsDigit).ToArray());
            if (phone.Length < 10 || phone.Length > 11)
            {
                errorLabel.Text = "Phone number must be 10-11 digits.";
                return;
            }

            // Email is optional, but if provided it must look like an email.
            var email = emailTxtBx.Text.Trim();
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(email) && !IsValidEmail(email))
            {
                errorLabel.Text = "Please enter a valid email address (e.g. name@example.com).";
                return;
            }

            errorLabel.Text = string.Empty;
            ShowConfirmStep();
        }

        private static bool IsValidEmail(string email)
        {
            // Lightweight check: something@something.something with no whitespace.
            // The server does a stricter check (contains '@' and '.'); this is the UX-level
            // gate so the user sees the error before submitting.
            var atIdx = email.IndexOf('@');
            if (atIdx <= 0) return false;                        // must have @, and not at position 0
            if (atIdx != email.LastIndexOf('@')) return false;   // only one @

            var dotIdx = email.IndexOf('.', atIdx + 1);
            if (dotIdx < 0 || dotIdx == atIdx + 1) return false; // must have . after @, not immediately
            if (dotIdx == email.Length - 1) return false;        // must have something after the .

            return !email.Any(char.IsWhiteSpace);
        }

        private async void ConfirmBtn_ClickAsync(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                errorLabel.Text = string.Empty;
                confirmBtn.Enabled = false;
                CreatedCustomer = await _controller.RegisterAsync(
                    firstNameTxtBx.Text, lastNameTxtBx.Text, phoneTxtBx.Text,
                    string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(emailTxtBx.Text) ? null : emailTxtBx.Text,
                    _birthday);
                DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                Close();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ShowDetailsStep();
                errorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                confirmBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }

        private void ShowDetailsStep()
        {
            detailsPanel.Visible = true;
            confirmPanel.Visible = false;
            nextBtn.Visible = true;
            backBtn.Visible = false;
            confirmBtn.Visible = false;
            stepSubtitleLabel.Text = "Step 1 of 2 — Details";
        }

        private void ShowConfirmStep()
        {
            nameValueLabel.Text = $"Name: {firstNameTxtBx.Text} {lastNameTxtBx.Text}";
            phoneValueLabel.Text = $"Phone: {phoneTxtBx.Text}";
            emailValueLabel.Text = $"Email: {(string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(emailTxtBx.Text) ? "Not provided" : emailTxtBx.Text)}";
            birthdayValueLabel.Text = $"Birthday: {(_birthday.HasValue ? _birthday.Value.ToString("MMMM d, yyyy") : "Not provided")}";

            detailsPanel.Visible = false;
            confirmPanel.Visible = true;
            nextBtn.Visible = false;
            backBtn.Visible = true;
            confirmBtn.Visible = true;
            stepSubtitleLabel.Text = "Step 2 of 2 — Confirm";
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/FrmItemCustomizer.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views
{
    public enum CustomizerMode
    {
        NewItem,
        EditExisting,
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Code-only modal (no paired Designer.cs — everything is built in the constructor
    /// below, same convention as Views/Controls/ProductCardControl.cs). Drop this file in
    /// alongside the other Forms; it needs no designer surface to work.
    ///
    /// Opened either from a drink card click (Mode == NewItem) or from clicking an existing
    /// cart line to edit it (Mode == EditExisting). Caller reads SelectedSize / Quantity /
    /// SelectedModifiers after ShowDialog() returns OK.
    ///
    /// MVC note: no ViewModel — this modal doesn't talk to any Model/Service at all (it's
    /// pure local price-preview arithmetic on the size/modifiers/quantity picked so far), so
    /// it just keeps its own plain fields instead of wrapping them in a bindable object.
    /// </summary>
    public class FrmItemCustomizer : Form
    {
        public Product Product { get; }
        public CustomizerMode Mode { get; }

        private readonly List<ProductSize> _sizes;
        private readonly Dictionary<ProductModifier, CheckBox> _modifierChecks = new();

        public ProductSize SelectedSize { get; private set; }
        public int Quantity { get; private set; } = 1;
        public IEnumerable<ProductModifier> SelectedModifiers =>
            _modifierChecks.Where(kv => kv.Value.Checked).Select(kv => kv.Key);

        private decimal CalculatedUnitPrice => SelectedSize.Price + SelectedModifiers.Sum(m => m.Price);
        private decimal CalculatedLineTotal => CalculatedUnitPrice * Quantity;

        private readonly Panel _sizePanel = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Top, AutoSize = true, Padding = new Padding(0, 4, 0, 10) };
        private readonly Panel _modifiersPanel = new()
        {
            Dock = DockStyle.Top,
            AutoSize = false,
            Height = 320,
            Width = 900,// scrollable fixed height
            AutoScroll = true,
            Padding = new Padding(0, 4, 0, 10)
        };
        private readonly Label _priceLabel = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 34, TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleRight };
        private readonly Label _qtyValueLabel = new() { Width = 36, TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleCenter, Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(11f) };
        private readonly Button _confirmBtn;

        /// <param name="editingItem">Pass the existing OrderItem to pre-populate for editing; null for a brand new line.</param>
        public FrmItemCustomizer(Product product, IReadOnlyList<ProductModifier> allModifiers, OrderItem? editingItem = null)
        {
            Product = product;
            _sizes = product.ProductSizes.ToList();
            if (_sizes.Count == 0)
                throw new InvalidOperationException($"{product.ProductName} has no sizes configured.");

            Mode = editingItem != null ? CustomizerMode.EditExisting : CustomizerMode.NewItem;
            SelectedSize = editingItem != null
                ? _sizes.FirstOrDefault(s => s.ProductSizeId == editingItem.ProductSizeId) ?? _sizes[0]
                : _sizes[0];
            Quantity = editingItem?.Quantity ?? 1;

            Text = Mode == CustomizerMode.NewItem ? "Customize Item" : "Edit Item";
            Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f);
            FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
            MaximizeBox = false;
            MinimizeBox = false;
            ClientSize = new Size(720, 720);
            BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary;
            Padding = new Padding(18);

            var productNameLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = product.ProductName,
                Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                Height = 30,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(13f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralText,
            };

            var sizeHeader = SectionHeader("Size");
            BuildSizeOptions();

            var modifierHeader = SectionHeader("Modifiers");
            BuildModifierOptions(allModifiers, editingItem);

            var qtyHeader = SectionHeader("Quantity");
            var qtyRow = BuildQuantityStepper();

            _confirmBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = Mode == CustomizerMode.NewItem ? "Add to Cart" : "Update Item",
                Dock = DockStyle.Bottom,
                Height = 42,
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(10.5f),
            };
            _confirmBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _confirmBtn.Click += (s, e) => { DialogResult = DialogResult.OK; Close(); };

            var cancelBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Cancel",
                Dock = DockStyle.Bottom,
                Height = 34,
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                Margin = new Padding(0, 0, 0, 6),
            };
            cancelBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            cancelBtn.Click += (s, e) => { DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel; Close(); };

            // Added bottom-up because these are Dock.Bottom/Top mixed; Controls order below
            // keeps the visual stack: name -> size -> modifiers -> qty -> price -> buttons.
            Controls.Add(_priceLabel);
            Controls.Add(qtyRow);
            Controls.Add(qtyHeader);
            Controls.Add(_modifiersPanel);
            Controls.Add(modifierHeader);
            Controls.Add(_sizePanel);
            Controls.Add(sizeHeader);
            Controls.Add(productNameLabel);
            Controls.Add(cancelBtn);
            Controls.Add(_confirmBtn);

            RefreshPriceLabel();
        }

        private static Label SectionHeader(string text) => new()
        {
            Text = text,
            Dock = DockStyle.Top,
            Height = 22,
            Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(9.5f),
            ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
        };

        private void BuildSizeOptions()
        {
            foreach (var size in _sizes)
            {
                var radio = new RadioButton
                {
                    Text = $"{size.SizeName} — ₱{size.Price:0.##}",
                    Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                    Height = 26,
                    Checked = size.ProductSizeId == SelectedSize.ProductSizeId,
                    Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                };
                radio.CheckedChanged += (s, e) =>
                {
                    if (radio.Checked)
                    {
                        SelectedSize = size;
                        RefreshPriceLabel();
                    }
                };
                _sizePanel.Controls.Add(radio);
            }
            // Panel.Controls.Add with Dock=Top stacks in reverse visual order (last added is
            // topmost) — reverse the child order so sizes read top-to-bottom as seeded.
            var children = _sizePanel.Controls.Cast<Control>().ToList();
            _sizePanel.Controls.Clear();
            for (int i = children.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                _sizePanel.Controls.Add(children[i]);
        }

        private void BuildModifierOptions(IReadOnlyList<ProductModifier> allModifiers, OrderItem? editingItem)
        {
            foreach (var modifier in allModifiers)
            {
                var isPreSelected = editingItem != null &&
                    editingItem.OrderItemModifiers.Any(m => m.ProductModifierId == modifier.ProductModifierId);

                var check = new CheckBox
                {
                    Text = $"{modifier.Name} (+₱{modifier.Price:0.##})",
                    Dock = DockStyle.Top,
                    Height = 26,
                    Checked = isPreSelected,
                    Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f),
                };
                check.CheckedChanged += (s, e) => RefreshPriceLabel();
                _modifierChecks[modifier] = check;
                _modifiersPanel.Controls.Add(check);
            }
            var children = _modifiersPanel.Controls.Cast<Control>().ToList();
            _modifiersPanel.Controls.Clear();
            for (int i = children.Count - 1; i >= 0; i--)
                _modifiersPanel.Controls.Add(children[i]);
        }

        private Panel BuildQuantityStepper()
        {
            var row = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 40 };

            var minusBtn = MakeStepperButton("-");
            minusBtn.Left = 0;
            minusBtn.Click += (s, e) =>
            {
                Quantity = Math.Max(1, Quantity - 1);
                _qtyValueLabel.Text = Quantity.ToString();
                RefreshPriceLabel();
            };

            var plusBtn = MakeStepperButton("+");
            plusBtn.Left = minusBtn.Width + 44;
            plusBtn.Click += (s, e) =>
            {
                Quantity += 1;
                _qtyValueLabel.Text = Quantity.ToString();
                RefreshPriceLabel();
            };

            _qtyValueLabel.Left = minusBtn.Width + 4;
            _qtyValueLabel.Top = 4;
            _qtyValueLabel.Height = 32;
            _qtyValueLabel.Text = Quantity.ToString();

            row.Controls.Add(minusBtn);
            row.Controls.Add(_qtyValueLabel);
            row.Controls.Add(plusBtn);
            return row;
        }

        private static Button MakeStepperButton(string text) => new()
        {
            Text = text,
            Width = 32,
            Height = 32,
            Top = 4,
            FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
            BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryFill,
            ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonSecondaryText,
            Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(12f),
        };

        private void RefreshPriceLabel()
        {
            _priceLabel.Text = $"Total: ₱{CalculatedLineTotal:0.00}";
            _priceLabel.Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(13f);
            _priceLabel.ForeColor = BrandPalette.Brand;
        }
    }
}


===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/FrmLogFeedback.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.Controllers;
using CRM_Morcaffein.api.DTOs;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Modal for logging a new feedback ticket. Step 1 finds/picks the customer (its own
    /// search — this modal doesn't depend on anything being pre-selected by the caller);
    /// step 2 fills in the rating/category/notes.
    ///
    /// MVC: calls FeedbackController methods directly and keeps its own plain fields for
    /// in-progress input. All validation is server-side.
    /// </summary>
    public partial class FrmLogFeedback : Form
    {
        private readonly FeedbackController _controller;
        private readonly int _loggedByEmployeeId;

        private CustomerResponseDto? _selectedCustomer;
        private int _rating;
        private string _category = string.Empty;

        public FeedbackResponseDto? CreatedFeedback { get; private set; }

        public FrmLogFeedback(FeedbackController controller, int loggedByEmployeeId)
        {
            _controller = controller;
            _loggedByEmployeeId = loggedByEmployeeId;
            InitializeComponent();

            // Explicit tab order — visual top-to-bottom.
            // Step 1: search -> results -> Next -> Cancel
            // Step 2: stars (mouse only for now) -> category -> notes -> Back -> Submit
            customerSearchTxtBx.TabIndex = 0;
            customerResultsListBox.TabIndex = 1;
            nextBtn.TabIndex = 2;
            cancelBtn.TabIndex = 3;

            starRating.TabIndex = 4;
            categoryCombo.TabIndex = 5;
            staffNotesTxtBx.TabIndex = 6;
            backBtn.TabIndex = 7;
            submitBtn.TabIndex = 8;

            customerSearchTxtBx.TextChanged += CustomerSearchTxtBx_TextChanged;
            customerResultsListBox.DisplayMember = nameof(CustomerResponseDto.FullName);
            customerResultsListBox.SelectedIndexChanged += (s, e) =>
                nextBtn.Enabled = customerResultsListBox.SelectedItem != null;

            starRating.RatingChanged += (s, e) => { _rating = starRating.Rating; UpdateSubmitEnabled(); };
            categoryCombo.SelectedIndexChanged += (s, e) => { _category = categoryCombo.SelectedItem as string ?? string.Empty; UpdateSubmitEnabled(); };

            nextBtn.Click += NextBtn_Click;
            backBtn.Click += (s, e) => ShowPickCustomerStep();
            submitBtn.Click += SubmitBtn_Click;

            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(nextBtn);
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(submitBtn);

            ShowPickCustomerStep();
            CustomerSearchTxtBx_TextChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty); // populate the initial (unfiltered) results list
        }

        private async void CustomerSearchTxtBx_TextChanged(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                var results = await _controller.SearchCustomersAsync(customerSearchTxtBx.Text);
                customerResultsListBox.DataSource = results;
            }
            catch
            {
                customerResultsListBox.DataSource = null;
            }
        }

        private void NextBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (customerResultsListBox.SelectedItem is not CustomerResponseDto customer)
                return;

            _selectedCustomer = customer;
            customerLabel.Text = $"For: {customer.FullName}";
            ShowDetailsStep();
            UpdateSubmitEnabled();
        }

        private void UpdateSubmitEnabled()
        {
            submitBtn.Enabled = _selectedCustomer != null && _rating is >= 1 and <= 5 && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_category);
        }

        private async void SubmitBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (_selectedCustomer is null) return;

            try
            {
                errorLabel.Text = string.Empty;
                submitBtn.Enabled = false;

                CreatedFeedback = await _controller.LogFeedbackAsync(
                    _selectedCustomer.CustomerId,
                    _loggedByEmployeeId,
                    _rating,
                    _category,
                    staffNotesTxtBx.Text);

                DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                Close();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                errorLabel.Text = ex.Message;
            }
            finally
            {
                submitBtn.Enabled = true;
            }
        }

        private void ShowPickCustomerStep()
        {
            customerPickPanel.Visible = true;
            detailsPanel.Visible = false;
            cancelBtn.Visible = true;
            nextBtn.Visible = true;
            backBtn.Visible = false;
            submitBtn.Visible = false;
            stepSubtitleLabel.Text = "Step 1 of 2 — Find Customer";
        }

        private void ShowDetailsStep()
        {
            customerPickPanel.Visible = false;
            detailsPanel.Visible = true;
            cancelBtn.Visible = false; // step 2 offers Back instead — Cancel would just duplicate it
            nextBtn.Visible = false;
            backBtn.Visible = true;
            submitBtn.Visible = true;
            stepSubtitleLabel.Text = "Step 2 of 2 — Feedback Details";
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/FrmLogin.cs =====
﻿using CRM_Morcaffein.Session;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;
using CRM_Morcaffein.domain.entities;
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Services.Api;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Login screen. Two-pane layout: brand panel on the left (BLANK — fill in Figma),
    /// login form on the right. On success, stores the JWT in ApiConfig.AuthToken,
    /// seeds EmployeeSession, and closes with DialogResult.OK.
    /// </summary>
    public class FrmLogin : Form
    {
        protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
        {
            if (disposing)
            {
                _brandBackground?.Dispose();
                _brandBackground = null;
            }
            base.Dispose(disposing);
        }

        private Image? _brandBackground;
        private TextBox _usernameTxtBx = null!;
        private TextBox _passwordTxtBx = null!;
        private Button _loginBtn = null!;
        private Label _errorLabel = null!;

        // --- Brand pane dimensions for Figma handoff ---
        // The left pane is a Panel, Dock = Left, Width = 300px.
        // Its height = the form's client height = 440px.
        // So the design canvas for the brand pane is 300 x 440.
        private const int BrandPaneWidth = 300;
        private const int FormClientWidth = 760;
        private const int FormClientHeight = 440;

        public FrmLogin()
        {
            Text = "Morcaffein CRM — Sign In";
            FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
            StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterScreen;
            MaximizeBox = false;
            MinimizeBox = false;
            ClientSize = new Size(FormClientWidth, FormClientHeight);
            BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary;
            Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9.5f);

            BuildLayout();
        }

        private void BuildLayout()
        {
            // ---------- LEFT PANE: brand (BLANK) ----------
            // Sized 300 x 440. Fill this in Figma, then we drop the designed
            // controls back in here. For now it's just a colored surface.
            var brandPane = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Left,
                Width = BrandPaneWidth,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.Brand,   // fallback if the image fails to load
            };

            // Load the branding background image. If it's missing, the pane just
            // keeps the solid brand color above — no crash.
            try
            {
                var imagePath = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, "Resources", "Login Background.png");
                if (File.Exists(imagePath))
                    _brandBackground = Image.FromFile(imagePath);
            }
            catch
            {
                _brandBackground = null;   // image is optional
            }

            brandPane.Paint += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (_brandBackground is null) return;

                var g = e.Graphics;
                g.InterpolationMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.InterpolationMode.HighQualityBicubic;
                g.DrawImage(_brandBackground, new Rectangle(0, 0, brandPane.Width, brandPane.Height));
            };

            // ---------- RIGHT PANE: login form ----------
            var formPane = new Panel
            {
                Dock = DockStyle.Fill,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.SurfacePrimary,
                Padding = new Padding(60, 80, 60, 60),
            };

            var headingLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Morcaffein",
                Location = new Point(60, 70),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(26f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.Brand,
            };

            var hintLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Customer Retention & Loyalty System",
                Location = new Point(60, 112),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
            };

            var usernameLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Username",
                Location = new Point(60, 172),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
            };

            _usernameTxtBx = new TextBox
            {
                Location = new Point(60, 192),
                Size = new Size(340, 28),
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
            };

            var passwordLabel = new Label
            {
                Text = "Password",
                Location = new Point(60, 236),
                AutoSize = true,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(9f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NeutralTextMuted,
            };

            _passwordTxtBx = new TextBox
            {
                Location = new Point(60, 256),
                Size = new Size(340, 28),
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
                BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle,
                UseSystemPasswordChar = true,
            };

            _errorLabel = new Label
            {
                Location = new Point(60, 294),
                Size = new Size(340, 32),
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(8.5f),
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.StatusNegativeFill,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft,
            };

            _loginBtn = new Button
            {
                Text = "Sign In",
                Location = new Point(60, 330),
                Size = new Size(340, 38),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryFill,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.ButtonPrimaryText,
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(11f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
            };
            _loginBtn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            _loginBtn.Click += LoginBtn_Click;
            ButtonStyler.TrackEnabledState(_loginBtn);

            formPane.Controls.Add(_loginBtn);
            formPane.Controls.Add(_errorLabel);
            formPane.Controls.Add(_passwordTxtBx);
            formPane.Controls.Add(passwordLabel);
            formPane.Controls.Add(_usernameTxtBx);
            formPane.Controls.Add(usernameLabel);
            formPane.Controls.Add(hintLabel);
            formPane.Controls.Add(headingLabel);
            // Explicit tab order — visual top-to-bottom. The controls were added in reverse
            // order for correct layout, so the default TabIndex values are backwards.
            _usernameTxtBx.TabIndex = 0;
            _passwordTxtBx.TabIndex = 1;
            _loginBtn.TabIndex = 2;

            // ---------- COMPOSE ----------
            // Fill added first, then Left — so the brand pane docks to the left and
            // the form pane fills the rest.
            Controls.Add(formPane);
            Controls.Add(brandPane);

            // Enter key on either textbox submits
            _usernameTxtBx.KeyDown += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Enter) { LoginBtn_Click(this, EventArgs.Empty); e.SuppressKeyPress = true; }
            };
            _passwordTxtBx.KeyDown += (s, e) =>
            {
                if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Enter) { LoginBtn_Click(this, EventArgs.Empty); e.SuppressKeyPress = true; }
            };
        }

        private async void LoginBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            var username = _usernameTxtBx.Text.Trim();
            var password = _passwordTxtBx.Text;

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(username) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(password))
            {
                MessageBox.Show(
                    this,
                    "Please enter both username and password.",
                    "Sign In",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                    MessageBoxIcon.Warning);
                return;
            }

            try
            {
                _loginBtn.Enabled = false;
                _loginBtn.Text = "Signing in...";
                _errorLabel.Text = string.Empty;

                var authApi = new AuthApi();
                var result = await authApi.LoginAsync(username, password);

                if (!result.Success)
                {
                    // Every failure becomes a popup. No crash, no pause.
                    MessageBox.Show(
                        this,
                        result.ErrorMessage ?? "Login failed.",
                        "Sign In",
                        MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                        MessageBoxIcon.Warning);

                    _passwordTxtBx.Text = string.Empty;
                    _passwordTxtBx.Focus();
                    return;
                }

                // Success — store token, seed session, close.
                var response = result.Data!;
                ApiConfig.AuthToken = response.Token;

                var employee = new Employee
                {
                    EmployeeId = response.EmployeeId,
                    FirstName = ExtractFirstName(response.FullName),
                    LastName = ExtractLastName(response.FullName),
                    Role = response.Role,
                    IsActive = true,
                    CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                    UpdatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow,
                };
                EmployeeSession.Start(employee);

                DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
                Close();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                // Truly unexpected — still a popup, never a raw crash dialog.
                MessageBox.Show(
                    this,
                    $"An unexpected error occurred:\n\n{ex.Message}",
                    "Unexpected Error",
                    MessageBoxButtons.OK,
                    MessageBoxIcon.Error);
            }
            finally
            {
                _loginBtn.Enabled = true;
                _loginBtn.Text = "Log In";
            }
        }

        private static string ExtractFirstName(string fullName)
        {
            var parts = fullName.Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
            return parts.Length > 0 ? parts[0] : fullName;
        }

        private static string ExtractLastName(string fullName)
        {
            var parts = fullName.Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
            return parts.Length > 1 ? string.Join(" ", parts.Skip(1)) : string.Empty;
        }
    }
}

===== FILE: CRM_Morcaffein/Views/FrmMain.cs =====
using CRM_Morcaffein.Session;
using CRM_Morcaffein.Theme;
using CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views.Controls;

namespace CRM_Morcaffein.winforms.Views
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Staff console shell: a right-docked nav strip and a content area that swaps in
    /// whichever tab is active. The nav items shown depend on the logged-in employee's
    /// role:
    ///   Barista → Transactions, Customers, Feedback
    ///   Manager → Dashboard, Campaign, Customers, Feedback
    ///   Admin   → Dashboard, Approvals, Manage Users
    ///
    /// Customers and Feedback reuse the same views across roles that have them.
    /// Placeholder tabs (Dashboard, Campaign, Approvals, Manage Users) use PlaceholderView.
    /// </summary>
    public partial class FrmMain : Form
    {
        public bool ClosedViaLogout { get; private set; }

        private const string IconsFolder = "Resources/Icons";
        private const string BrandingFolder = "Resources/Branding";

        public FrmMain()
        {
            InitializeComponent();

            var employee = EmployeeSession.Current!.Employee;
            staffNameLabel.Text = employee.FullName;
            staffRoleLabel.Text = employee.Role;
            avatarPanel.Paint += AvatarPanel_Paint;

            // Top bar brand mark — load the icon PNG into the panel as a background.
            LoadBrandMarkInto(brandMarkPanel);

            BuildNavButtons(employee.Role);

            logoutBtn.Click += LogoutBtn_Click;
        }

        private void FrmMain_Load(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (sidebarPanel.Controls.OfType<Button>().Any())
            {
                var firstNav = sidebarPanel.Controls.OfType<Button>().First();
                firstNav.PerformClick();
            }
        }

        protected override void OnFormClosing(FormClosingEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnFormClosing(e);

            if (e.CloseReason == CloseReason.UserClosing && !ClosedViaLogout)
            {
                var confirm = MessageBox.Show(this,
                    "Are you sure you want to exit? Any unsaved progress will be lost.",
                    "Exit Morcaffein",
                    MessageBoxButtons.YesNo,
                    MessageBoxIcon.Question);

                if (confirm != DialogResult.Yes)
                {
                    e.Cancel = true;
                    return;
                }
            }
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // NAV CONSTRUCTION
        // =====================================================================

        private void BuildNavButtons(string role)
        {
            var isManager = role.Equals("Manager", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                            role.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                            role.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
            var isAdmin = role.Equals("Admin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                          role.Equals("SuperAdmin", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

            // (label, icon filename without extension, view factory).
            var navItems = new List<(string Text, string IconFile, Action onClick)>();

            if (isAdmin)
            {
                navItems.Add(("Dashboard", "Dashboard", () => ShowView(new PlaceholderView(
                    "Dashboard",
                    "Business intelligence and reports. Coming soon."))));
                navItems.Add(("Approvals", "Approvals", () => ShowView(new ApprovalsView())));
                navItems.Add(("Manage Users", "User Mgmt", () => ShowView(new ManageUsersView())));
            }
            else if (isManager)
            {
                navItems.Add(("Dashboard", "Dashboard", () => ShowView(new PlaceholderView(
                    "Dashboard",
                    "Business intelligence and reports. Coming soon."))));
                navItems.Add(("Campaign", "Campaign", () => ShowView(new CampaignManagementView())));
                navItems.Add(("Customers", "Customers", () => ShowView(new CustomerManagementView())));
                navItems.Add(("Feedback", "Feedback", () => ShowView(new FeedbackManagementView())));
            }
            else
            {
                navItems.Add(("Transactions", "Cart", () => ShowView(new TransactionView())));
                navItems.Add(("Customers", "Customers", () => ShowView(new CustomerManagementView())));
                navItems.Add(("Feedback", "Feedback", () => ShowView(new FeedbackManagementView())));
            }

            int y = 20;
            const int buttonWidth = 184;
            const int buttonHeight = 46;
            const int gap = 12;

            foreach (var (text, iconFile, onClick) in navItems)
            {
                var btn = CreateNavButton(text, iconFile, buttonWidth, buttonHeight);
                btn.Location = new Point(18, y);

                var capturedText = text;
                var capturedOnClick = onClick;
                btn.Click += (s, e) =>
                {
                    SetActiveNav(btn);
                    topBarTabLabel.Text = "/ " + capturedText;
                    capturedOnClick();
                };

                sidebarPanel.Controls.Add(btn);
                y += buttonHeight + gap;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Builds a nav button that renders [icon] [label] left-aligned.
        ///
        /// WinForms does not expose a "gap between image and text" property, so we
        /// compose the button's content manually: the Image is placed via ImageAlign
        /// (MiddleLeft), and the text is offset from the left edge with Padding.Left.
        /// The icon itself sits at the far left because ImageAlign puts it before the
        /// padded text region.
        /// </summary>
        private static Button CreateNavButton(string text, string iconFile, int width, int height)
        {
            var btn = new Button
            {
                Text = text,
                Size = new Size(width, height),
                FlatStyle = FlatStyle.Flat,
                // TextImageRelation is what actually sequences [icon] [text] with a gap.
                TextImageRelation = TextImageRelation.ImageBeforeText,
                TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft,
                ImageAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft,
                // A modest left inset for the whole icon+text pair; the internal gap between
                // them is handled by WinForms once TextImageRelation is set.
                Padding = new Padding(12, 0, 0, 0),
                Font = BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f),
                Cursor = Cursors.Hand,
                BackColor = BrandPalette.NavBackground,
                ForeColor = BrandPalette.NavText,
                Image = LoadIcon(iconFile),
            };

            btn.FlatAppearance.BorderSize = 0;
            btn.FlatAppearance.MouseOverBackColor = BrandPalette.NavHoverBackground;
            return btn;
        }

        private void SetActiveNav(Button active)
        {
            foreach (var button in sidebarPanel.Controls.OfType<Button>())
            {
                button.BackColor = button == active ? BrandPalette.NavActiveBackground : BrandPalette.NavBackground;
                button.ForeColor = button == active ? BrandPalette.NavActiveText : BrandPalette.NavText;
                button.Font = button == active ? BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(10f) : BrandPalette.BrandFontRegular(10f);
            }
        }

        // =====================================================================
        // ICON / IMAGE LOADING
        // =====================================================================

        private static Image? LoadIcon(string filenameWithoutExtension)
        {
            try
            {
                var path = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, IconsFolder, filenameWithoutExtension + ".png");
                if (!File.Exists(path))
                    return null;

                // Load from bytes to avoid GDI+ path caching and to release the file
                // handle immediately (so the file isn't locked while the app runs).
                var bytes = File.ReadAllBytes(path);
                using var ms = new MemoryStream(bytes);
                using var temp = Image.FromStream(ms);
                return new Bitmap(temp);
            }
            catch
            {
                return null;   // missing/corrupt file → no icon
            }
        }

        private static void LoadBrandMarkInto(Panel panel)
        {
            try
            {
                var path = Path.Combine(AppContext.BaseDirectory, BrandingFolder, "Top Bar Icon.png");
                if (!File.Exists(path))
                    return;

                var bytes = File.ReadAllBytes(path);
                using var ms = new MemoryStream(bytes);
                using var temp = Image.FromStream(ms);
                panel.BackgroundImage = new Bitmap(temp);
                panel.BackgroundImageLayout = ImageLayout.Zoom;
            }
            catch
            {
                // Silently skip — panel just renders empty if the brand mark isn't found.
            }
        }

        private void ShowView(UserControl view)
        {
            contentPanel.Controls.Clear();
            view.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
            contentPanel.Controls.Add(view);
        }

        private void LogoutBtn_Click(object? sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            var confirm = MessageBox.Show(this,
                $"Log out of {EmployeeSession.Current?.DisplayName}'s session?",
                "Log Out", MessageBoxButtons.YesNo, MessageBoxIcon.Question);

            if (confirm != DialogResult.Yes)
                return;

            ClosedViaLogout = true;
            EmployeeSession.End();
            Close();
        }

        private void AvatarPanel_Paint(object? sender, PaintEventArgs e)
        {
            var g = e.Graphics;
            g.SmoothingMode = System.Drawing.Drawing2D.SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;

            using var brush = new SolidBrush(BrandPalette.Brand);
            g.FillEllipse(brush, 0, 0, avatarPanel.Width - 1, avatarPanel.Height - 1);

            var initials = GetInitials(EmployeeSession.Current?.DisplayName);
            using var font = BrandPalette.BrandFontBold(11f);
            var textSize = g.MeasureString(initials, font);
            g.DrawString(initials, font, Brushes.White,
                (avatarPanel.Width - textSize.Width) / 2, (avatarPanel.Height - textSize.Height) / 2);
        }

        private static string GetInitials(string? fullName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fullName))
                return "?";

            var parts = fullName.Split(' ', StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
            return parts.Length switch
            {
                0 => "?",
                1 => parts[0][..1].ToUpperInvariant(),
                _ => (parts[0][..1] + parts[^1][..1]).ToUpperInvariant(),
            };
        }
    }
}
